#: 测试环境的 mock 模型供应商：只在 LABEL_SYSTEM_DEPLOY_ENV 为非生产时把请求改发到这个地址，
#: 冻结快照、漂移校验、计费账本都不动——只换传输目的地。生产环境无论怎么配都忽略。
PROVIDER_STUB_ENV = "LABEL_SYSTEM_MODEL_PROVIDER_STUB_URL"
_NON_PRODUCTION_DEPLOY_ENVS = frozenset({"local", "dev", "test", "ci"})


def provider_stub_base_url(environ: Mapping[str, str] | None = None) -> str | None:
    env = os.environ if environ is None else environ
    deploy_env = (env.get("LABEL_SYSTEM_DEPLOY_ENV") or "production").strip().lower()
    raw = (env.get(PROVIDER_STUB_ENV) or "").strip()
    if deploy_env not in _NON_PRODUCTION_DEPLOY_ENVS or not raw:
        return None
    parsed = urlsplit(raw)
    host = (parsed.hostname or "").lower()
    if (parsed.scheme not in ("http", "https") or not host or host.endswith("volces.com")
            or parsed.username or parsed.password or parsed.query or parsed.fragment):
        raise ValueError("LABEL_SYSTEM_MODEL_PROVIDER_STUB_URL 必须是测试地址（http(s)，非火山域名，无凭据/查询串）")
    return raw


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class _LegacyAutoFidelity:
    value: Literal["auto"] = "auto"
    mode: Literal["detail"] = field(default="detail", init=False)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ProviderImageSample:
    label: str
    path: Path = field(repr=False)
    mime_type: str | None
    role: Literal["target", "anchor"]
    ordinal: int
    fidelity: VisionFidelity | _LegacyAutoFidelity
    policy_hash: str | None

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not isinstance(self.label, str) or not self.label.strip():
            raise ValueError("模型图片标签不能为空")
        if not isinstance(self.path, Path):
            raise ValueError("模型图片路径不合法")
        if self.mime_type is not None and (
            not isinstance(self.mime_type, str)
            or not self.mime_type.startswith("image/")
        ):
            raise ValueError("模型图片 MIME 不合法")
        if self.role not in {"target", "anchor"}:
            raise ValueError("模型图片角色不合法")
        if (
            not isinstance(self.ordinal, int)
            or isinstance(self.ordinal, bool)
            or self.ordinal < 0
        ):
            raise ValueError("模型图片序号不合法")
        if not isinstance(
            self.fidelity,
            (DetailFidelity, PixelLimitFidelity, _LegacyAutoFidelity),
        ):
            raise ValueError("模型图片保真策略不合法")
        if (
            isinstance(self.fidelity, _LegacyAutoFidelity)
            and self.policy_hash is not None
        ):
            raise ValueError("显式图片策略不支持 auto 保真度")
        if self.policy_hash is not None and not re.fullmatch(
            r"[0-9a-f]{64}",
            self.policy_hash,
        ):
            raise ValueError("模型图片策略摘要不合法")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SerializedDoubaoRequest:
    """Exact UTF-8 JSON bytes checked before reservation and sent unchanged."""

    body: bytes = field(repr=False)
    input_image_bytes: int
    output_budget: int
    reasoning_effort: str | None
    thinking_mode: str
    sample_count: int

    @property
    def body_sha256(self) -> str:
        return hashlib.sha256(self.body).hexdigest()


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CacheUsageObservation:
    status: Literal["hit", "miss", "unreported", "invalid"]
    cached_input_tokens: int | None
    source: Literal[
        "usage.prompt_tokens_details.cached_tokens",
        "usage.input_tokens_details.cached_tokens",
    ] | None
    reason: str | None = None

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        payload: dict[str, Any] = {
            "status": self.status,
            "cached_input_tokens": self.cached_input_tokens,
            "source": self.source,
        }
        if self.reason is not None:
            payload["reason"] = self.reason
        return payload


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DoubaoResponse:
    parsed: dict[str, Any]
    raw_text: str
    raw_payload: dict[str, Any]
    upstream_status_code: int | None = None
    request_correlation_id: str | None = None
    attempt_count: int = 1
    output_budget: int | None = None
    reasoning_effort: str | None = None
    thinking_mode: str = "auto"
    input_image_bytes: int | None = None
    input_image_tokens: int | None = None
    per_image_tokens: tuple[int, ...] | None = None
    input_tokens: int | None = None
    output_tokens: int | None = None
    total_tokens: int | None = None
    cache_usage: CacheUsageObservation = field(
        default_factory=lambda: CacheUsageObservation(
            status="unreported",
            cached_input_tokens=None,
            source=None,
        )
    )
    coercions: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    output_normalization: tuple[dict[str, Any], ...] = ()


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class _UpstreamResponse:
    payload: dict[str, Any]
    status_code: int
    request_correlation_id: str | None


class DoubaoError(RuntimeError):
    technical_error_type = "non_retryable"
    retryable = False
    upstream_status_code: int | None = None
    request_correlation_id: str | None = None
    attempt_count = 0
    retry_after_seconds: int | None = None
    outcome_unknown = False
    provider_usage: dict[str, Any] | None = None
    finish_reason: str | None = None
    provider_error_code: str | None = None


def _attach_failure_usage(error: DoubaoError, raw: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    """Keep billing evidence even when the generated content is invalid."""
    usage = response_usage(raw)
    if usage is not None:
        cache = response_cache_usage(raw, input_tokens=usage[0])
        error.provider_usage = {"input_tokens": usage[0], "output_tokens": usage[1],
            "cached_input_tokens": cache.cached_input_tokens,
            "cache_observation_status": cache.status}
    choices = raw.get("choices")
    if isinstance(choices, list) and choices and isinstance(choices[0], dict):
        reason = choices[0].get("finish_reason")
        if reason in {"stop", "length", "content_filter", "tool_calls", "function_call"}:
            error.finish_reason = reason
            # JSONDecodeError points at the opening quote of an unterminated
            # string, which can be near the start even when the provider hit
            # its output limit. The provider's terminal reason is authoritative.
            if reason == "length" and isinstance(error, DoubaoParseError):
                error.technical_error_type = "json_truncated"


class DoubaoHTTPError(DoubaoError):
    def __init__(self, status_code: int, headers: dict[str, str]):
        super().__init__(f"模型 API HTTP {status_code}")
        self.status_code = status_code
        self.upstream_status_code = status_code
        self.request_correlation_id = _request_correlation_id(headers)
        self.headers = headers
        self.technical_error_type = (
            "429"
            if status_code == 429
            else "provider5xx"
            if 500 <= status_code <= 599
            else "non_retryable"
        )
        self.outcome_unknown = self.technical_error_type == "provider5xx"
        self.retryable = self.technical_error_type == "429"
        self.retry_after_seconds = (
            _retry_after_seconds(headers) if status_code == 429 else None
        )


class DoubaoTransportError(DoubaoError):
    def __init__(self, error_type: str):
        if error_type not in {"timeout", "network"}:
            raise ValueError("不支持的传输错误类型")
        super().__init__(f"模型 API {error_type}")
        self.technical_error_type = error_type
        # A timeout/reset can happen after the provider accepted and charged
        # the request.  This client has no provider idempotency key, therefore
        # an automatic retry could duplicate paid work.
        self.retryable = False
        self.outcome_unknown = True


class DoubaoPoolTimeoutError(DoubaoError):
    """本地连接池排队超时：请求字节从未离开本机，供应商不可能计费。"""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__("模型 API 本地连接池排队超时，请求未发出")
        self.technical_error_type = "pool_timeout"
        self.retryable = True
        self.outcome_unknown = False
        # dispatch hook 在 client.post 之前触发；这里显式声明未发出，供调用方回退。
        self.provider_dispatched = False


class DoubaoParseError(DoubaoError):
    def __init__(self, message: str, *, truncated: bool = False):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.technical_error_type = (
            "json_truncated" if truncated else "transient_parse"
        )
        self.retryable = True


class DoubaoStructuredOutputError(DoubaoError):
    def __init__(self, message: str):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.technical_error_type = "structured_output_schema_mismatch"
        self.retryable = True
        self.raw_excerpt = ""


class DoubaoCapabilityMismatchError(DoubaoError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code
        self.technical_error_type = "vision_capability_mismatch"
        self.retryable = False


_RETRY_BACKOFF_CAP_SECONDS = 30.0


async def _retry_sleep(delay: float) -> None:
    await asyncio.sleep(delay)


def _retry_delay(error: BaseException, attempt: int) -> float | None:
    """客户端内重试策略：返回下一次尝试前的等待秒数，None 表示不能重试。

    - 429 / 5xx：供应商明确拒绝或出错，退避后重试（优先 Retry-After）。
    - 结果不明（超时、已发出后断开、未知异常）：可能已计费，没有幂等键，不重发。
    - 输出解析 / 结构化校验失败：可重试错误，立即重问。
    - 其余不可重试错误直接抛出。
    """

    if not isinstance(error, DoubaoError):
        return None
    kind = error.technical_error_type
    if kind in {"429", "provider5xx", "pool_timeout"}:
        hint = error.retry_after_seconds
        base = (
            float(hint)
            if isinstance(hint, (int, float)) and not isinstance(hint, bool) and hint > 0
            else float(2 ** attempt)
        )
        return min(_RETRY_BACKOFF_CAP_SECONDS, base)
    if error.outcome_unknown or not error.retryable:
        return None
    return 0.0


def _with_json_retry_nudge(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """在重问请求里追加一句“只返回合法 JSON”；按协议追加到 messages 或 input。"""

    current = dict(payload)
    key = "messages" if isinstance(current.get("messages"), list) else "input" if isinstance(current.get("input"), list) else None
    if key is None:
        return current
    current[key] = list(current[key]) + [
        {
            "role": "user",
            "content": "上次输出无法通过 JSON 校验。请严格只返回一个合法 JSON 对象，不要包含 Markdown。",
        }
    ]
    return current


_REQUEST_ID_HEADERS = (
    "x-request-id",
    "request-id",
    "x-trace-id",
    "trace-id",
    "x-tt-logid",
)
_SAFE_REQUEST_ID = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z0-9._:/-]{1,200}$")


def _retry_after_seconds(headers: Mapping[str, str]) -> int | None:
    normalized = {str(key).lower(): str(value).strip() for key, value in headers.items()}
    raw = normalized.get("retry-after")
    if not raw:
        return None
    try:
        seconds = float(raw)
        if not math.isfinite(seconds) or seconds < 0:
            return None
        return min(86_400, max(1, math.ceil(seconds)))
    except (TypeError, ValueError, OverflowError):
        pass
    try:
        retry_at = parsedate_to_datetime(raw)
        if retry_at.tzinfo is None:
            retry_at = retry_at.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
        seconds = (retry_at - datetime.now(timezone.utc)).total_seconds()
        if seconds <= 0:
            return 1
        return min(86_400, max(1, math.ceil(seconds)))
    except (TypeError, ValueError, OverflowError):
        return None


def _request_correlation_id(headers: Mapping[str, str]) -> str | None:
    normalized = {str(key).lower(): str(value).strip() for key, value in headers.items()}
    for header_name in _REQUEST_ID_HEADERS:
        value = normalized.get(header_name)
        if value and _SAFE_REQUEST_ID.fullmatch(value):
            return value
    return None


SCHEMA_IN_PROMPT_HEADER = (
    "\n\n【输出格式硬约束】只输出一个 JSON 对象，不要 Markdown 代码块、不要任何解释文字，"
    "不要把下面的格式说明或骨架原样输出。所有必填字段都要出现，枚举字段只能取列出的值，"
    "数值与布尔按类型输出。\n"
)


def _append_schema_to_system(payload: dict[str, Any], contract: PromptOutputContract, protocol: str) -> None:
    """json_object 模式下把冻结合同渲染成占位符骨架附到系统提示词末尾（确定性，可复现）。"""
    from .prompt_output_contract import render_schema_guide

    suffix = SCHEMA_IN_PROMPT_HEADER + render_schema_guide(contract.schema)
    if protocol == "anthropic_messages":
        payload["system"] = str(payload.get("system") or "") + suffix
        return
    key = "input" if protocol == "openai_responses" else "messages"
    for message in payload.get(key) or []:
        if isinstance(message, dict) and message.get("role") == "system" and isinstance(message.get("content"), str):
            message["content"] = message["content"] + suffix
            return
    raise ValueError("json_object 模式需要系统消息承载输出合同")


def _image_data_url(path: Path, mime_type: str | None = None) -> str:
    media_type = mime_type or mimetypes.guess_type(path.name)[0] or "image/jpeg"
    encoded = base64.b64encode(path.read_bytes()).decode("ascii")
    return f"data:{media_type};base64,{encoded}"


def _bounded_image_data_url(
    path: Path,
    mime_type: str | None,
    *,
    max_bytes: int,
) -> tuple[str, int]:
    """Read and encode one immutable payload snapshot with a hard byte ceiling."""
    if max_bytes <= 0:
        raise ValueError("诊断图片总量超过安全请求上限")
    try:
        with path.open("rb") as image_file:
            raw = image_file.read(max_bytes + 1)
    except OSError as exc:
        raise ValueError("诊断图片不可读取") from exc
    if not raw:
        raise ValueError("诊断图片为空文件")
    if len(raw) > max_bytes:
        raise ValueError("诊断图片超过安全请求字节上限")
    media_type = mime_type or mimetypes.guess_type(path.name)[0] or "image/jpeg"
    encoded = base64.b64encode(raw).decode("ascii")
    return f"data:{media_type};base64,{encoded}", len(raw)


def _image_content(
    sample: ProviderImageSample,
    image_url: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    payload: dict[str, Any] = {"url": image_url}
    if isinstance(sample.fidelity, PixelLimitFidelity):
        payload["image_pixel_limit"] = {
            "min_pixels": sample.fidelity.min_pixels,
            "max_pixels": sample.fidelity.max_pixels,
        }
    else:
        payload["detail"] = sample.fidelity.value
    return {"type": "image_url", "image_url": payload}


def _split_samples_for_prefix_cache(
    samples: Sequence[ProviderImageSample],
) -> tuple[tuple[ProviderImageSample, ...], tuple[ProviderImageSample, ...]]:
    """把跨请求稳定的图片（锚点）排到可变文本之前，其余（待评图）排在文本之后。

    方舟对 doubao-seed-2.0 及之后模型自动做隐式前缀缓存（Chat API，最小 1024 token），
    命中要求请求前缀逐字节一致。此前 content 顺序是「用户文本（含每条素材的工作流上下文 JSON）
    → 5 张锚点 → 待评图」，可变文本在前把前缀截断在 system prompt 处，137 上所有多图调用
    cached_tokens 恒为 0。改成「锚点 → 文本 → 待评图」后，system prompt + 锚点（约 7.5k token）
    成为稳定前缀，可按缓存命中单价计费。锚点内部保持原有 ordinal 顺序，语义（第 1-5 张锚点、
    最后一张待评图）不变。
    """
    prefix = tuple(sample for sample in samples if sample.role == "anchor")
    suffix = tuple(sample for sample in samples if sample.role != "anchor")
    return prefix, suffix


def _legacy_fidelity(value: str) -> VisionFidelity | _LegacyAutoFidelity:
    if value == "auto":
        return _LegacyAutoFidelity()
    if value in {"low", "high", "xhigh"}:
        return DetailFidelity(value)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    raise ValueError("多图清晰度参数不合法")


def _extract_message_text(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    choices = payload.get("choices") or []
    if choices:
        content = (choices[0].get("message") or {}).get("content")
        if isinstance(content, str):
            return content
        if isinstance(content, list):
            return "\n".join(
                str(item.get("text", "")) for item in content if isinstance(item, dict)
            )
    output = payload.get("output") or []
    for item in output:
        if not isinstance(item, dict) or item.get("type") != "message":
            continue
        for content in item.get("content") or []:
            if isinstance(content, dict) and content.get("type") == "output_text":
                return str(content.get("text", ""))
    content = payload.get("content")
    if isinstance(content, str):
        return content
    if isinstance(content, list):
        texts = [str(item.get("text", "")) for item in content if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get("type") == "text"]
        if texts:
            return "\n".join(texts)
    raise DoubaoParseError("模型响应中没有可读取的文本内容")


def parse_json_text(text: str, *, repairs: list[str] | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    cleaned = text.strip()
    cleaned = re.sub(r"^```(?:json)?\s*", "", cleaned, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
    cleaned = re.sub(r"\s*```$", "", cleaned)

    def decode(candidate: str) -> Any:
        try:
            return json.loads(candidate)
        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
            if exc.msg == "Extra data" and repairs is not None:
                value, end = json.JSONDecoder().raw_decode(candidate)
                # Only one redundant closer has an unambiguous, value-preserving
                # repair. Multiple objects, arrays, fields or other suffixes fail.
                if isinstance(value, dict) and candidate[end:].strip() == "}":
                    repairs.append("$:drop_redundant_object_closer")
                    return value
            raise

    def invalid(exc: json.JSONDecodeError) -> DoubaoParseError:
        error = DoubaoParseError("模型未返回合法 JSON",
            truncated=exc.msg != "Extra data" and exc.pos >= max(0, len(exc.doc) - 2))
        # Structural diagnostics only: never include model text or credentials.
        error.evidence = {"json_parse": {"reason": exc.msg, "offset": exc.pos,
            "document_chars": len(exc.doc),
            "single_redundant_closer": exc.msg == "Extra data" and exc.doc[exc.pos:].strip() == "}"}}
        return error

    try:
        value = decode(cleaned)
    except json.JSONDecodeError as first_error:
        if first_error.msg == "Extra data":
            raise invalid(first_error) from first_error
        start, end = cleaned.find("{"), cleaned.rfind("}")
        if start < 0 or end <= start:
            raise invalid(first_error) from first_error
        try:
            value = decode(cleaned[start:])
        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
            raise invalid(exc) from exc
    if not isinstance(value, dict):
        raise DoubaoParseError("模型 JSON 顶层必须是对象")
    return value


def response_usage(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[int, int, int] | None:
    """Read the common Chat Completions/Responses usage shapes."""
    usage = payload.get("usage")
    if not isinstance(usage, dict):
        return None
    input_tokens = usage.get("input_tokens", usage.get("prompt_tokens"))
    output_tokens = usage.get("output_tokens", usage.get("completion_tokens"))
    total_tokens = usage.get("total_tokens")
    values = (input_tokens, output_tokens, total_tokens)
    if any(isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, int) for value in values):
        return None
    if input_tokens < 0 or output_tokens < 0 or total_tokens < 0:
        return None
    if total_tokens < input_tokens + output_tokens:
        return None
    return input_tokens, output_tokens, total_tokens


def response_cache_usage(
    payload: dict[str, Any],
    *,
    input_tokens: int | None,
) -> CacheUsageObservation:
    """Read provider-reported cache usage without inferring a cache hit."""

    usage = payload.get("usage")
    if not isinstance(usage, dict):
        return CacheUsageObservation("unreported", None, None)
    candidates = (
        (
            "prompt_tokens_details",
            "usage.prompt_tokens_details.cached_tokens",
        ),
        (
            "input_tokens_details",
            "usage.input_tokens_details.cached_tokens",
        ),
    )
    for details_key, source in candidates:
        details = usage.get(details_key)
        if not isinstance(details, dict) or "cached_tokens" not in details:
            continue
        value = details["cached_tokens"]
        if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, int):
            return CacheUsageObservation(
                "invalid",
                None,
                source,
                "cached_tokens_not_integer",
            )
        if value < 0:
            return CacheUsageObservation(
                "invalid",
                None,
                source,
                "cached_tokens_negative",
            )
        if (
            not isinstance(input_tokens, int)
            or isinstance(input_tokens, bool)
            or input_tokens < 0
        ):
            return CacheUsageObservation(
                "invalid",
                None,
                source,
                "input_tokens_invalid",
            )
        if value > input_tokens:
            return CacheUsageObservation(
                "invalid",
                None,
                source,
                "cached_tokens_exceed_input",
            )
        return CacheUsageObservation(
            "hit" if value > 0 else "miss",
            value,
            source,
        )
    return CacheUsageObservation("unreported", None, None)


def response_media_usage(
    payload: dict[str, Any],
) -> tuple[int | None, tuple[int, ...] | None]:
    """Read provider image-token details without inventing missing granularity."""

    usage = payload.get("usage")
    if not isinstance(usage, dict):
        return None, None
    details = usage.get("prompt_tokens_details")
    if not isinstance(details, dict):
        details = usage.get("input_tokens_details")
    if not isinstance(details, dict):
        return None, None
    raw_total = details.get("image_tokens")
    total = (
        raw_total
        if isinstance(raw_total, int)
        and not isinstance(raw_total, bool)
        and raw_total >= 0
        else None
    )
    raw_per_image = details.get(
        "image_tokens_by_image",
        details.get("per_image_tokens"),
    )
    per_image: tuple[int, ...] | None = None
    if isinstance(raw_per_image, list) and all(
        isinstance(value, int)
        and not isinstance(value, bool)
        and value >= 0
        for value in raw_per_image
    ):
        candidate = tuple(raw_per_image)
        candidate_total = sum(candidate)
        if total is None:
            total = candidate_total
            per_image = candidate
        elif candidate_total == total:
            per_image = candidate
    return total, per_image


class DoubaoClient:
    def __init__(self, config: ModelConfig | OptimizerConfig):
        if not config.encrypted_api_key:
            raise ValueError("尚未配置模型 API Key")
        self.config = config
        self.api_key = unprotect_secret(config.encrypted_api_key)
        self._dispatch_hook: Callable[[], None] | None = None

    def set_dispatch_hook(self, hook: Callable[[], None] | None) -> None:
        """Install a local signal fired at the actual HTTP dispatch boundary."""

        self._dispatch_hook = hook

    @property
    def url(self) -> str:
        base = provider_stub_base_url() or self.config.base_url
        return f"{base.rstrip('/')}/{self.config.api_path.lstrip('/')}"

    @property
    def request_model(self) -> str:
        """请求体 ``model`` 字段：绑定了豆包推理接入点时写 ep-xxx，否则写模型 ID。

        接入点只负责路由与成本归因；``model_id`` 继续表示真实模型身份，
        thinking / 计价合同等按模型身份判断的逻辑不受影响。
        """
        endpoint_id = getattr(self.config, "endpoint_id", None)
        if (
            isinstance(endpoint_id, str)
            and endpoint_id.strip()
            and is_volcengine_ark_provider(getattr(self.config, "provider", None))
        ):
            return endpoint_id.strip()
        return str(self.config.model_id)

    def _headers(self) -> dict[str, str]:
        headers = {"Content-Type": "application/json"}
        if (getattr(self.config, "protocol", None) or "openai_chat") == "anthropic_messages":
            headers.update({"x-api-key": self.api_key, "anthropic-version": "2023-06-01"})
        else:
            headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {self.api_key}"
        return headers

    def _vision_capability(self) -> VisionCapabilityProfile:
        raw_json = getattr(self.config, "vision_capability_profile_json", None)
        raw = (
            getattr(self.config, "vision_capability_profile", {})
            if raw_json is None
            else raw_json
        )
        raw = raw or {}
        try:
            decoded = json.loads(raw) if isinstance(raw, str) else raw
            if not isinstance(decoded, Mapping):
                raise ValueError("capability profile is not an object")
            return parse_vision_capability_profile(
                decoded,
                provider=str(getattr(self.config, "provider", "") or ""),
                model_id=str(getattr(self.config, "model_id", "") or ""),
            )
        except (ValueError, TypeError, json.JSONDecodeError, VisionInputPolicyError) as exc:
            raise DoubaoCapabilityMismatchError(
                "vision_capability_profile_invalid",
                "模型图片能力档案不可用",
            ) from exc

    def _validate_explicit_samples(
        self,
        samples: Sequence[ProviderImageSample],
    ) -> None:
        if (getattr(self.config, "protocol", None) or "openai_chat") == "anthropic_messages":
            raise DoubaoCapabilityMismatchError(
                "vision_fidelity_protocol_unsupported",
                "当前模型协议无法保留逐图保真参数",
            )
        capability = self._vision_capability()
        policy_hashes = {sample.policy_hash for sample in samples}
        if None in policy_hashes or len(policy_hashes) != 1:
            raise DoubaoCapabilityMismatchError(
                "vision_policy_hash_mismatch",
                "同一模型调用的图片策略摘要不一致",
            )
        identities: set[tuple[str, int]] = set()
        for sample in samples:
            identity = (sample.role, sample.ordinal)
            if identity in identities:
                raise DoubaoCapabilityMismatchError(
                    "vision_sample_identity_duplicate",
                    "同一模型调用的图片角色序号重复",
                )
            identities.add(identity)
            fidelity = sample.fidelity
            if isinstance(fidelity, DetailFidelity):
                if not capability.supports_detail(fidelity.value):
                    raise DoubaoCapabilityMismatchError(
                        "vision_fidelity_unsupported",
                        "图片 detail 不在冻结模型能力档案中",
                    )
            elif isinstance(fidelity, PixelLimitFidelity):
                if (
                    not capability.pixel_limit_supported
                    or fidelity.min_pixels < capability.min_source_pixels
                    or fidelity.max_pixels > capability.max_source_pixels
                ):
                    raise DoubaoCapabilityMismatchError(
                        "vision_pixel_limit_unsupported",
                        "image_pixel_limit 不在冻结模型能力档案中",
                    )
            else:
                raise DoubaoCapabilityMismatchError(
                    "vision_fidelity_unsupported",
                    "显式图片策略不支持 auto 保真度",
                )
            if (
                sample.mime_type is not None
                and sample.mime_type not in capability.allowed_mime_types
            ):
                raise DoubaoCapabilityMismatchError(
                    "media_mime_type_unsupported",
                    "图片 MIME 不在冻结模型能力档案中",
                )

    def _provider_samples(
        self,
        samples: Sequence[
            ProviderImageSample | tuple[str, Path, str | None]
        ],
        *,
        legacy_detail: str,
    ) -> tuple[ProviderImageSample, ...]:
        frozen = tuple(samples)
        if not frozen:
            return ()
        if all(isinstance(sample, ProviderImageSample) for sample in frozen):
            explicit = tuple(
                sample
                for sample in frozen
                if isinstance(sample, ProviderImageSample)
            )
            self._validate_explicit_samples(explicit)
            return explicit
        if any(isinstance(sample, ProviderImageSample) for sample in frozen):
            raise ValueError("同一模型调用不能混用显式图片样本和历史三元组")
        fidelity = _legacy_fidelity(legacy_detail)
        converted: list[ProviderImageSample] = []
        for ordinal, raw_sample in enumerate(frozen):
            if not isinstance(raw_sample, tuple) or len(raw_sample) != 3:
                raise ValueError("历史模型图片样本必须是三元组")
            label, path, mime_type = raw_sample
            converted.append(
                ProviderImageSample(
                    label=label,
                    path=path,
                    mime_type=mime_type,
                    role="target",
                    ordinal=ordinal,
                    fidelity=fidelity,
                    policy_hash=None,
                )
            )
        return tuple(converted)

    def _protocol_payload(self, system_prompt: str, user_content: str | list[dict[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        protocol = getattr(self.config, "protocol", None) or "openai_chat"
        if protocol == "anthropic_messages":
            content = user_content
            if isinstance(content, list):
                converted: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
                for item in content:
                    if item.get("type") == "text":
                        converted.append({"type": "text", "text": item.get("text", "")})
                    elif item.get("type") == "image_url":
                        url = str((item.get("image_url") or {}).get("url", ""))
                        if url.startswith("data:") and ";base64," in url:
                            media, encoded = url[5:].split(";base64,", 1)
                            converted.append({"type": "image", "source": {"type": "base64", "media_type": media, "data": encoded}})
                content = converted
            return {"model": self.request_model, "system": system_prompt, "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": content}]}
        if protocol == "openai_responses":
            return {"model": self.request_model, "input": [{"role": "system", "content": system_prompt}, {"role": "user", "content": user_content}]}
        return {"model": self.request_model, "messages": [{"role": "system", "content": system_prompt}, {"role": "user", "content": user_content}]}

    async def _http_post(
        self,
        *,
        payload: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
        serialized_payload: bytes | None = None,
    ) -> _UpstreamResponse:
        # HTTPX bounds individual I/O phases, not total wall-clock lifetime.
        # A slowly progressing response must not extend the call indefinitely.
        try:
            async with asyncio.timeout(float(self.config.timeout_seconds)):
                return await self._http_post_within_deadline(
                    payload=payload, serialized_payload=serialized_payload)
        except TimeoutError as exc:
            # The supplier may still bill a dispatched request. Preserve the
            # existing unknown-outcome settlement and never retry it blindly.
            raise DoubaoTransportError("timeout") from exc

    async def _http_post_within_deadline(
        self,
        *,
        payload: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
        serialized_payload: bytes | None = None,
    ) -> _UpstreamResponse:
        if (payload is None) == (serialized_payload is None):
            raise ValueError("模型请求必须且只能提供一种 JSON 载荷")
        total_seconds = float(self.config.timeout_seconds)
        # 排队等连接的上限略短于整体截止，让“未发出”的池超时先于整体超时被识别，
        # 而不是被整体截止误记成供应商结果未知。
        timeout = httpx.Timeout(total_seconds, pool=max(0.001, total_seconds * 0.9))
        headers = self._headers()
        from .provider_http_pool import request_client
        async with request_client(timeout) as client:
            try:
                if self._dispatch_hook is not None:
                    self._dispatch_hook()
                if serialized_payload is not None:
                    response = await client.post(
                        self.url,
                        headers=headers,
                        timeout=timeout,
                        content=serialized_payload,
                    )
                else:
                    response = await client.post(
                        self.url,
                        headers=headers,
                        timeout=timeout,
                        json=payload,
                    )
            except httpx.PoolTimeout as exc:
                # 连接池排队超时发生在任何字节发出之前：不是供应商超时，不留费用责任。
                raise DoubaoPoolTimeoutError() from exc
            except httpx.TimeoutException as exc:
                raise DoubaoTransportError("timeout") from exc
            except httpx.NetworkError as exc:
                raise DoubaoTransportError("network") from exc
            if response.is_error:
                error = DoubaoHTTPError(
                    response.status_code,
                    dict(response.headers),
                )
                try:
                    body = response.json()
                except (ValueError, UnicodeError):
                    body = None
                detail = body.get("error") if isinstance(body, dict) else None
                code = detail.get("code") if isinstance(detail, dict) else None
                if isinstance(code, str) and re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_.-]{0,119}", code):
                    error.provider_error_code = code
                raise error
            try:
                data = response.json()
            except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
                error = DoubaoParseError(
                    "模型 API 返回无效 JSON",
                    truncated=exc.pos >= max(0, len(exc.doc) - 2),
                )
                error.upstream_status_code = response.status_code
                error.request_correlation_id = _request_correlation_id(
                    response.headers
                )
                raise error from exc
            if not isinstance(data, dict):
                error = DoubaoParseError(
                    "模型 API 返回了无法识别的数据结构"
                )
                error.upstream_status_code = response.status_code
                error.request_correlation_id = _request_correlation_id(
                    response.headers
                )
                raise error
            return _UpstreamResponse(
                payload=data,
                status_code=response.status_code,
                request_correlation_id=_request_correlation_id(response.headers),
            )

    async def _post(self, payload: dict[str, Any]) -> _UpstreamResponse:
        return await self._http_post(payload=payload)

    async def _post_serialized(
        self,
        serialized_payload: bytes,
    ) -> _UpstreamResponse:
        return await self._http_post(serialized_payload=serialized_payload)

    def _generation_options(
        self,
        payload: dict[str, Any],
        *,
        output_budget: int | None,
        reasoning_effort: str | None,
        structured_output: bool | None,
        output_contract: PromptOutputContract | Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
    ) -> tuple[int, str | None, str]:
        actual_budget = (
            self.config.max_tokens if output_budget is None else output_budget
        )
        if actual_budget < 1:
            raise ValueError("模型输出预算必须大于 0")
        actual_reasoning_effort = (
            reasoning_effort
            if reasoning_effort is not None
            else getattr(self.config, "reasoning_effort", None)
        )
        thinking_mode = getattr(self.config, "thinking_mode", "auto") or "auto"
        if thinking_mode not in {"auto", "enabled", "disabled"}:
            raise ValueError("thinking_mode 必须是 auto、enabled 或 disabled")
        protocol = getattr(self.config, "protocol", None) or "openai_chat"
        model_id = str(getattr(self.config, "model_id", "") or "")
        doubao_reasoning_chat = (
            protocol == "openai_chat"
            and is_volcengine_ark_provider(self.config.provider)
            and model_id.startswith("doubao-seed-2-")
        )
        if protocol == "openai_responses":
            payload["max_output_tokens"] = actual_budget
            actual_reasoning_effort = actual_reasoning_effort or "high"
            if is_volcengine_ark_provider(self.config.provider) and thinking_mode != "auto":
                payload["thinking"] = {"type": thinking_mode}
        elif protocol == "openai_chat" and (
            self.config.provider == "openai" or doubao_reasoning_chat
        ):
            payload["max_completion_tokens"] = actual_budget
            if self.config.provider == "openai":
                actual_reasoning_effort = actual_reasoning_effort or "high"
                payload["reasoning_effort"] = actual_reasoning_effort
            else:
                if actual_reasoning_effort is not None:
                    payload["reasoning_effort"] = actual_reasoning_effort
                if thinking_mode != "auto":
                    payload["thinking"] = {"type": thinking_mode}
        else:
            payload["temperature"] = self.config.temperature
            payload["max_tokens"] = actual_budget
            if is_volcengine_ark_provider(self.config.provider):
                if thinking_mode != "auto":
                    payload["thinking"] = {"type": thinking_mode}
            elif self.config.provider == "local_qwen" and protocol == "openai_chat":
                # vLLM's Qwen template defaults to thinking (xhigh on .205).
                # The frozen control must reach the provider, not merely be
                # recorded in our invocation evidence.
                if thinking_mode != "auto":
                    payload["chat_template_kwargs"] = {
                        "enable_thinking": thinking_mode == "enabled",
                    }
                if actual_reasoning_effort is not None:
                    payload["reasoning_effort"] = actual_reasoning_effort
            elif actual_reasoning_effort is not None:
                payload["reasoning_effort"] = actual_reasoning_effort
        use_structured_output = (
            self.config.structured_output
            if structured_output is None
            else structured_output
        )
        contract = parse_output_contract(output_contract)
        response_format = ark_response_format(contract)
        # 节点冻结 response_format_mode=json_object：只约束"输出是 JSON"，不把合同 schema
        # 下发成语法约束（09-19 实测 xgrammar 严格解码单素材慢 1.85 倍）；字段仍由平台按
        # 冻结合同校验，不符合会按 schema 重问一次。
        if (response_format is not None and response_format.get("type") == "json_schema"
                and getattr(self.config, "response_format_mode", "contract") == "json_object"):
            # 语法约束撤了，字段说明得跟着进提示词：09-19 run 362 只发 json_object 时
            # prompt_a 全部漏掉 required 字段 trait（250/250 熔断），提示词正文本身并没有
            # 把合同讲全，之前全靠 schema 兜着。
            _append_schema_to_system(payload, contract, protocol)
            response_format = {"type": "json_object"}
        if response_format is not None:
            if protocol == "anthropic_messages":
                raise ValueError("当前模型协议不支持 response_format 输出合同")
            payload["response_format"] = response_format
        elif use_structured_output and protocol != "anthropic_messages":
            payload["response_format"] = {"type": "json_object"}
        return actual_budget, actual_reasoning_effort, thinking_mode

    def _attempts(self, max_attempts: int | None) -> int:
        if max_attempts is None:
            return max(1, self.config.max_retries + 1)
        if (
            not isinstance(max_attempts, int)
            or isinstance(max_attempts, bool)
            or max_attempts < 1
        ):
            raise ValueError("模型调用次数必须大于 0")
        return max_attempts

    @staticmethod
    def _unpack_upstream(
        response: _UpstreamResponse | dict[str, Any],
    ) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], int | None, str | None]:
        # 保持测试替身和既有私有扩展返回 dict 时的兼容性。
        if isinstance(response, _UpstreamResponse):
            return (
                response.payload,
                response.status_code,
                response.request_correlation_id,
            )
        return response, None, None

    async def chat_json(
        self,
        system_prompt: str,
        user_prompt: str,
        image_path: Path | None = None,
        mime_type: str | None = None,
        *,
        max_attempts: int | None = None,
        output_budget: int | None = None,
        reasoning_effort: str | None = None,
        structured_output: bool | None = None,
        output_contract: PromptOutputContract | Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
    ) -> DoubaoResponse:
        content: str | list[dict[str, Any]]
        if image_path:
            content = [
                {"type": "text", "text": user_prompt},
                {"type": "image_url", "image_url": {"url": _image_data_url(image_path, mime_type)}},
            ]
        else:
            content = user_prompt
        payload = self._protocol_payload(system_prompt, content)
        contract = parse_output_contract(output_contract)
        actual_budget, actual_reasoning_effort, actual_thinking_mode = self._generation_options(
            payload,
            output_budget=output_budget,
            reasoning_effort=reasoning_effort,
            structured_output=structured_output,
            output_contract=contract,
        )

        last_error: Exception | None = None
        attempts = self._attempts(max_attempts)
        for attempt in range(attempts):
            # 只有输出解析 / 结构化校验失败后的重问才追加提示；429/5xx 重发原请求。
            current_payload = (
                _with_json_retry_nudge(payload)
                if attempt > 0
                and isinstance(last_error, DoubaoError)
                and last_error.technical_error_type not in {"429", "provider5xx", "pool_timeout"}
                else dict(payload)
            )
            try:
                upstream = await self._post(current_payload)
                raw, status_code, request_id = self._unpack_upstream(upstream)
                framing_repairs: list[str] = []
                try:
                    raw_text = _extract_message_text(raw)
                    parsed = parse_json_text(raw_text, repairs=framing_repairs)
                except DoubaoError as exc:
                    _attach_failure_usage(exc, raw)
                    exc.upstream_status_code = status_code
                    exc.request_correlation_id = request_id
                    raise
                coercions = framing_repairs + coerce_output_payload(contract, parsed)
                from .production_title_normalization import normalize_production_title
                normalization = normalize_production_title(
                    contract, parsed, getattr(self.config, "production_title_normalization_profile", None)
                )
                try:
                    validate_output_payload(contract, parsed)
                except PromptOutputContractError as exc:
                    error = DoubaoStructuredOutputError(str(exc))
                    _attach_failure_usage(error, raw)
                    error.upstream_status_code = status_code
                    error.request_correlation_id = request_id
                    error.raw_excerpt = raw_text[:600]
                    # Retain only bounded content fields as rejected candidates,
                    # never as a successful node result. Runtime evidence redacts secrets.
                    fields = parsed.get("production_fields") if isinstance(parsed, dict) else None
                    if isinstance(fields, dict) and len(json.dumps(fields, ensure_ascii=False)) <= 20000:
                        error.evidence = {"candidate_production_fields": fields, "validation_error": str(exc)}
                    if normalization:
                        error.evidence = {**(getattr(error, "evidence", None) or {}),
                                          "output_normalization": normalization}
                    raise error from exc
                usage = response_usage(raw)
                media_usage = response_media_usage(raw)
                return DoubaoResponse(
                    parsed=parsed,
                    raw_text=raw_text,
                    raw_payload=raw,
                    coercions=tuple(coercions),
                    output_normalization=tuple(normalization),
                    upstream_status_code=status_code,
                    request_correlation_id=request_id,
                    attempt_count=attempt + 1,
                    output_budget=actual_budget,
                    reasoning_effort=actual_reasoning_effort,
                    thinking_mode=actual_thinking_mode,
                    input_image_tokens=media_usage[0],
                    per_image_tokens=media_usage[1],
                    input_tokens=usage[0] if usage else None,
                    output_tokens=usage[1] if usage else None,
                    total_tokens=usage[2] if usage else None,
                    cache_usage=response_cache_usage(
                        raw,
                        input_tokens=usage[0] if usage else None,
                    ),
                )
            except Exception as exc:
                if isinstance(exc, DoubaoError):
                    exc.attempt_count = attempt + 1
                last_error = exc
                delay = _retry_delay(exc, attempt)
                if delay is None or attempt + 1 >= attempts:
                    raise
                if delay > 0:
                    await _retry_sleep(delay)
        if last_error is not None:
            raise last_error
        raise DoubaoError("模型调用失败")

    async def chat_text(
        self,
        system_prompt: str,
        user_prompt: str,
        image_path: Path | None = None,
        mime_type: str | None = None,
        *,
        max_attempts: int | None = None,
        output_budget: int | None = None,
        reasoning_effort: str | None = None,
    ) -> DoubaoResponse:
        """Call the model without imposing a JSON response contract.

        Transport/provider failures retain the normal retry policy.  Arbitrary
        text is a successful response and is therefore never retried merely
        because it is not JSON.  ``parsed`` is populated opportunistically for
        backward-compatible scoring when the text is a JSON object.
        """

        content: str | list[dict[str, Any]]
        if image_path:
            content = [
                {"type": "text", "text": user_prompt},
                {
                    "type": "image_url",
                    "image_url": {"url": _image_data_url(image_path, mime_type)},
                },
            ]
        else:
            content = user_prompt
        payload = self._protocol_payload(system_prompt, content)
        actual_budget, actual_reasoning_effort, actual_thinking_mode = self._generation_options(
            payload,
            output_budget=output_budget,
            reasoning_effort=reasoning_effort,
            structured_output=False,
        )

        last_error: Exception | None = None
        attempts = self._attempts(max_attempts)
        for attempt in range(attempts):
            try:
                upstream = await self._post(payload)
                raw, status_code, request_id = self._unpack_upstream(upstream)
                raw_text = _extract_message_text(raw)
                try:
                    parsed = parse_json_text(raw_text)
                except DoubaoError:
                    parsed = {}
                usage = response_usage(raw)
                media_usage = response_media_usage(raw)
                return DoubaoResponse(
                    parsed=parsed,
                    raw_text=raw_text,
                    raw_payload=raw,
                    upstream_status_code=status_code,
                    request_correlation_id=request_id,
                    attempt_count=attempt + 1,
                    output_budget=actual_budget,
                    reasoning_effort=actual_reasoning_effort,
                    thinking_mode=actual_thinking_mode,
                    input_image_tokens=media_usage[0],
                    per_image_tokens=media_usage[1],
                    input_tokens=usage[0] if usage else None,
                    output_tokens=usage[1] if usage else None,
                    total_tokens=usage[2] if usage else None,
                    cache_usage=response_cache_usage(
                        raw,
                        input_tokens=usage[0] if usage else None,
                    ),
                )
            except Exception as exc:
                if isinstance(exc, DoubaoError):
                    exc.attempt_count = attempt + 1
                last_error = exc
                delay = _retry_delay(exc, attempt)
                if delay is None or attempt + 1 >= attempts:
                    raise
                if delay > 0:
                    await _retry_sleep(delay)
        if last_error is not None:
            raise last_error
        raise DoubaoError("模型调用失败")


    async def chat_text_images(
        self,
        system_prompt: str,
        user_prompt: str,
        samples: Sequence[
            ProviderImageSample | tuple[str, Path, str | None]
        ],
        *,
        max_attempts: int | None = None,
        output_budget: int | None = None,
        reasoning_effort: str | None = None,
        image_detail: str = "high",
        max_image_count: int | None = None,
        max_single_image_bytes: int | None = None,
        max_total_image_bytes: int | None = None,
    ) -> DoubaoResponse:
        provider_samples = self._provider_samples(
            samples,
            legacy_detail=image_detail,
        )
        if max_image_count is not None and len(provider_samples) > max_image_count:
            raise ValueError("诊断图片数量超过安全请求上限")
        remaining_bytes = max_total_image_bytes
        input_image_bytes = 0
        content: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        prefix_samples, suffix_samples = _split_samples_for_prefix_cache(
            provider_samples
        )

        def _append_sample(sample: ProviderImageSample) -> None:
            nonlocal remaining_bytes, input_image_bytes
            limits = [
                limit
                for limit in (remaining_bytes, max_single_image_bytes)
                if limit is not None
            ]
            image_url, image_bytes = _bounded_image_data_url(
                sample.path,
                sample.mime_type,
                max_bytes=min(limits) if limits else sample.path.stat().st_size,
            )
            input_image_bytes += image_bytes
            if remaining_bytes is not None:
                remaining_bytes -= image_bytes
            content.append({"type": "text", "text": sample.label})
            content.append(_image_content(sample, image_url))

        for sample in prefix_samples:
            _append_sample(sample)
        content.append({"type": "text", "text": user_prompt})
        for sample in suffix_samples:
            _append_sample(sample)
        payload = self._protocol_payload(system_prompt, content)
        actual_budget, actual_reasoning_effort, actual_thinking_mode = self._generation_options(
            payload,
            output_budget=output_budget,
            reasoning_effort=reasoning_effort,
            structured_output=False,
        )

        last_error: Exception | None = None
        attempts = self._attempts(max_attempts)
        for attempt in range(attempts):
            try:
                upstream = await self._post(payload)
                raw, status_code, request_id = self._unpack_upstream(upstream)
                raw_text = _extract_message_text(raw)
                try:
                    parsed = parse_json_text(raw_text)
                except DoubaoError:
                    parsed = {}
                usage = response_usage(raw)
                media_usage = response_media_usage(raw)
                return DoubaoResponse(
                    parsed=parsed,
                    raw_text=raw_text,
                    raw_payload=raw,
                    upstream_status_code=status_code,
                    request_correlation_id=request_id,
                    attempt_count=attempt + 1,
                    output_budget=actual_budget,
                    reasoning_effort=actual_reasoning_effort,
                    thinking_mode=actual_thinking_mode,
                    input_image_bytes=input_image_bytes,
                    input_image_tokens=media_usage[0],
                    per_image_tokens=media_usage[1],
                    input_tokens=usage[0] if usage else None,
                    output_tokens=usage[1] if usage else None,
                    total_tokens=usage[2] if usage else None,
                    cache_usage=response_cache_usage(
                        raw,
                        input_tokens=usage[0] if usage else None,
                    ),
                )
            except Exception as exc:
                if isinstance(exc, DoubaoError):
                    exc.attempt_count = attempt + 1
                last_error = exc
                delay = _retry_delay(exc, attempt)
                if delay is None or attempt + 1 >= attempts:
                    raise
                if delay > 0:
                    await _retry_sleep(delay)
        if last_error is not None:
            raise last_error
        raise DoubaoError("模型调用失败")


    def _build_chat_json_images_request(
        self,
        system_prompt: str,
        samples: Sequence[
            ProviderImageSample | tuple[str, Path, str | None]
        ],
        *,
        user_prompt: str | None,
        output_budget: int | None,
        reasoning_effort: str | None,
        structured_output: bool | None,
        output_contract: PromptOutputContract | Mapping[str, Any] | None,
        max_image_count: int | None,
        max_single_image_bytes: int | None,
        max_total_image_bytes: int | None,
    ) -> SerializedDoubaoRequest:
        provider_samples = self._provider_samples(
            samples,
            legacy_detail="high",
        )
        if max_image_count is not None and len(provider_samples) > max_image_count:
            raise ValueError("诊断图片数量超过安全请求上限")
        remaining_bytes = max_total_image_bytes
        input_image_bytes = 0
        content: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        prefix_samples, suffix_samples = _split_samples_for_prefix_cache(
            provider_samples
        )

        def _append_sample(sample: ProviderImageSample) -> None:
            nonlocal remaining_bytes, input_image_bytes
            limits = [
                limit
                for limit in (remaining_bytes, max_single_image_bytes)
                if limit is not None
            ]
            image_url, image_bytes = _bounded_image_data_url(
                sample.path,
                sample.mime_type,
                max_bytes=(
                    min(limits) if limits else sample.path.stat().st_size
                ),
            )
            input_image_bytes += image_bytes
            if remaining_bytes is not None:
                remaining_bytes -= image_bytes
            content.append({"type": "text", "text": sample.label})
            content.append(_image_content(sample, image_url))

        for sample in prefix_samples:
            _append_sample(sample)
        if user_prompt is not None and user_prompt.strip():
            content.append({"type": "text", "text": user_prompt})
        for sample in suffix_samples:
            _append_sample(sample)
        payload = self._protocol_payload(system_prompt, content)
        contract = parse_output_contract(output_contract)
        (
            actual_budget,
            actual_reasoning_effort,
            actual_thinking_mode,
        ) = self._generation_options(
            payload,
            output_budget=output_budget,
            reasoning_effort=reasoning_effort,
            structured_output=structured_output,
            output_contract=contract,
        )
        body = json.dumps(
            payload,
            ensure_ascii=False,
            sort_keys=True,
            separators=(",", ":"),
        ).encode("utf-8")
        return SerializedDoubaoRequest(
            body=body,
            input_image_bytes=input_image_bytes,
            output_budget=actual_budget,
            reasoning_effort=actual_reasoning_effort,
            thinking_mode=actual_thinking_mode,
            sample_count=len(provider_samples),
        )

    @classmethod
    def serialize_chat_json_images_request(
        cls,
        config: ModelConfig | OptimizerConfig | Any,
        system_prompt: str,
        samples: Sequence[
            ProviderImageSample | tuple[str, Path, str | None]
        ],
        *,
        user_prompt: str | None = None,
        output_budget: int | None = None,
        reasoning_effort: str | None = None,
        structured_output: bool | None = None,
        output_contract: PromptOutputContract | Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
        max_image_count: int | None = None,
        max_single_image_bytes: int | None = None,
        max_total_image_bytes: int | None = None,
    ) -> SerializedDoubaoRequest:
        """Build a request without decrypting credentials or performing I/O."""

        builder = object.__new__(cls)
        builder.config = config
        return builder._build_chat_json_images_request(
            system_prompt,
            samples,
            user_prompt=user_prompt,
            output_budget=output_budget,
            reasoning_effort=reasoning_effort,
            structured_output=structured_output,
            output_contract=output_contract,
            max_image_count=max_image_count,
            max_single_image_bytes=max_single_image_bytes,
            max_total_image_bytes=max_total_image_bytes,
        )

    async def chat_json_images(
        self,
        system_prompt: str,
        samples: Sequence[
            ProviderImageSample | tuple[str, Path, str | None]
        ],
        *,
        user_prompt: str | None = None,
        max_attempts: int | None = None,
        output_budget: int | None = None,
        reasoning_effort: str | None = None,
        structured_output: bool | None = None,
        output_contract: PromptOutputContract | Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
        max_image_count: int | None = None,
        max_single_image_bytes: int | None = None,
        max_total_image_bytes: int | None = None,
        prepared_request: SerializedDoubaoRequest | None = None,
    ) -> DoubaoResponse:
        if prepared_request is not None:
            # The executor already embedded the exact prepared image bytes,
            # serialized the final payload and passed the request-body gate
            # before reserving budget.  Do not read files or serialize again:
            # the checked bytes below are the bytes sent on the wire.
            provider_samples = self._provider_samples(
                samples,
                legacy_detail="high",
            )
            if prepared_request.sample_count != len(provider_samples):
                raise DoubaoCapabilityMismatchError(
                    "vision_prepared_request_drift",
                    "已通过门禁的图片请求与当前样本数量不一致",
                )
            request = prepared_request
        else:
            request = self._build_chat_json_images_request(
                system_prompt,
                samples,
                user_prompt=user_prompt,
                output_budget=output_budget,
                reasoning_effort=reasoning_effort,
                structured_output=structured_output,
                output_contract=output_contract,
                max_image_count=max_image_count,
                max_single_image_bytes=max_single_image_bytes,
                max_total_image_bytes=max_total_image_bytes,
            )
        contract = parse_output_contract(output_contract)

        last_error: Exception | None = None
        attempts = self._attempts(max_attempts)
        for attempt in range(attempts):
            try:
                upstream = (
                    await self._post_serialized(request.body)
                    if prepared_request is not None
                    else await self._post(json.loads(request.body))
                )
                raw, status_code, request_id = self._unpack_upstream(upstream)
                framing_repairs: list[str] = []
                try:
                    raw_text = _extract_message_text(raw)
                    parsed = parse_json_text(raw_text, repairs=framing_repairs)
                except DoubaoError as exc:
                    _attach_failure_usage(exc, raw)
                    exc.upstream_status_code = status_code
                    exc.request_correlation_id = request_id
                    raise
                coercions = framing_repairs + coerce_output_payload(contract, parsed)
                from .production_title_normalization import normalize_production_title
                normalization = normalize_production_title(
                    contract, parsed, getattr(self.config, "production_title_normalization_profile", None)
                )
                try:
                    validate_output_payload(contract, parsed)
                except PromptOutputContractError as exc:
                    error = DoubaoStructuredOutputError(str(exc))
                    _attach_failure_usage(error, raw)
                    error.upstream_status_code = status_code
                    error.request_correlation_id = request_id
                    error.raw_excerpt = raw_text[:600]
                    # Retain only bounded content fields as rejected candidates,
                    # never as a successful node result. Runtime evidence redacts secrets.
                    fields = parsed.get("production_fields") if isinstance(parsed, dict) else None
                    if isinstance(fields, dict) and len(json.dumps(fields, ensure_ascii=False)) <= 20000:
                        error.evidence = {"candidate_production_fields": fields, "validation_error": str(exc)}
                    if normalization:
                        error.evidence = {**(getattr(error, "evidence", None) or {}),
                                          "output_normalization": normalization}
                    raise error from exc
                usage = response_usage(raw)
                media_usage = response_media_usage(raw)
                return DoubaoResponse(
                    parsed=parsed,
                    raw_text=raw_text,
                    raw_payload=raw,
                    coercions=tuple(coercions),
                    output_normalization=tuple(normalization),
                    upstream_status_code=status_code,
                    request_correlation_id=request_id,
                    attempt_count=attempt + 1,
                    output_budget=request.output_budget,
                    reasoning_effort=request.reasoning_effort,
                    thinking_mode=request.thinking_mode,
                    input_image_bytes=request.input_image_bytes,
                    input_image_tokens=media_usage[0],
                    per_image_tokens=media_usage[1],
                    input_tokens=usage[0] if usage else None,
                    output_tokens=usage[1] if usage else None,
                    total_tokens=usage[2] if usage else None,
                    cache_usage=response_cache_usage(
                        raw,
                        input_tokens=usage[0] if usage else None,
                    ),
                )
            except Exception as exc:
                if isinstance(exc, DoubaoError):
                    exc.attempt_count = attempt + 1
                last_error = exc
                delay = _retry_delay(exc, attempt)
                if delay is None or attempt + 1 >= attempts:
                    raise
                if delay > 0:
                    await _retry_sleep(delay)
        if last_error is not None:
            raise last_error
        raise DoubaoError("模型调用失败")

    async def test_connection(self) -> str:
        payload = self._protocol_payload("", "只回复：连接成功")
        protocol = getattr(self.config, "protocol", None) or "openai_chat"
        if protocol == "openai_responses":
            payload["max_output_tokens"] = 64
        elif protocol == "openai_chat" and self.config.provider == "openai":
            payload["max_completion_tokens"] = 64
            payload["reasoning_effort"] = "low"
        else:
            payload["temperature"] = 0
            payload["max_tokens"] = 64
        upstream = await self._post(payload)
        raw, _status_code, _request_id = self._unpack_upstream(upstream)
        return _extract_message_text(raw).strip()

===== FILE backend/app/doubao_endpoint_models.py =====
"""豆包推理接入点、火山账单同步与成本对账的持久化模型（schema 107）。

接入点由管理员在火山控制台创建；平台只负责录入、校验、绑定、停用与监控。
API Key 继续负责推理鉴权，接入点 ID 只负责模型路由与成本归因。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime

from sqlalchemy import (
    Boolean,
    CheckConstraint,
    DateTime,
    ForeignKey,
    Integer,
    String,
    Text,
    UniqueConstraint,
)
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column

from .database import Base
from .models import utcnow


class DoubaoInferenceEndpoint(Base):
    """一个火山方舟推理接入点（ep-xxx），最多绑定一个类目工作流。"""

    __tablename__ = "doubao_inference_endpoints"
    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint("endpoint_id", name="uq_doubao_inference_endpoints_endpoint"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status IN ('active','disabled')",
            name="ck_doubao_inference_endpoints_status",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "verification_status IN ('unverified','verified','failed')",
            name="ck_doubao_inference_endpoints_verification",
        ),
        CheckConstraint("revision >= 1", name="ck_doubao_inference_endpoints_revision"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "json_valid(verification_json) "
            "AND json_type(verification_json, '$') = 'object'",
            name="ck_doubao_inference_endpoints_verification_json",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "json_valid(tags_json) AND json_type(tags_json, '$') = 'array'",
            name="ck_doubao_inference_endpoints_tags_json",
        ),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    endpoint_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), index=True)
    display_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(160))
    category_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), index=True)
    workflow_definition_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("workflow_definitions.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"),
        nullable=True,
        index=True,
    )
    foundation_model: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(200), default="", server_default="")
    model_version: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), default="", server_default="")
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20), default="active", server_default="active", index=True)
    verification_status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(20), default="unverified", server_default="unverified"
    )
    verification_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="{}", server_default="{}")
    verified_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True)
    notes: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="", server_default="")
    # 平台通过 CreateEndpoint 自动创建时记录火山侧名称与标签；人工录入时为空。
    remote_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(160), default="", server_default="")
    provisioned_by_platform: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, server_default="0")
    tags_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="[]", server_default="[]")
    revision: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=1, server_default="1")
    created_by: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), default="system", server_default="system")
    updated_by: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), default="system", server_default="system")
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow, onupdate=utcnow
    )

class ModelCostReconciliation(Base):
    """按接入点、按业务日（北京时间）对本地流水与火山用量的对账结果。"""

    __tablename__ = "model_cost_reconciliations"

    )

    business_date: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(10), index=True)
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20), default="open", server_default="open", index=True)
    match_status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20), default="pending", server_default="pending")
    local_calls: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, server_default="0")
    local_input_tokens: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, server_default="0")

    remote_output_tokens: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, server_default="0")
