_DEFINITIONS = {item.key: item for item in _CATALOG}


def module_type_catalog() -> tuple[ModuleTypeDefinition, ...]:
    return _CATALOG


def require_module_type(key: str) -> ModuleTypeDefinition:
    definition = _DEFINITIONS.get(key)
    if definition is None:
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            "module_type_unknown", f"规则模块类型未注册：{key or '（空）'}"
        )
    return definition


def _validate_redline(content: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    content.setdefault("format_version", REDLINE_POLICY_FORMAT_VERSION)
    content.setdefault("hit_score_cap", 49)
    content.setdefault("rules", [])
    if content.get("signal") == "precheck.红线检查.命中项[].类型":
        _validate_text_redline(content)
        return
    validate_redline_policy(content)


def _validate_text_redline(content: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    if content.get("format_version") != REDLINE_POLICY_FORMAT_VERSION:
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            "module_content_invalid.format_version_unsupported",
            f"准入与红线版本必须是 {REDLINE_POLICY_FORMAT_VERSION}",
        )
    if not isinstance(content.get("enabled"), bool):
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            "module_content_invalid.enabled_not_bool",
            "文本红线 enabled 必须是布尔值",
        )
    if content.get("hit_level") not in {"L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5"}:
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            "module_content_invalid.hit_level_invalid",
            "文本红线命中等级必须是 L1 至 L5",
        )
    hit_score_cap = content.get("hit_score_cap")
    if (
        not isinstance(hit_score_cap, int)
        or isinstance(hit_score_cap, bool)
        or not 0 <= hit_score_cap <= 100
    ):
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            "module_content_invalid.hit_score_cap_out_of_range",
            "文本红线分数上限必须是 0 至 100 的整数",
        )
    if "terminal" in content and not isinstance(content["terminal"], bool):
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            "module_content_invalid.terminal_not_bool",
            "文本红线 terminal 必须是布尔值",
        )
    rules = content.get("rules")
    if not isinstance(rules, list):
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            "module_content_invalid.rules_not_list", "文本红线 rules 必须是数组"
        )
    seen_keys: set[str] = set()
    for rule in rules:
        if not isinstance(rule, Mapping):
            raise EvaluationComponentError(
                "module_content_invalid.rule_not_object", "文本红线规则必须是对象"
            )
        rule_key = rule.get("rule_key")
        if not isinstance(rule_key, str) or re.fullmatch(
            r"[a-z][a-z0-9_]{2,39}", rule_key
        ) is None:
            raise EvaluationComponentError(
                "module_content_invalid.rule_key_invalid",
                "文本红线 rule_key 不符合命名规范",
            )
        if rule_key in seen_keys:
            raise EvaluationComponentError(
                "module_content_invalid.rule_key_duplicate",
                f"文本红线 rule_key 重复：{rule_key}",
            )
        seen_keys.add(rule_key)
        match_any = rule.get("match_any")
        if (
            not isinstance(match_any, list)
            or not match_any
            or any(not isinstance(item, str) or not item.strip() for item in match_any)
        ):
            raise EvaluationComponentError(
                "module_content_invalid.match_any_empty",
                "文本红线 match_any 必须是非空业务值字符串数组",
            )


def _validate_track(content: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    content.setdefault("format_version", "track-classification-v1")
    if isinstance(content.get("tracks"), Mapping):
        _validate_text_track_mapping(content)
        return
    validate_track_classification(content)


def _validate_text_track_mapping(content: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    if content.get("format_version") != "track-classification-v1":
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            "module_content_invalid.track_classification_version",
            "文本赛道识别版本必须是 track-classification-v1",
        )
    source = content.get("source")
    if not isinstance(source, str) or not source.strip():
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            "module_content_invalid.track_source_invalid",
            "文本赛道识别 source 必须是非空字符串",
        )
    tracks = content["tracks"]
    if not tracks:
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            "module_content_invalid.tracks_empty", "文本赛道至少需要一个赛道"
        )
    for track_key, track in tracks.items():
        if not isinstance(track_key, str) or re.fullmatch(
            r"[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,39}", track_key
        ) is None:
            raise EvaluationComponentError(
                "module_content_invalid.track_key_invalid",
                "文本赛道 key 不符合命名规范",
            )
        if not isinstance(track, Mapping):
            raise EvaluationComponentError(
                "module_content_invalid.track_not_object", "文本赛道配置必须是对象"
            )
        if not isinstance(track.get("display_name"), str) or not track[
            "display_name"
        ].strip():
            raise EvaluationComponentError(
                "module_content_invalid.track_label_invalid",
                "文本赛道 display_name 必须是非空字符串",
            )
        members = track.get("members")
        if not isinstance(members, list) or any(
            not isinstance(item, str) or not item.strip() for item in members
        ):
            raise EvaluationComponentError(
                "module_content_invalid.track_members_invalid",
                "文本赛道 members 必须是业务值字符串数组",
            )
        if len(members) != len(set(members)):
            raise EvaluationComponentError(
                "module_content_invalid.track_members_duplicate",
                "文本赛道 members 不得重复",
            )
        maxima = []
        for field in ("visual_max", "narrative_max", "innovation_max"):
            value = track.get(field)
            if (
                not isinstance(value, int)
                or isinstance(value, bool)
                or not 0 <= value <= 100
            ):
                raise EvaluationComponentError(
                    "module_content_invalid.track_score_out_of_range",
                    f"文本赛道 {field} 必须是 0 至 100 的整数",
                )
            maxima.append(value)
        if sum(maxima) > 100:
            raise EvaluationComponentError(
                "module_content_invalid.track_cap_inconsistent",
                "文本赛道三个分项上限之和不得超过 100",
            )
    resolution = content.get("other_resolution")
    if resolution is not None:
        if not isinstance(resolution, Mapping):
            raise EvaluationComponentError(
                "module_content_invalid.other_resolution_invalid",
                "文本赛道 other_resolution 必须是对象",
            )
        fallback = resolution.get("fallback_track")
        if fallback is not None and fallback not in tracks:
            raise EvaluationComponentError(
                "module_content_invalid.default_track_unknown",
                "文本赛道 fallback_track 必须引用已定义赛道",
            )


def _validate_media(content: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    validate_common_modifiers(
        {
            "format_version": COMMON_MODIFIERS_V2_FORMAT_VERSION,
            "media_type_penalty": content,
        }
    )


def _validate_quality(content: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    veto = content.get("high_score_veto")
    modifier_format = (
        COMMON_MODIFIERS_V2_FORMAT_VERSION
        if isinstance(veto, dict)
        and ("policy_version" in veto or "tiers" in veto)
        else COMMON_MODIFIERS_FORMAT_VERSION
    )
    common = {
        "format_version": modifier_format,
        **{
            key: content[key]
            for key in ("high_score_veto", "hard_defect_penalty")
            if key in content
        },
    }
    validate_common_modifiers(common)
    if QUALITY_RULES_KEY in content:
        validate_quality_rules_block({QUALITY_RULES_KEY: content[QUALITY_RULES_KEY]})


def _validate_dimensions(content: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    dimensions = content.get("subcategory_dimensions", {})
    if not isinstance(dimensions, dict):
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            "module_content_invalid.subcategory_dimensions_not_object",
            "维度计分的赛道维度必须是对象",
        )
    if "profile_type" in dimensions:
        _validate_text_dimension_profile(content, dimensions)
        return
    for track_key, config in dimensions.items():
        if not isinstance(track_key, str) or not track_key.strip():
            raise EvaluationComponentError(
                "module_content_invalid.track_key_invalid",
                "维度计分包含空赛道名称",
            )
        validate_subcategory_dimensions(config)
    deduction_rules = content.get("dimension_deduction_rules", {})
    if not isinstance(deduction_rules, dict):
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            "module_content_invalid.dimension_deduction_rules_not_object",
            "维度扣分规则必须是对象",
        )


def _validate_text_dimension_profile(
    content: dict[str, Any], dimensions: Mapping[str, Any]
) -> None:
    profile_type = dimensions.get("profile_type")
    if not isinstance(profile_type, str) or not profile_type.strip():
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            "module_content_invalid.dimension_profile_invalid",
            "文本维度计分 profile_type 必须是非空字符串",
        )
    tracks = dimensions.get("tracks")
    if (
        not isinstance(tracks, list)
        or not tracks
        or any(not isinstance(item, str) or not item.strip() for item in tracks)
        or len(tracks) != len(set(tracks))
    ):
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            "module_content_invalid.dimension_tracks_invalid",
            "文本维度计分 tracks 必须是非空且去重的字符串数组",
        )
    deduction_rules = content.get("dimension_deduction_rules", {})
    if not isinstance(deduction_rules, Mapping):
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            "module_content_invalid.dimension_deduction_rules_not_object",
            "文本维度扣分规则必须是对象",
        )


def _validate_anchor(content: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    validate_anchor_mechanism({ANCHOR_MECHANISM_KEY: content})


def _validate_level_matcher(content: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    resolve_level_scale({"level_scale": content})
    if "recommendation_calibration" in content:
        from .recommendation_calibration import validate_profile

        validate_profile(content["recommendation_calibration"])
    formula = content.get("case_score_formula")
    if formula is None:
        return
    expected_fields = {
        "schema_version",
        "model_score_field",
        "quality_score_field",
        "time_score_field",
        "threshold_field",
        "comparison",
        "deletion_mode",
    }
    if not isinstance(formula, Mapping) or set(formula) != expected_fields:
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            "module_content_invalid.case_score_formula_invalid",
            "案例最终评分合同字段不完整或包含未知字段",
        )
    field_keys = (
        "model_score_field",
        "quality_score_field",
        "time_score_field",
        "threshold_field",
    )
    if any(
        not isinstance(formula.get(key), str)
        or re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]{0,79}", formula[key]) is None
        for key in field_keys
    ):
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            "module_content_invalid.case_score_formula_invalid",
            "案例最终评分字段映射必须是安全字段名",
        )
    if (
        formula.get("schema_version") != "case-final-score-v1"
        or formula.get("comparison") != "strict_gt"
        or formula.get("deletion_mode") != "recommend_only"
    ):
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            "module_content_invalid.case_score_formula_invalid",
            "案例最终评分只允许 strict_gt 且只能输出删图建议",
        )



def _validate_classification_level_map(content: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    from .classification_level_map import (
        ClassificationLevelMapError,
        validate_classification_level_map_content,
    )

    try:
        validate_classification_level_map_content(content)
    except ClassificationLevelMapError as exc:
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            f"module_content_invalid.{exc.code}", f"分类定级表配置无效：{exc}"
        ) from exc


def _validate_upstream_type_short_circuit(content: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    from .upstream_type_short_circuit import (
        UpstreamTypeShortCircuitError,
        validate_upstream_type_short_circuit_content,
    )

    try:
        validate_upstream_type_short_circuit_content(content)
    except UpstreamTypeShortCircuitError as exc:
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            f"module_content_invalid.{exc.code}", f"上游类型前置短路配置无效：{exc}"
        ) from exc


def _validate_dictionary_filter(content: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    from .dictionary_filter import DictionaryFilterError, validate_dictionary_filter_content

    try:
        validate_dictionary_filter_content(content)
    except DictionaryFilterError as exc:
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            f"module_content_invalid.{exc.code}", f"字典表撞库过滤配置无效：{exc}"
        ) from exc


_VALIDATORS: dict[str, Callable[[dict[str, Any]], None]] = {
    "eligibility_redline": _validate_redline,
    "track_routing": _validate_track,
    "media_adjustment": _validate_media,
    "quality_cap_exception": _validate_quality,
    "dimension_scoring": _validate_dimensions,
    "anchor_calibration": _validate_anchor,
    "score_level_matcher": _validate_level_matcher,
    "dictionary_filter": _validate_dictionary_filter,
    "classification_level_map": _validate_classification_level_map,
    "upstream_type_short_circuit": _validate_upstream_type_short_circuit,
}


def _validate_correction_guidance(value: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if not isinstance(value, Mapping):
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            "module_content_invalid.correction_guidance_not_object",
            "规则纠偏说明必须是对象",
        )
    source_version_id = value.get("source_version_id")
    evidence_fingerprint = value.get("evidence_fingerprint")
    fields = value.get("fields")
    if value.get("schema_version") != "rule-correction-guidance-v1":
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            "module_content_invalid.correction_guidance_version",
            "规则纠偏说明版本不受支持",
        )
    if (
        not isinstance(source_version_id, int)
        or isinstance(source_version_id, bool)
        or source_version_id < 1
    ):
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            "module_content_invalid.correction_guidance_source",
            "规则纠偏说明缺少有效源版本",
        )
    if not isinstance(evidence_fingerprint, str) or re.fullmatch(
        r"[0-9a-f]{64}", evidence_fingerprint
    ) is None:
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            "module_content_invalid.correction_guidance_evidence",
            "规则纠偏说明缺少有效证据指纹",
        )
    if not isinstance(fields, list) or not fields:
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            "module_content_invalid.correction_guidance_fields",
            "规则纠偏说明至少需要一个字段",
        )
    normalized_fields: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for item in fields:
        if not isinstance(item, Mapping):
            raise EvaluationComponentError(
                "module_content_invalid.correction_guidance_field",
                "规则纠偏字段说明必须是对象",
            )
        field_path = item.get("field_path")
        operator_guidance = item.get("operator_guidance")
        if not isinstance(field_path, str) or not field_path.strip():
            raise EvaluationComponentError(
                "module_content_invalid.correction_guidance_field_path",
                "规则纠偏字段路径不能为空",
            )
        if (
            not isinstance(operator_guidance, list)
            or not operator_guidance
            or any(
                not isinstance(note, str) or not note.strip()
                for note in operator_guidance
            )
        ):
            raise EvaluationComponentError(
                "module_content_invalid.correction_guidance_notes",
                "规则纠偏字段必须包含运营说明",
            )
        normalized_fields.append(
            {
                "field_path": field_path.strip(),
                "operator_guidance": [note.strip() for note in operator_guidance],
            }
        )
    return {
        "schema_version": "rule-correction-guidance-v1",
        "source_version_id": source_version_id,
        "evidence_fingerprint": evidence_fingerprint,
        "fields": normalized_fields,
    }


def validate_module_content(
    key: str, content: Mapping[str, Any]
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    definition = require_module_type(key)
    if not isinstance(content, Mapping):
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            "module_content_not_object", f"{definition.display_name}配置必须是对象"
        )
    try:
        normalized = json.loads(canonical_json(dict(content)))
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            "module_content_not_json", f"{definition.display_name}配置不是合法 JSON"
        ) from exc
    correction_guidance = normalized.pop("correction_guidance", None)
    try:
        _VALIDATORS[definition.key](normalized)
    except EvaluationComponentError:
        raise
    except ValueError as exc:
        reason_code = str(getattr(exc, "code", "invalid"))
        label = "命中等级" if reason_code == "hit_level_invalid" else "内容"
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            f"module_content_invalid.{reason_code}",
            f"{definition.display_name}的{label}配置无效：{exc}",
        ) from exc
    if correction_guidance is not None:
        normalized["correction_guidance"] = _validate_correction_guidance(
            correction_guidance
        )
    return normalized


def canonical_component_hash(content: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
    try:
        encoded = canonical_json(dict(content))
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            "module_content_not_json", "组件内容不是合法 JSON"
        ) from exc
    return hashlib.sha256(encoded.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()

===== FILE backend/app/evaluation_components.py =====
"""Services for operator-managed prompt and rule-module components."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import re
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any, Literal, Mapping

from sqlalchemy import func, select, update
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .dimension_schema_registry import canonical_json
from .evaluation_component_catalog import (
    EvaluationComponentError,
    canonical_component_hash,
    require_module_type,
    validate_module_content,
)
from .models import (
    CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding,
    EvaluationCategoryProfile,
    EvaluationComponentWorkingCopy,
    PromptVersion,
    RuleModuleVersion,
    WorkflowNodeAsset,
    WorkflowNodeAssetBinding,
    WorkflowVersion,
)
from .workflow_assets import bind_prompt_version, bind_rule_module_version


_ASSET_KEY_PATTERN = re.compile(r"^[a-z][a-z0-9_]{0,79}$")
_PROMPT_STAGE_PATTERN = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,19}$")
_COMPONENT_KINDS = frozenset({"prompt", "rule_module"})


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ComponentVersionRef:
    kind: Literal["prompt", "rule_module"]
    id: int
    version: str
    content_hash: str
    reference_facts: dict[str, bool]


def _require_active_category(db: Session, category_key: str) -> None:
    profile = db.scalar(
        select(EvaluationCategoryProfile).where(
            EvaluationCategoryProfile.category_key == category_key
        )
    )
    if profile is None or profile.status == "retired":
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            "component_category_unknown",
            "组件所属类目不存在或已经停用",
            status_code=404,
        )


def _validate_asset_identity(
    *, asset_key: str, display_name: str, kind: str
) -> None:
    if kind not in _COMPONENT_KINDS:
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            "component_kind_invalid", "组件类型只支持提示词或规则模块"
        )
    if not _ASSET_KEY_PATTERN.fullmatch(asset_key):
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            "component_asset_key_invalid",
            "组件键只能使用小写字母、数字和下划线，并且必须以字母开头",
        )
    if not display_name or len(display_name) > 160:
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            "component_display_name_invalid",
            "组件名称不能为空且不能超过 160 个字符",
        )


def _editor_schema_for(asset: WorkflowNodeAsset) -> str:
    if asset.kind == "prompt":
        return "prompt-component-editor-v1"
    return require_module_type(str(asset.module_type_key or "")).editor_schema_version


def create_component_asset(
    db: Session,
    *,
    category_key: str,
    asset_key: str,
    kind: Literal["prompt", "rule_module"] | str,
    display_name: str,
    module_type_key: str | None = None,
    prompt_stage: str | None = None,
    description: str = "",
    created_by: str,
) -> WorkflowNodeAsset:
    category_key = category_key.strip()
    asset_key = asset_key.strip()
    display_name = display_name.strip()
    _require_active_category(db, category_key)
    _validate_asset_identity(
        asset_key=asset_key, display_name=display_name, kind=kind
    )
    if kind == "rule_module":
        require_module_type(module_type_key or "")
        if prompt_stage is not None:
            raise EvaluationComponentError(
                "rule_prompt_stage_forbidden",
                "规则模块不能设置提示词节点标识",
            )
    else:
        if module_type_key is not None:
            raise EvaluationComponentError(
                "prompt_module_type_forbidden",
                "提示词资产不能设置规则模块类型",
            )
        if not prompt_stage or not _PROMPT_STAGE_PATTERN.fullmatch(prompt_stage):
            raise EvaluationComponentError(
                "prompt_stage_invalid",
                "提示词节点标识必须填写，例如 A、B 或 C",
            )
    existing = db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowNodeAsset.id).where(
            WorkflowNodeAsset.category_key == category_key,
            WorkflowNodeAsset.asset_key == asset_key,
        )
    )
    if existing is not None:
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            "component_asset_conflict",
            "该类目下已经存在同名组件",
            status_code=409,
        )
    asset = WorkflowNodeAsset(
        category_key=category_key,
        asset_key=asset_key,
        kind=kind,
        display_name=display_name,
        module_type_key=module_type_key,
        description=description.strip(),
        is_discoverable=True,
        status="active",
        created_by=created_by,
    )
    db.add(asset)
    db.flush()
    empty_content = {"stage": prompt_stage} if kind == "prompt" else {}
    db.add(
        EvaluationComponentWorkingCopy(
            asset_id=asset.id,
            content_json=canonical_json(empty_content),
            editor_schema_version=_editor_schema_for(asset),
            lock_version=1,
            updated_by=created_by,
        )
    )
    db.flush()
    return asset


def require_component_asset(db: Session, asset_id: int) -> WorkflowNodeAsset:
    asset = db.get(WorkflowNodeAsset, asset_id)
    if asset is None or asset.kind not in _COMPONENT_KINDS:
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            "component_asset_unknown", "组件不存在", status_code=404
        )
    return asset


def get_component_working_copy(
    db: Session, asset_id: int
) -> EvaluationComponentWorkingCopy:
    require_component_asset(db, asset_id)
    row = db.scalar(
        select(EvaluationComponentWorkingCopy).where(
            EvaluationComponentWorkingCopy.asset_id == asset_id
        )
    )
    if row is None:
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            "component_working_copy_unknown",
            "组件工作副本不存在",
            status_code=404,
        )
    return row


def _validate_prompt_content(
    content: Mapping[str, Any], *, expected_stage: str | None
) -> dict[str, str]:
    stage = content.get("stage")
    system_prompt = content.get("system_prompt")
    user_prompt = content.get("user_prompt", "")
    rubric_version = content.get("rubric_version")
    if not isinstance(stage, str) or not _PROMPT_STAGE_PATTERN.fullmatch(stage):
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            "prompt_stage_invalid", "提示词节点标识必须填写，例如 A、B 或 C"
        )
    if expected_stage is not None and stage != expected_stage:
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            "prompt_stage_immutable", "提示词节点标识创建后不能修改"
        )
    if not isinstance(system_prompt, str) or not system_prompt.strip():
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            "prompt_system_prompt_required", "系统提示词不能为空"
        )
    if not isinstance(user_prompt, str):
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            "prompt_user_prompt_invalid", "用户提示词必须是文本"
        )
    if not isinstance(rubric_version, str) or not rubric_version.strip():
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            "prompt_rubric_version_required", "评测说明版本不能为空"
        )
    return {
        "stage": stage,
        "system_prompt": system_prompt,
        "user_prompt": user_prompt,
        "rubric_version": rubric_version,
    }


def _validate_component_content(
    asset: WorkflowNodeAsset,
    content: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    expected_stage: str | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if not isinstance(content, Mapping):
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            "component_content_not_object", "组件内容必须是对象"
        )
    if asset.kind == "prompt":
        return _validate_prompt_content(content, expected_stage=expected_stage)
    module_type_key = str(asset.module_type_key or "")
    normalized = validate_module_content(module_type_key, content)
    # 运营写入口专用的一致性拦截：只约束新保存的配置，冻结版本与运行时不受影响。
    from .mechanism_authoring_policy import (
        MechanismAuthoringPolicyError,
        apply_module_authoring_policy,
    )

    try:
        return apply_module_authoring_policy(module_type_key, normalized)
    except MechanismAuthoringPolicyError as exc:
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            f"module_content_invalid.{exc.code}", str(exc)
        ) from exc


def save_component_working_copy(
    db: Session,
    *,
    asset_id: int,
    expected_lock_version: int,
    content: Mapping[str, Any],
    updated_by: str,
) -> EvaluationComponentWorkingCopy:
    asset = require_component_asset(db, asset_id)
    if asset.status != "active":
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            "component_asset_retired",
            "已停用组件不能继续编辑",
            status_code=409,
        )
    current = get_component_working_copy(db, asset_id)
    current_content = json.loads(current.content_json)
    expected_stage = (
        current_content.get("stage") if asset.kind == "prompt" else None
    )
    normalized = _validate_component_content(
        asset, content, expected_stage=expected_stage
    )
    result = db.execute(
        update(EvaluationComponentWorkingCopy)
        .where(
            EvaluationComponentWorkingCopy.asset_id == asset_id,
            EvaluationComponentWorkingCopy.lock_version == expected_lock_version,
        )
        .values(
            content_json=canonical_json(normalized),
            lock_version=EvaluationComponentWorkingCopy.lock_version + 1,
            updated_by=updated_by,
            updated_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
        )
        .execution_options(synchronize_session=False)
    )
    if result.rowcount != 1:
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            "component_working_copy_conflict",
            "工作副本已被其他人更新，请刷新后重试",
            status_code=409,
        )
    db.expire_all()
    return get_component_working_copy(db, asset_id)


def _next_prompt_sequence(db: Session, asset_id: int) -> int:
    count = db.scalar(
        select(func.count(WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.id)).where(
            WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.asset_id == asset_id,
            WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.prompt_version_id.is_not(None),
        )
    )
    return int(count or 0) + 1


def _next_rule_module_version(db: Session, asset_id: int) -> int:
    maximum = db.scalar(
        select(func.max(RuleModuleVersion.version)).where(
            RuleModuleVersion.asset_id == asset_id
        )
    )
    return int(maximum or 0) + 1


def _manifest_nodes(raw: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    try:
        parsed = json.loads(raw)
    except (TypeError, json.JSONDecodeError):
        return []
    if isinstance(parsed, dict):
        parsed = parsed.get("nodes")
    if not isinstance(parsed, list):
        return []
    return [item for item in parsed if isinstance(item, dict)]


def component_reference_facts(
    db: Session,
    *,
    prompt_version_id: int | None = None,
    rule_module_version_id: int | None = None,
) -> dict[str, bool]:
    if (prompt_version_id is None) == (rule_module_version_id is None):
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            "component_reference_shape_invalid",
            "查询引用时必须且只能选择一个组件版本",
        )
    version_key = (
        "prompt_version_id"
        if prompt_version_id is not None
        else "rule_module_version_id"
    )
    version_id = (
        prompt_version_id
        if prompt_version_id is not None
        else rule_module_version_id
    )
    referenced_workflows = {
        row.id
        for row in db.scalars(
            select(WorkflowVersion).where(
                WorkflowVersion.workflow_schema_version == "workflow-v3"
            )
        ).all()
        if any(
            isinstance(node.get("config"), dict)
            and node["config"].get(version_key) == version_id
            for node in _manifest_nodes(row.step_manifest_json)
        )
    }
    current_workflows = set(
        db.scalars(
            select(CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding.workflow_version_id)
        ).all()
    )
    return {
        "current_incremental": bool(referenced_workflows & current_workflows),
        "historical": bool(referenced_workflows - current_workflows),
        "unreferenced": not referenced_workflows,
    }


def _generate_prompt_version(
    db: Session,
    *,
    asset: WorkflowNodeAsset,
    working: EvaluationComponentWorkingCopy,
    content: Mapping[str, Any],
    created_by: str,
) -> ComponentVersionRef:
    digest = canonical_component_hash(content)
    row = next(
        (
            version
            for version in db.scalars(
                select(PromptVersion)
                .join(
                    WorkflowNodeAssetBinding,
                    WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.prompt_version_id
                    == PromptVersion.id,
                )
                .where(
                    WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.asset_id == asset.id,
                    PromptVersion.status != "archived",
                )
                .order_by(PromptVersion.id.asc())
            ).all()
            if canonical_component_hash(
                {
                    "stage": version.stage,
                    "system_prompt": version.system_prompt,
                    "user_prompt": version.user_prompt,
                    "rubric_version": version.rubric_version,
                }
            )
            == digest
        ),
        None,
    )
    if row is None:
        sequence = _next_prompt_sequence(db, asset.id)
        row = PromptVersion(
            category_key=asset.category_key,
            pipeline_scope="shared",
            stage=str(content["stage"]),
            name=asset.display_name,
            version=f"component-v{sequence}",
            system_prompt=str(content["system_prompt"]),
            user_prompt=str(content["user_prompt"]),
            rubric_version=str(content["rubric_version"]),
            status="published",
            source="workflow_component",
            change_note=f"由组件工作副本 revision {working.lock_version} 生成",
            created_by=created_by,
        )
        db.add(row)
        db.flush()
        bind_prompt_version(
            db, asset=asset, prompt_version=row, created_by=created_by
        )
    working.base_prompt_version_id = row.id
    db.flush()
    return ComponentVersionRef(
        "prompt",
        row.id,
        row.version,
        digest,
        component_reference_facts(db, prompt_version_id=row.id),
    )


def _generate_rule_module_version(
    db: Session,
    *,
    asset: WorkflowNodeAsset,
    working: EvaluationComponentWorkingCopy,
    content: Mapping[str, Any],
    created_by: str,
) -> ComponentVersionRef:
    digest = canonical_component_hash(content)
    row = db.scalar(
        select(RuleModuleVersion).where(
            RuleModuleVersion.asset_id == asset.id,
            RuleModuleVersion.content_hash == digest,
        )
    )
    if row is None:
        row = RuleModuleVersion(
            asset_id=asset.id,
            version=_next_rule_module_version(db, asset.id),
            module_type_key=str(asset.module_type_key),
            content_json=canonical_json(content),
            content_hash=digest,
            source_working_copy_revision=working.lock_version,
            created_by=created_by,
        )
        db.add(row)
        db.flush()
        bind_rule_module_version(
            db, asset=asset, rule_module_version=row, created_by=created_by
        )
    working.base_rule_module_version_id = row.id
    db.flush()
    return ComponentVersionRef(
        "rule_module",
        row.id,
        str(row.version),
        digest,
        component_reference_facts(db, rule_module_version_id=row.id),
    )


def generate_component_version(
    db: Session, *, asset_id: int, created_by: str
) -> ComponentVersionRef:
    asset = require_component_asset(db, asset_id)
    if asset.status != "active":
        raise EvaluationComponentError(
            "component_asset_retired",
            "已停用组件不能生成新版本",
            status_code=409,
        )
    working = get_component_working_copy(db, asset_id)
    raw = json.loads(working.content_json)
    normalized = _validate_component_content(
        asset,
        raw,
        expected_stage=raw.get("stage") if asset.kind == "prompt" else None,
    )
    if asset.kind == "prompt":
        return _generate_prompt_version(
            db,
            asset=asset,
            working=working,
            content=normalized,
            created_by=created_by,
        )
    return _generate_rule_module_version(
        db,
        asset=asset,
        working=working,
        content=normalized,
        created_by=created_by,
    )


def copy_component_asset(
    db: Session,
    *,
    source_asset_id: int,
    target_category_key: str,
    target_asset_key: str,
    display_name: str,
    created_by: str,
) -> WorkflowNodeAsset:
    source = require_component_asset(db, source_asset_id)
    source_copy = get_component_working_copy(db, source.id)
    source_content = json.loads(source_copy.content_json)
    target = create_component_asset(
        db,
        category_key=target_category_key,
        asset_key=target_asset_key,
        kind=source.kind,
        display_name=display_name,
        module_type_key=source.module_type_key,
        prompt_stage=(
            str(source_content.get("stage"))
            if source.kind == "prompt"
            else None
        ),
        description=source.description,
        created_by=created_by,
    )
    target.copied_from_asset_id = source.id
    target_copy = get_component_working_copy(db, target.id)
    target_copy.content_json = source_copy.content_json
    target_copy.editor_schema_version = source_copy.editor_schema_version
    db.flush()
    return target


def update_component_asset(
    db: Session,
    *,
    asset_id: int,
    display_name: str | None = None,
    description: str | None = None,
    is_discoverable: bool | None = None,
) -> WorkflowNodeAsset:
    asset = require_component_asset(db, asset_id)
    if display_name is not None:
        cleaned = display_name.strip()
        if not cleaned or len(cleaned) > 160:
            raise EvaluationComponentError(
                "component_display_name_invalid",
                "组件名称不能为空且不能超过 160 个字符",
            )
        asset.display_name = cleaned
    if description is not None:
        asset.description = description.strip()
    if is_discoverable is not None:
        asset.is_discoverable = is_discoverable
    db.flush()
    return asset


def set_component_discoverability(
    db: Session, *, asset_id: int, is_discoverable: bool
) -> WorkflowNodeAsset:
    return update_component_asset(
        db, asset_id=asset_id, is_discoverable=is_discoverable
    )


def list_component_assets(
    db: Session, *, category_key: str, include_hidden: bool = False
) -> list[WorkflowNodeAsset]:
    statement = select(WorkflowNodeAsset).where(
        WorkflowNodeAsset.category_key == category_key,
        WorkflowNodeAsset.kind.in_(_COMPONENT_KINDS),
    )
    if not include_hidden:
        statement = statement.where(WorkflowNodeAsset.is_discoverable.is_(True))
    return list(
        db.scalars(
            statement.order_by(WorkflowNodeAsset.asset_key, WorkflowNodeAsset.id)
        ).all()
    )

===== FILE backend/app/evaluation_components_api.py =====
"""HTTP API for operator-managed evaluation components."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from dataclasses import asdict
from typing import Any, Callable

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .authz import has_permission
from .database import get_db
from .evaluation_component_catalog import (
    EvaluationComponentError,
    canonical_component_hash,
    module_type_catalog,
)
from .evaluation_components import (
    component_reference_facts,
    copy_component_asset,
    create_component_asset,
    generate_component_version,
    get_component_working_copy,
    list_component_assets,
    require_component_asset,
    save_component_working_copy,
    update_component_asset,
)
from .models import (
    PromptVersion,
    RuleModuleVersion,
    User,
    WorkflowNodeAsset,
    WorkflowNodeAssetBinding,
)


class ComponentCreate(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    category_key: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=40)
    asset_key: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=80)
    kind: str
    display_name: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=160)
    module_type_key: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=60)
    prompt_stage: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=20)
    description: str = ""


class ComponentUpdate(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    display_name: str | None = Field(default=None, min_length=1, max_length=160)
    description: str | None = None
    is_discoverable: bool | None = None


class ComponentCopy(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    target_category_key: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=40)
    target_asset_key: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=80)
    display_name: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=160)


class WorkingCopySave(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    expected_lock_version: int = Field(ge=1)
    content: dict[str, Any]


def _http_error(exc: EvaluationComponentError) -> HTTPException:
    return HTTPException(
        status_code=exc.status_code,
        detail={"code": exc.code, "message": str(exc)},
    )


def _require_permission(user: User, permission: str) -> None:
    if has_permission(user, permission):
        return
    code = (
        "component_version_forbidden"
        if permission == "evaluation_components:version"
        else "component_write_forbidden"
    )
    raise HTTPException(
        status_code=403,
        detail={"code": code, "message": "当前账号没有所需的组件管理权限"},
    )


def _asset_payload(row: WorkflowNodeAsset) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "id": row.id,
        "category_key": row.category_key,
        "asset_key": row.asset_key,
        "kind": row.kind,
        "display_name": row.display_name,
        "module_type_key": row.module_type_key,
        "description": row.description,
        "is_discoverable": row.is_discoverable,
        "copied_from_asset_id": row.copied_from_asset_id,
        "created_by": row.created_by,
        "created_at": row.created_at,
        "updated_at": row.updated_at,
    }


def _working_copy_payload(row) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "id": row.id,
        "asset_id": row.asset_id,
        "content": json.loads(row.content_json),
        "editor_schema_version": row.editor_schema_version,
        "lock_version": row.lock_version,
        "updated_by": row.updated_by,
        "updated_at": row.updated_at,
    }


def _version_items(db: Session, asset: WorkflowNodeAsset) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    if asset.kind == "rule_module":
        rows = db.scalars(
            select(RuleModuleVersion)
            .where(RuleModuleVersion.asset_id == asset.id)
            .order_by(RuleModuleVersion.version.desc())
        ).all()
        return [
            {
                "kind": "rule_module",
                "id": row.id,
                "version": str(row.version),
                "content_hash": row.content_hash,
                "content": json.loads(row.content_json),
                "source_working_copy_revision": row.source_working_copy_revision,
                "created_by": row.created_by,
                "created_at": row.created_at,
                "reference_facts": component_reference_facts(
                    db, rule_module_version_id=row.id
                ),
            }
            for row in rows
        ]
    prompt_ids = db.scalars(
        select(WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.prompt_version_id).where(
            WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.asset_id == asset.id,
            WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.prompt_version_id.is_not(None),
        )
    ).all()
    rows = (
        db.scalars(
            select(PromptVersion)
            .where(PromptVersion.id.in_(prompt_ids))
            .order_by(PromptVersion.id.desc())
        ).all()
        if prompt_ids
        else []
    )
    return [
        {
            "kind": "prompt",
            "id": row.id,
            "version": row.version,
            "content": {
                "stage": row.stage,
                "system_prompt": row.system_prompt,
                "user_prompt": row.user_prompt,
                "rubric_version": row.rubric_version,
            },
            "content_hash": canonical_component_hash(
                {
                    "stage": row.stage,
                    "system_prompt": row.system_prompt,
                    "user_prompt": row.user_prompt,
                    "rubric_version": row.rubric_version,
                }
            ),
            "created_by": row.created_by,
            "created_at": row.created_at,
            "reference_facts": component_reference_facts(
                db, prompt_version_id=row.id
            ),
        }
        for row in rows
    ]


def build_evaluation_components_router(
    require_user: Callable[..., Any],
) -> APIRouter:
    router = APIRouter(
        prefix="/api/evaluation-components", tags=["evaluation-components"]
    )

    @router.get("/module-types")
    def list_module_types(
        _user: User = Depends(require_user),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {"items": [asdict(item) for item in module_type_catalog()]}

    @router.get("")
    def list_assets(
        category_key: str = Query(min_length=1, max_length=40),
        include_hidden: bool = False,
        _user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "items": [
                _asset_payload(row)
                for row in list_component_assets(
                    db,
                    category_key=category_key,
                    include_hidden=include_hidden,
                )
            ]
        }

    @router.post("", status_code=201)
    def create_asset(
        payload: ComponentCreate,
        user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        _require_permission(user, "evaluation_components:write")
        try:
            row = create_component_asset(
                db,
                category_key=payload.category_key,
                asset_key=payload.asset_key,
                kind=payload.kind,
                display_name=payload.display_name,
                module_type_key=payload.module_type_key,
                prompt_stage=payload.prompt_stage,
                description=payload.description,
                created_by=user.username,
            )
            db.commit()
            db.refresh(row)
            return _asset_payload(row)
        except EvaluationComponentError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _http_error(exc) from exc

    @router.get("/{asset_id}")
    def get_asset(
        asset_id: int,
        _user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            return _asset_payload(require_component_asset(db, asset_id))
        except EvaluationComponentError as exc:
            raise _http_error(exc) from exc

    @router.patch("/{asset_id}")
    def update_asset(
        asset_id: int,
        payload: ComponentUpdate,
        user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        _require_permission(user, "evaluation_components:write")
        try:
            row = update_component_asset(
                db,
                asset_id=asset_id,
                display_name=payload.display_name,
                description=payload.description,
                is_discoverable=payload.is_discoverable,
            )
            db.commit()
            db.refresh(row)
            return _asset_payload(row)
        except EvaluationComponentError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _http_error(exc) from exc

    @router.post("/{asset_id}/copy", status_code=201)
    def copy_asset(
        asset_id: int,
        payload: ComponentCopy,
        user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        _require_permission(user, "evaluation_components:write")
        try:
            row = copy_component_asset(
                db,
                source_asset_id=asset_id,
                target_category_key=payload.target_category_key,
                target_asset_key=payload.target_asset_key,
                display_name=payload.display_name,
                created_by=user.username,
            )
            db.commit()
            db.refresh(row)
            return _asset_payload(row)
        except EvaluationComponentError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _http_error(exc) from exc

    @router.get("/{asset_id}/working-copy")
    def get_working_copy(
        asset_id: int,
        _user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            return _working_copy_payload(
                get_component_working_copy(db, asset_id)
            )
        except EvaluationComponentError as exc:
            raise _http_error(exc) from exc

    @router.put("/{asset_id}/working-copy")
    def save_working_copy(
        asset_id: int,
        payload: WorkingCopySave,
        user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        _require_permission(user, "evaluation_components:write")
        try:
            row = save_component_working_copy(
                db,
                asset_id=asset_id,
                expected_lock_version=payload.expected_lock_version,
                content=payload.content,
                updated_by=user.username,
            )
            db.commit()
            db.refresh(row)
            return _working_copy_payload(row)
        except EvaluationComponentError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _http_error(exc) from exc

    @router.post("/{asset_id}/versions", status_code=201)
    def generate_version(
        asset_id: int,
        user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        _require_permission(user, "evaluation_components:version")
        try:
            row = generate_component_version(
                db, asset_id=asset_id, created_by=user.username
            )
            db.commit()
            return asdict(row)
        except EvaluationComponentError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _http_error(exc) from exc

    @router.get("/{asset_id}/versions")
    def list_versions(
        asset_id: int,
        _user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            return {
                "items": _version_items(
                    db, require_component_asset(db, asset_id)
                )
            }
        except EvaluationComponentError as exc:
            raise _http_error(exc) from exc

    @router.get("/{asset_id}/versions/{version_id}/references")
    def version_references(
        asset_id: int,
        version_id: int,
        _user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, bool]:
        try:
            asset = require_component_asset(db, asset_id)
            if asset.kind == "prompt":
                binding = db.scalar(
                    select(WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.id).where(
                        WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.asset_id == asset.id,
                        WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.prompt_version_id == version_id,
                    )
                )
                if binding is None:
                    raise EvaluationComponentError(
                        "component_version_unknown",
                        "组件版本不存在",
                        status_code=404,
                    )
                return component_reference_facts(
                    db, prompt_version_id=version_id
                )
            row = db.scalar(
                select(RuleModuleVersion.id).where(
                    RuleModuleVersion.id == version_id,
                    RuleModuleVersion.asset_id == asset.id,
                )
            )
            if row is None:
                raise EvaluationComponentError(
                    "component_version_unknown",
                    "组件版本不存在",
                    status_code=404,
                )
            return component_reference_facts(
                db, rule_module_version_id=version_id
            )
        except EvaluationComponentError as exc:
            raise _http_error(exc) from exc

    return router

===== FILE backend/app/evaluation_credentials.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone

from sqlalchemy import func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .models import (
    EvaluationJob,
    ModelConfig,
    ModelNodeBinding,
    ModelQuotaLease,
    ModelQuotaPool,
)
from .security import secret_reference_platform_compatible


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ModelConcurrencyResource:
    """One real provider-capacity scope used by an incremental job.

    Legacy models deliberately share one compatibility resource so multiple
    unbound credentials cannot accidentally multiply the old global limit.
    """

    key: str
    limit: int
    quota_pool_id: int | None
    dispatchable: bool


def model_has_credentials(model: ModelConfig | None) -> bool:
    return bool(
        model is not None
        and model.encrypted_api_key
        and model.encrypted_api_key.strip()
        and secret_reference_platform_compatible(model.encrypted_api_key)
    )


def default_evaluation_model(db: Session) -> ModelConfig | None:
    binding = db.scalar(
        select(ModelNodeBinding).where(
            ModelNodeBinding.node_key == "evaluation_main",
            ModelNodeBinding.category_key.is_(None),
            ModelNodeBinding.enabled.is_(True),
        )
    )
    if (
        binding is not None
        and binding.model.active
        and model_has_credentials(binding.model)
    ):
        return binding.model
    for model in db.scalars(
        select(ModelConfig)
        .where(ModelConfig.active.is_(True))
        .order_by(ModelConfig.id.asc())
    ):
        if model_has_credentials(model):
            return model
    return None


def frozen_job_model_ids(job: EvaluationJob) -> tuple[int, ...] | None:
    if not job.category_profile_snapshot_json:
        return None
    try:
        snapshot = json.loads(job.category_profile_snapshot_json)
    except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError):
        return ()
    if not isinstance(snapshot, dict):
        return ()
    main_id = snapshot.get("model_config_id")
    if not isinstance(main_id, int) or isinstance(main_id, bool) or main_id < 1:
        return ()
    model_ids = [main_id]
    summary_id = snapshot.get("pdf_summary_model_config_id")
    if summary_id is not None:
        if (
            not isinstance(summary_id, int)
            or isinstance(summary_id, bool)
            or summary_id < 1
        ):
            return ()
        model_ids.append(summary_id)
    return tuple(dict.fromkeys(model_ids))


def job_primary_model(db: Session, job: EvaluationJob) -> ModelConfig | None:
    model_ids = frozen_job_model_ids(job)
    if not model_ids:
        return None
    return db.get(ModelConfig, model_ids[0])


def job_models(
    db: Session,
    job: EvaluationJob,
    *,
    fallback_model: ModelConfig | None,
) -> tuple[ModelConfig, ...]:
    model_ids = frozen_job_model_ids(job)
    if model_ids is None:
        return (fallback_model,) if fallback_model is not None else ()
    if not model_ids:
        return ()
    models = tuple(db.get(ModelConfig, model_id) for model_id in model_ids)
    if any(model is None for model in models):
        return ()
    return tuple(model for model in models if model is not None)


def _as_utc(value: datetime) -> datetime:
    if value.tzinfo is None:
        return value.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    return value.astimezone(timezone.utc)


def job_concurrency_resources(
    db: Session,
    job: EvaluationJob,
    *,
    fallback_model: ModelConfig | None,
    now: datetime | None = None,
    active_quota_leases: Mapping[int, int] | None = None,
) -> tuple[ModelConcurrencyResource, ...]:
    """Resolve distinct quota scopes without exposing credential material."""

    current = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    if current.tzinfo is None:
        current = current.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    resources: dict[str, ModelConcurrencyResource] = {}
    for model in job_models(db, job, fallback_model=fallback_model):
        if not model_has_credentials(model):
            return ()
        if model.quota_pool_id is None:
            legacy_limit = max(
                1,
                int(
                    getattr(fallback_model, "max_concurrency", 0)
                    or model.max_concurrency
                    or 1
                ),
            )
            resources["legacy-global"] = ModelConcurrencyResource(
                key="legacy-global",
                limit=legacy_limit,
                quota_pool_id=None,
                dispatchable=True,
            )
            continue
        pool = db.get(ModelQuotaPool, model.quota_pool_id)
        if pool is None:
            return ()
        cooling_down = (
            pool.cooldown_until is not None
            and _as_utc(pool.cooldown_until) > _as_utc(current)
        )
        active_leases = (
            active_quota_leases.get(pool.id, 0)
            if active_quota_leases is not None
            else db.scalar(
                select(func.count(ModelQuotaLease.id)).where(
                    ModelQuotaLease.pool_id == pool.id,
                    ModelQuotaLease.status == "active",
                    ModelQuotaLease.expires_at > current,
                )
            )
            or 0
        )
        resources[f"quota-pool:{pool.id}"] = ModelConcurrencyResource(
            key=f"quota-pool:{pool.id}",
            limit=max(1, pool.max_concurrency),
            quota_pool_id=pool.id,
            dispatchable=bool(
                pool.enabled
                and not cooling_down
                and active_leases < pool.max_concurrency
            ),
        )
    return tuple(resources[key] for key in sorted(resources))


def incremental_global_concurrency_limit(
    db: Session,
    *,
    fallback_model: ModelConfig,
    now: datetime | None = None,
) -> int:
    """Sum genuinely independent provider pools once each.

    Unbound models retain one legacy global bucket. This is intentionally not
    a sum of API keys or model rows.
    """

    current = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    if current.tzinfo is None:
        current = current.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    limits: dict[str, int] = {}
    has_legacy = False
    models = list(
        db.scalars(
            select(ModelConfig)
            .where(ModelConfig.active.is_(True))
            .order_by(ModelConfig.id.asc())
        )
    )
    if all(model.id != fallback_model.id for model in models):
        models.append(fallback_model)
    for model in models:
        if not model_has_credentials(model):
            continue
        if model.quota_pool_id is None:
            has_legacy = True
            continue
        pool = db.get(ModelQuotaPool, model.quota_pool_id)
        if pool is None or not pool.enabled:
            continue
        if (
            pool.cooldown_until is not None
            and _as_utc(pool.cooldown_until) > _as_utc(current)
        ):
            continue
        limits[f"quota-pool:{pool.id}"] = max(1, pool.max_concurrency)
    if has_legacy:
        limits["legacy-global"] = max(1, fallback_model.max_concurrency)
    return max(1, sum(limits.values()))


def job_has_required_credentials(
    db: Session,
    job: EvaluationJob,
    *,
    fallback_model: ModelConfig | None,
) -> bool:
    model_ids = frozen_job_model_ids(job)
    if model_ids is None:
        return model_has_credentials(fallback_model)
    if not model_ids:
        return False
    return all(model_has_credentials(db.get(ModelConfig, model_id)) for model_id in model_ids)

===== FILE backend/app/evaluation_mechanism_migration.py =====
"""Deterministic, auditable slicing of legacy evaluation revisions.

This module creates component assets and immutable rule-module histories. A
new, untouched operator working copy is initialized from the latest imported
revision so editing starts from the real current contract. It deliberately
does not create workflow versions or change incremental bindings; those
higher-risk operations belong to the workflow-v3 migration.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from copy import deepcopy
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
import json
from typing import Any, Iterable, Mapping

from sqlalchemy import func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .category_evaluation_v3_revisions import revision_bundle
from .dimension_schema_registry import canonical_json
from .evaluation_component_catalog import (
    MODULE_TYPE_KEYS,
    canonical_component_hash,
    module_type_catalog,
    validate_module_content,
)
from .evaluation_components import create_component_asset
from .models import (
    CategoryEvaluationV3Revision,
    EvaluationComponentWorkingCopy,
    LegacyEvaluationRevisionMapping,
    RuleModuleVersion,
    WorkflowDefinition,
    WorkflowNodeAsset,
    WorkflowVersion,
)
from .workflow_assets import bind_rule_module_version
from .level_scale import CANONICAL_LEVELS, resolve_level_scale
