            from .factory_submit_delivery import resolve_locator_endpoint
            from .connector_delivery import ConnectorDeliveryError
            allowed=None
            if category_key=='inspiration_image':
                from .factory_test_claim import INSPIRATION_UPSTREAMS
                allowed=INSPIRATION_UPSTREAMS
            try:endpoint=resolve_locator_endpoint(down.logical_locator,allowed=allowed)
            except ConnectorDeliveryError:return closed
            if expected_mode!='real' or (expected_upstream is not None and endpoint!=expected_upstream):return closed
            if category_key=='inspiration_image' and not inspiration_endpoint_enabled(endpoint):return closed
            sink=None
        else:sink=make_url(resolve_locator_to_dsn(down.logical_locator))
        if sink is None:pass
        elif sink.drivername=='sqlite':
            if (expected_mode!='simulated' or not sink.database or
                    Path(sink.database).resolve().parent!=Path(journal).resolve().parent or
                    Path(sink.database).resolve()==Path(journal).resolve()):return closed
        elif (sink.drivername not in ('mysql+pymysql','mysql') or
                sink.host not in ('127.0.0.1','localhost','host.docker.internal') or
                not sink.database or not sink.database.startswith('factory_test_gray_') or
                sink.password or sink.query):return closed
        snapshot=resolve_incremental_mechanism_for_enqueue(db,category_key).workflow_snapshot
        nodes=snapshot['workflow']['manifest']['nodes']
        if category_key=='inspiration_image':
            prompts=[n for n in nodes if n.get('kind')=='prompt']
            if not prompts or any(n.get('config',{}).get('model_snapshot',{}).get('provider')!='local_qwen' for n in prompts):
                return closed
            from .inspiration_metadata_generation import production_fields_ready
            if not production_fields_ready(snapshot['workflow']['manifest']):
                return closed
        for kind,contract in [('upstream_connector',up),('downstream_projection',down)]:
            matched=[n for n in nodes if n.get('kind')==kind]
            if len(matched)!=1 or matched[0].get('config',{}).get('connector_snapshot',{}).get('connector_contract_id')!=contract.id:
                return closed
            if kind=='downstream_projection' and matched[0].get('config',{}).get('mapping',{}).get('schema_version')!=projection_schema:
                return closed
        if expected_mode=='real':
            evidence=resolve_configured_downstream_adapter(down).verify_target()
            if not evidence.least_privileged or evidence.schema_fingerprint!=down.schema_fingerprint:return closed
        scope=dict(category_key=category_key,workflow_definition_id=version.workflow_definition_id)
        routing=current_policy(db,**scope)
        if routing is None or routing.admission_paused or routing.mode!='split' or routing.ratio_percent!=100:return closed
        if connector_is_paused(db,category_key=category_key,
                mechanism_key='workflow-version:'+str(workflow_version_id),contract_key=up.contract_key):return closed
        control=db.scalar(select(IncrementalAutomationControl).where(
            IncrementalAutomationControl.category_key==category_key,
            IncrementalAutomationControl.workflow_definition_id==version.workflow_definition_id))
        return dict(poll=True,confirm=confirm_enabled is True,material=control is None or control.enabled)


def material_cycle(path, *, sessions, controls, max_tasks=1, send=None):
    """Bounded lifetime intake. Never converts a task to a placeholder.

    Every externally visible effect rechecks platform gates. The total journal
    count, including quarantined/foreign tasks, consumes the intake allowance.
    Reopening a process never resets this allowance.
    """
    from .factory_pump import pump_once
    if type(max_tasks) is not int or not 1 <= max_tasks <= 10:
        raise ClaimGateError('factory_intake_limit_invalid')
    path = Path(path).absolute()
    if path.is_symlink():raise ClaimGateError('factory_private_journal_required')
    def gates():
        flags=controls()
        count=0
        if path.exists():
            with closing(sqlite3.connect(f'file:{path}?mode=ro',uri=True)) as db:
                count=db.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM tasks').fetchone()[0]
        if count>max_tasks:raise ClaimGateError('factory_intake_existing_tasks_exceed_limit')
        return dict(poll=isinstance(flags,dict) and flags.get('poll') is True and count<max_tasks,
                    confirm=isinstance(flags,dict) and flags.get('confirm') is True,
                    stage=False)
    # pump metadata value is a journal sentinel, never used to stage a changes row.
    if path.exists():
        with closing(sqlite3.connect(f'file:{path}?mode=ro',uri=True)) as db:
            sentinel=db.execute('SELECT asset_id FROM pump_meta').fetchone()[0]
            if db.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM changes').fetchone()[0]:
                raise ClaimGateError('factory_intake_placeholder_journal_refused')
    else:sentinel=1
    result=pump_once(path,asset_id=sentinel,controls=gates,max_pending=max_tasks,
                     **({'send':send} if send is not None else {}))
    flags=controls()
    intake=enqueue_confirmed_materials(path,sessions=sessions,
        enabled=isinstance(flags,dict) and flags.get('material') is True,limit=max_tasks)
    return {**result,'material_enqueued':intake['enqueued']}


def enqueue_confirmed_materials(path, *, sessions, enabled=False, limit=1, upstream=TEST_URL):
    if enabled is not True:
        return {'enqueued': 0, 'disabled': True}
    # Refuse an unknown target before any journal work happens.
    prefixes = upstream_prefixes(upstream)
    version_prefix = prefixes.version
    if type(limit) is not int or not 1 <= limit <= 10:
        raise ClaimGateError('factory_material_limit_invalid')
    path = Path(path).absolute()
    if (path.parent.is_symlink() or not path.parent.is_dir() or
            path.is_symlink() or not path.is_file()):
        raise ClaimGateError('factory_private_journal_required')
    lock = _private_fd(str(path)+'.lock')
    try:
        try:
            fcntl.flock(lock, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
        except BlockingIOError:
            return {'enqueued': 0, 'busy': True}
        os.close(_private_fd(path))
        with closing(sqlite3.connect(path, timeout=5)) as journal:
            tables = {r[0] for r in journal.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'")}
            if tables != {'pump_meta', 'tasks', 'confirmations', 'changes'}:
                raise ClaimGateError('factory_pump_journal_not_dedicated')
            journal.execute('PRAGMA synchronous=FULL')
            rows = journal.execute("SELECT task_id,payload_json,first_seen FROM tasks WHERE state='confirmed' ORDER BY task_id LIMIT ?", (limit,)).fetchall()
            enqueued = 0
            for ident, raw, first_seen in rows:
                owned = False
                for ids_raw, response in journal.execute("SELECT task_ids,response FROM confirmations WHERE state='received'"):
                    ids = json.loads(ids_raw)
                    if ident in ids:
                        partition = parse_confirm_response(_decode(response), requested_task_ids=ids)
                        owned = ident in partition['confirmed_task_ids']
                        if owned:
                            break
                if not owned:
                    raise ClaimGateError('factory_ownership_receipt_required')
                task = json.loads(raw)
                if task.get('task_id') != ident or type(ident) is not int or ident <= 0:
                    raise ClaimGateError('factory_task_identity_conflict')
                kind, identity = resource_identity(task['res_type'], task['ll_id'], task['img_id'], url=upstream)
                if type(task.get('class_id_1')) is not int or task['class_id_1'] <= 0:
                    raise ClaimGateError('factory_category_identity_invalid')
                observed = datetime.fromisoformat(first_seen)
                if observed.tzinfo is None:
                    raise ClaimGateError('factory_observation_timezone_required')
                # Task-scoped test content avoids pretending receipt IDs express
                # ordering of revisions of a resource. Keep resource identity too.
                payload = dict(content_id=identity+':task:'+str(ident),
                    content_version=version_prefix+str(ident), category_key='model_3d_su',
                    material={'url': task['image_url']}, material_type=kind,
                    source_time_kind='platform_first_observed',
                    factory_task={key: task[key] for key in ('task_id','res_type','ll_id','img_id','class_id_1')})
                with sessions() as db:
                    submit_manifest(db, source_system='factory-test-material',
                        submission_id=prefixes.submission+str(ident), occurred_at=observed,
                        payload=payload, actor='factory-test-material-intake')
                    db.commit()
                # The cross-database commit window is replay-safe through the
                # material service's immutable identity + manifest hash.
                journal.execute("UPDATE tasks SET state='material_enqueued' WHERE task_id=? AND state='confirmed'", (ident,))
                journal.commit()
                enqueued += 1
            return {'enqueued': enqueued, 'disabled': False}
    finally:
        os.close(lock)

===== FILE backend/app/factory_material_stream.py =====
"""TEST-only continuous material intake, with conservative Beijing-day allowance.

The dedicated allowance reserves the whole frozen worst-case material cost before
confirmation. Today's reservations are never refunded. Outstanding older work
carries its reservation across midnight. Existing runtime budgets remain intact.
No production upstream, placeholder staging or real downstream submit exists here.
"""
from contextlib import closing
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from dataclasses import asdict
import fcntl
import io
import json
import logging
import os
from pathlib import Path
import sqlite3
import time
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo
from PIL import Image
from sqlalchemy import func, select
from .factory_pump import _private_fd, pump_once
from .factory_claim_contract import claim_form, parse_claim_response
from .factory_test_claim import TEST_URL, send_upstream_request, ClaimGateError, upstream_prefixes
from .factory_material_intake import enqueue_confirmed_materials
from .incremental_material_models import IncrementalMaterialJob
from .models import ProductionRun, WorkflowModelInvocation
from . import incremental_materials

logger=logging.getLogger(__name__)


# The ceiling a day's reservations may not exceed. It was 200_000_000 while a
# material reserved the frozen worst case; once reservations price the expected
# prompt the same money buys an order of magnitude more, and the operator asked
# for the allowance to stop being what limits a throughput measurement. This is
# still a real cash ceiling, not an absence of one.
#
# 2026-09-16：随日门槛一起上调一个数量级。旧值 1000 元/天是按更早的口径定的，
# 而目标吞吐是每天 10 万素材（约 20 万次调用、按实测单价约 6800 元），旧值在
# 正常一天的 15% 处就把配置卡死。这里仍然是个真实的现金上限，只是不再比业务
# 目标还小。停止进料那一下已经另行关闭（见 stream_cycle 里的开关）。
MAX_DAILY_ALLOWANCE_MICROS = 10_000_000_000


class DailyAllowance:
    def __init__(self, path, limit):
        if type(limit) is not int or not 0 < limit <= MAX_DAILY_ALLOWANCE_MICROS:
            raise ValueError('factory_daily_allowance_invalid')
        self.path, self.limit = Path(path), limit
        os.close(_private_fd(self.path))
        with closing(sqlite3.connect(self.path)) as db:
            db.execute('PRAGMA synchronous=FULL')
            db.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
            db.execute('CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS config (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK(id=1), limit_micros INTEGER NOT NULL)')
            db.execute('CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS reservations (task_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, business_day TEXT NOT NULL, cost INTEGER NOT NULL, finished INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, finished_day TEXT)')
            row=db.execute('SELECT limit_micros FROM config WHERE id=1').fetchone()
            # Raising the ceiling is safe: every past reservation passed a
            # tighter check than the new one. Lowering it is not - it would make
            # days that were within budget look retroactively over it - so the
            # ledger still refuses that and the operator must start a new one.
            if row and row[0] > limit:raise ValueError('factory_allowance_configuration_changed')
            if row and row[0] < limit:db.execute('UPDATE config SET limit_micros=? WHERE id=1',(limit,))
            if not row:db.execute('INSERT INTO config VALUES (1,?)',(limit,))
            db.commit()

    @staticmethod
    def day(now):
        if now.tzinfo is None:raise ValueError('aware_time_required')
        return now.astimezone(ZoneInfo('Asia/Shanghai')).date().isoformat()

    def usage(self, now):
        with closing(sqlite3.connect(self.path)) as db:
            return db.execute('SELECT COALESCE(SUM(cost),0) FROM reservations WHERE business_day>=? OR finished_day>=? OR finished=0',(self.day(now),self.day(now))).fetchone()[0]

    def reserve(self, ident, cost, now):
        if type(ident) is not int or ident<=0 or type(cost) is not int or cost<=0:
            raise ValueError('factory_reservation_invalid')
        with closing(sqlite3.connect(self.path)) as db:
            db.execute('PRAGMA synchronous=FULL');db.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
            old=db.execute('SELECT cost FROM reservations WHERE task_id=?',(ident,)).fetchone()
            if old:
                if old[0]!=cost:raise ValueError('factory_reserved_cost_changed')
                return True
            used=db.execute('SELECT COALESCE(SUM(cost),0) FROM reservations WHERE business_day>=? OR finished_day>=? OR finished=0',(self.day(now),self.day(now))).fetchone()[0]
            if used+cost>self.limit:return False
            db.execute('INSERT INTO reservations VALUES (?,?,?,0,NULL)',(ident,self.day(now),cost));db.commit()
            return True

    def finish(self, ident, *, now=None, settled_cost=None):
        # Settle down to what the provider actually charged, never up: the
        # reserved amount already passed the daily limit check and a larger one
        # never did. An unknown, zero or larger actual keeps the reservation as
        # recorded rather than pretending the work was cheaper than it was.
        with closing(sqlite3.connect(self.path)) as db:
            db.execute('PRAGMA synchronous=FULL');db.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
            row=db.execute('SELECT cost FROM reservations WHERE task_id=? AND finished=0',(ident,)).fetchone()
            if row is None:db.commit();return
            cost=settled_cost if type(settled_cost) is int and 0<settled_cost<row[0] else row[0]
            db.execute('UPDATE reservations SET cost=?,finished=1,finished_day=? WHERE task_id=? AND finished=0',
                (cost,self.day(now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)),ident));db.commit()

    def outstanding(self):
        with closing(sqlite3.connect(self.path)) as db:
            return [r[0] for r in db.execute('SELECT task_id FROM reservations WHERE finished=0')]

    def recorded(self):
        with closing(sqlite3.connect(self.path)) as db:
            return {r[0] for r in db.execute('SELECT task_id FROM reservations')}


def _released_by_upstream(journal, idents):
    # Tasks the upstream said belong to someone else ('elsewhere') or refused
    # ('rejected') never become a material job, so nothing else would ever
    # finish their reservation. 'unknown' is deliberately absent: the claim may
    # still be ours and stays reserved until reconciled.
    if journal is None or not idents or not Path(journal).exists():return set()
    with closing(sqlite3.connect(journal)) as db:
        marks=','.join('?'*len(idents))
        return {r[0] for r in db.execute(
            f"SELECT task_id FROM tasks WHERE state IN ('elsewhere','rejected') AND task_id IN ({marks})",list(idents))}


def _settle_allowance(allowance, sessions, now, *, submission_prefix='task:', source_system='factory-test-material', journal=None):
    # Only terminal platform work releases carry-over (not today's debit).
    outstanding=allowance.outstanding()
    released=_released_by_upstream(journal,outstanding)
    with sessions() as db:
        for ident in outstanding:
            job=db.scalar(select(IncrementalMaterialJob).where(IncrementalMaterialJob.source_system==source_system,IncrementalMaterialJob.submission_id==submission_prefix+str(ident)))
            if job is None:
                # Claimed elsewhere / rejected: no work, no charge, release now
                # (same as inspiration intake). Unknown confirmation stays reserved.
                if ident in released:allowance.finish(ident,now=max(now,datetime.now(timezone.utc)))
                continue
            if job.status=='failed' and job.event_id is None:
                allowance.finish(ident,now=max(now,datetime.now(timezone.utc)));continue
            if job.status!='completed' or job.event_id is None:continue
            runs=list(db.scalars(select(ProductionRun).where(ProductionRun.source_type=='content_ingress_event',ProductionRun.source_id==job.event_id)))
            if not runs or any(r.status not in ('succeeded','failed','canceled') for r in runs):continue
            if db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation.id).where(WorkflowModelInvocation.production_run_id.in_([r.id for r in runs]),WorkflowModelInvocation.status=='reserved').limit(1)):continue
            # Every call for this material is terminal, so the charged total is
            # known and the reservation no longer has to hold the worst case.
            charged=db.scalar(select(func.coalesce(func.sum(WorkflowModelInvocation.actual_cost_micros),0))
                .where(WorkflowModelInvocation.production_run_id.in_([r.id for r in runs])))
            allowance.finish(ident,now=max(now,datetime.now(timezone.utc)),settled_cost=int(charged or 0))


MAX_PER_CYCLE = 30
#: 单次调用里持续循环的上限（秒）。定时器每 10 秒起一个容器、容器启动就要 3 秒，
#: 一次只跑 per_cycle 条，供给上限就被定时器节奏钉死（3 条/10 秒 ≈ 2.6 万/天）。
#: 循环留在一次调用内、每轮之间只歇 cycle_interval 秒，才能把进料抬到槽位能吃的量。
MAX_LOOP_SECONDS = 55


def run_stream_loop(cycle, *, loop_seconds, interval_seconds, clock=time.monotonic, sleep=time.sleep):
    """在 ``loop_seconds`` 内反复调用 ``cycle()``（每次都是完整的 stream_cycle，含平台开关、
    额度、pending 上限检查），轮间歇 ``interval_seconds`` 秒。上游没料、到上限、stop/error
    都立刻收手，不空转打上游。返回按键累加后的汇总，附 ``loops`` 与最后一轮原样字段。"""
    if type(loop_seconds) is not int or not 0<=loop_seconds<=MAX_LOOP_SECONDS:
        raise ValueError('factory_stream_loop_invalid')
    if type(interval_seconds) is not int or not 1<=interval_seconds<=10:
        raise ValueError('factory_stream_loop_invalid')
    totals={'listed':0,'confirmed':0,'material_enqueued':0,'image_rejected':0,'claim_invalid':0,'cycles':0,'loops':0}
    deadline=clock()+loop_seconds
    last={}
    while True:
        last=cycle();totals['loops']+=1
        for key in ('listed','confirmed','material_enqueued','image_rejected','claim_invalid','cycles'):
            if type(last.get(key)) is int:totals[key]+=last[key]
        if last.get('stop') or last.get('error') or not last.get('material_enqueued'):break
        if clock()+interval_seconds>deadline:break
        sleep(interval_seconds)
    return {**last,**totals}


def stream_cycle(journal, *, sessions, controls, daily_limit_micros, per_task_micros,
                 max_pending=10, excluded_ids=(), send=send_upstream_request, now=None,
                 upstream=TEST_URL, estimate=None, per_cycle=1):
    # Refuse an unknown target before touching the journal or the network.
    prefixes=upstream_prefixes(upstream)
    journal=Path(journal)
    if not journal.is_absolute() or journal.is_symlink() or journal.parent.is_symlink() or journal.parent.stat().st_mode & 0o077:
        raise ClaimGateError('factory_private_directory_required')
    # In-flight materials, not a spend limit: the daily allowance still bounds
    # what a day can cost and the platform's own admission still bounds how many
    # calls actually run. Ten was tighter than the 96 execution slots, so the
    # intake could never fill the machine; the ceiling now matches the pump's own.
    if type(max_pending) is not int or not 1<=max_pending<=256:
        raise ValueError('factory_stream_pending_invalid')
    # 一轮里连续取几条。原先固定一条：定时器 10 秒一轮，供给上限就是 6 条/分——
    # 2026-09-17 上午 490 轮入队 366 条，工作端 144 槽根本吃不饱。每条仍走同一套
    # 「取一条 listed → 预留 → 确认 → 入队」，账本与日志语义逐条不变；上游没新料、
    # 到达 pending 上限或任何 stop/error 都立刻收手。
    if type(per_cycle) is not int or not 1<=per_cycle<=MAX_PER_CYCLE:
        raise ValueError('factory_stream_per_cycle_invalid')
    if type(per_task_micros) is not int or not 0<per_task_micros<=daily_limit_micros:
        raise ValueError('factory_stream_cost_invalid')
    lock=_private_fd(str(journal)+'.stream.lock')
    try:
        try:fcntl.flock(lock,fcntl.LOCK_EX|fcntl.LOCK_NB)
        except BlockingIOError:return {'stop':'busy'}
        budget_path=Path(str(journal)+'.allowance.sqlite')
        if journal.exists() and not budget_path.exists():
            raise ClaimGateError('factory_stream_allowance_missing')
        allowance=DailyAllowance(budget_path,daily_limit_micros)
        pump_once(journal,asset_id=1,max_pending=max_pending,controls=lambda:{},upstream=upstream)  # Initialize/recover unknown, no HTTP.
        now=now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        _settle_allowance(allowance,sessions,now,submission_prefix=prefixes.submission,journal=journal)
        def enabled():
            flags=controls()
            return all(flags.get(k) is True for k in ('poll','confirm','material'))
        if not enabled():return {'stop':'platform_paused','material_enqueued':0}
        with closing(sqlite3.connect(journal)) as db:
            owned=[r[0] for r in db.execute("SELECT task_id FROM tasks WHERE state='confirmed'")]
        if owned:
            if not set(owned)<=allowance.recorded():raise ClaimGateError('factory_material_allowance_required')
            return {'material_enqueued':enqueue_confirmed_materials(journal,sessions=sessions,enabled=enabled(),limit=10,upstream=upstream)['enqueued']}
        def _one():
            # 2026-09-16 老大裁决：进料侧这道日额度也一起取消，理由和平台侧那道一样
            # （见 workflow_model_budget.INCREMENTAL_DAILY_BUDGET_ENFORCED）。只关掉
            # 「到点就停」这一下；预留和结算照常，allowance 账本一条不少，所以
            # recorded()/outstanding() 这些正确性检查完全不受影响。
            from .workflow_model_budget import INCREMENTAL_DAILY_BUDGET_ENFORCED
            if (INCREMENTAL_DAILY_BUDGET_ENFORCED
                    and allowance.usage(now)+per_task_micros>daily_limit_micros):
                return {'stop':'daily_allowance','reserved_micros':allowance.usage(now),'material_enqueued':0}
            if len(allowance.outstanding())>=max_pending:return {'stop':'pending_limit','material_enqueued':0}
            with closing(sqlite3.connect(journal)) as db:
                listed=db.execute("SELECT payload_json FROM tasks WHERE state='listed' ORDER BY task_id LIMIT 1").fetchone()
                known={r[0] for r in db.execute('SELECT task_id FROM tasks')}|set(excluded_ids)
            selected=json.loads(listed[0]) if listed else None
            rejected=0
            skipped_invalid=0
            if selected is None:
                if not enabled():return {'stop':'platform_paused','material_enqueued':0}
                raw=send(upstream,claim_form(service='ResInternalLLM',timestamp=int(time.time()),limit=10))
                from .factory_pump import _decode
                # One unusable row (unmapped res_type, bad URL) no longer fails
                # the page: the upstream returns the same page again, so that
                # stalled intake for good. Skipped rows are not recorded as
                # known, so a corrected row is picked up on a later cycle.
                invalid=[]
                tasks=parse_claim_response(_decode(raw),requested_limit=10,allowed_res_types={1,6},skipped=invalid)
                for bad in invalid:
                    logger.warning('factory claim row skipped: task_id=%s code=%s',bad['task_id'],bad['code'])
                skipped_invalid+=len(invalid)
                for task in tasks:
                    if task.task_id in known:continue
                    with sessions() as db:
                        if db.scalar(select(IncrementalMaterialJob.id).where(IncrementalMaterialJob.source_system=='factory-test-material',IncrementalMaterialJob.submission_id==prefixes.submission+str(task.task_id))):continue
                    body=asdict(task)
                    try:
                        data=incremental_materials._download(task.image_url,incremental_materials._policy('factory-test-material'))
                        with Image.open(io.BytesIO(data)) as image:image.verify()
                    except (ValueError,OSError):
                        with closing(sqlite3.connect(journal)) as db:
                            db.execute("INSERT INTO tasks VALUES (?,?,'image_rejected',?)",(task.task_id,json.dumps(body),now.isoformat()));db.commit()
                        rejected+=1;continue
                    selected=body
                    with closing(sqlite3.connect(journal)) as db:
                        db.execute("INSERT INTO tasks VALUES (?,?,'listed',?)",(task.task_id,json.dumps(body),now.isoformat()));db.commit()
                    break
            if selected is None:return {'listed':0,'image_rejected':rejected,'claim_invalid':skipped_invalid,'material_enqueued':0}
            if not enabled():return {'stop':'platform_paused','material_enqueued':0}
            # Reserve what this material is expected to cost, capped by the frozen
            # worst case. The platform's own per-run cost ceiling is untouched, so a
            # low estimate can never let a run outspend its budget; it only stops the
            # daily allowance being consumed at about a hundred times the real charge.
            # A raising estimate stops the cycle on purpose: claiming at the cap while
            # pricing is broken would burn the day silently.
            reserve=per_task_micros
            if estimate is not None:
                measured=estimate(selected)
                if type(measured) is int and 0<measured<per_task_micros:reserve=measured
            if not allowance.reserve(selected['task_id'],reserve,now):return {'stop':'daily_allowance','material_enqueued':0}
            result=pump_once(journal,asset_id=1,max_pending=max_pending,upstream=upstream,
                controls=lambda:dict(poll=False,confirm=enabled(),stage=False),send=send)
            if result.get('error'):return result
            result['material_enqueued']=enqueue_confirmed_materials(journal,sessions=sessions,enabled=enabled(),limit=1,upstream=upstream)['enqueued']
            result['image_rejected']=rejected
            result['claim_invalid']=skipped_invalid
            result['reserved_micros']=allowance.usage(now)
            return result
        if per_cycle==1:return _one()
        totals={'listed':0,'confirmed':0,'material_enqueued':0,'image_rejected':0,'claim_invalid':0,'cycles':0}
        last={}
        for _ in range(per_cycle):
            last=_one();totals['cycles']+=1
            for key in ('listed','confirmed','material_enqueued','image_rejected','claim_invalid'):
                if type(last.get(key)) is int:totals[key]+=last[key]
            if last.get('stop') or last.get('error') or not last.get('material_enqueued'):break
        return {**last,**totals}
    finally:os.close(lock)

===== FILE backend/app/factory_pump.py =====
"""Durable TEST intake pump. Never executes models or submits real results.

One private journal is shared by all batches/restarts of this consumer. Its
``changes`` table is the read-only source for the native simulated pipeline.
The process lock serializes consumers without holding a platform transaction
during HTTP. Confirmation ambiguity is quarantined, NEVER inferred as ownership.
"""
from contextlib import closing
from dataclasses import asdict
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import fcntl
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import sqlite3
import time

from .factory_claim_contract import claim_form, confirm_form, parse_claim_response, parse_confirm_response
from .factory_test_claim import (TEST_URL, MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES, ClaimGateError, resource_identity,
                                 send_upstream_request, upstream_prefixes)


def platform_controls(sessions, *, journal, source_contract_id, upstream_contract_id,
                      downstream_contract_id, workflow_version_id, confirm_enabled=False):
    """Read the existing platform switches in a fresh, closed transaction.

    Only an explicitly simulated binding and a local SQLite downstream in the
    same private gray directory are accepted. Registration/configuration is an
    admin action; this reader never creates or updates platform rows.
    """
    from sqlalchemy import select
    from sqlalchemy.engine import make_url
    from .models import (UpstreamSourceContract, ConnectorContractVersion,
        CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding, WorkflowVersion, IncrementalAutomationControl)
    from .connector_governance import connector_is_paused
    from .incremental_routing import current_policy
    from .external_datasources import resolve_locator_to_dsn, resolve_locator_table
    closed = dict(poll=False, confirm=False, stage=False)
    category = 'model_3d_su'
    with sessions() as db:
        source = db.get(UpstreamSourceContract, source_contract_id)
        up = db.get(ConnectorContractVersion, upstream_contract_id)
        down = db.get(ConnectorContractVersion, downstream_contract_id)
        version = db.get(WorkflowVersion, workflow_version_id)
        binding = db.scalar(select(CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding).where(
            CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding.category_key==category))
        if (not all((source,up,down,version,binding)) or
                any(row.category_key!=category or row.status!='active' for row in (source,up,down)) or
                up.direction!='upstream' or down.direction!='downstream' or not source.read_only or
                binding.workflow_version_id!=workflow_version_id or binding.model_execution_mode!='simulated'):
            return closed
        from .incremental_mechanisms import resolve_incremental_mechanism_for_enqueue
        snapshot = resolve_incremental_mechanism_for_enqueue(db, category).workflow_snapshot
        nodes = snapshot['workflow']['manifest']['nodes']
        for kind,contract in [('upstream_connector',up),('downstream_projection',down)]:
            ids = {node.get('config',{}).get('connector_snapshot',{}).get('connector_contract_id')
                   for node in nodes if node.get('kind')==kind}
            if ids != {contract.id}:return closed
        src = make_url(resolve_locator_to_dsn(source.connection_locator))
        sink = make_url(resolve_locator_to_dsn(down.logical_locator))
        target = Path(journal).resolve()
        if (src.drivername!='sqlite' or sink.drivername!='sqlite' or not src.database or not sink.database or
                Path(src.database).resolve()!=target or Path(sink.database).resolve()==target or
                Path(sink.database).resolve().parent!=target.parent or
                resolve_locator_table(source.connection_locator)!='changes'):
            return closed
        if connector_is_paused(db,category_key=category,
                mechanism_key='workflow-version:'+str(workflow_version_id),contract_key=up.contract_key):
            return closed
        routing = current_policy(db,category_key=category,workflow_definition_id=version.workflow_definition_id)
        if routing is not None and routing.admission_paused:return closed
        control = db.scalar(select(IncrementalAutomationControl).where(
            IncrementalAutomationControl.category_key==category,
            IncrementalAutomationControl.workflow_definition_id==version.workflow_definition_id))
        return dict(poll=True,confirm=confirm_enabled is True,stage=control is None or control.enabled)


def _private_fd(path):
    fd = os.open(path, os.O_CREAT | os.O_RDWR | os.O_NOFOLLOW, 0o600)
    if os.fstat(fd).st_nlink != 1:
        os.close(fd)
        raise ClaimGateError('factory_journal_hardlink_refused')
    os.fchmod(fd, 0o600)
    return fd


def _enabled(controls, key):
    flags = controls()  # Fresh state immediately before every external effect.
    return isinstance(flags, dict) and flags.get(key) is True


def _decode(raw):
    if not isinstance(raw, bytes) or len(raw) > MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES:
        raise ClaimGateError('factory_response_limit')
    return json.loads(raw)


def _init(db, asset_id):
    tables = {r[0] for r in db.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'")}
    expected = {'pump_meta', 'tasks', 'confirmations', 'changes'}
    if tables and tables != expected:
        raise ClaimGateError('factory_pump_journal_not_dedicated')
    db.execute('PRAGMA synchronous=FULL')
    db.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
    db.execute('CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS pump_meta (version INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, asset_id INTEGER NOT NULL)')
    db.execute('CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tasks (task_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, payload_json TEXT NOT NULL, state TEXT NOT NULL, first_seen TEXT NOT NULL)')
    db.execute('CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS confirmations (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, task_ids TEXT NOT NULL, response BLOB, state TEXT NOT NULL)')
    db.execute('CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS changes (seq INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, task_id INTEGER NOT NULL UNIQUE, content_id TEXT NOT NULL, source_version TEXT NOT NULL, category_key TEXT NOT NULL, event_type TEXT NOT NULL, asset_id INTEGER NOT NULL, resource_kind TEXT NOT NULL, occurred_at TEXT NOT NULL)')
    old = db.execute('SELECT version,asset_id FROM pump_meta').fetchall()
    if old and old != [(1, asset_id)]:
        raise ClaimGateError('factory_pump_configuration_changed')
    if not old:db.execute('INSERT INTO pump_meta VALUES (1,?)', (asset_id,))
    # Crash after intent (even before actual send) cannot safely retry confirm.
    db.execute("UPDATE tasks SET state='unknown' WHERE state='sending'")
    db.execute("UPDATE confirmations SET state='unknown' WHERE state='sending'")
    db.commit()


def pump_once(path, *, controls, asset_id, send=send_upstream_request, max_pending=256,
              upstream=TEST_URL):
    """One bounded cycle: <=1 list, <=1 confirm, <=100 local staged records.

    ``asset_id`` is an explicitly configured local placeholder for simulated
    inference. It is frozen for this journal; real image download is NOT claimed.
    ``controls`` must supply bool poll/confirm/stage; missing values fail closed.
    Return only counts/codes, never upstream payloads, IDs or signing material.
    """
    # Refuse an unknown target before any journal or network work happens.
    version_prefix = upstream_prefixes(upstream).version
    if type(asset_id) is not int or asset_id <= 0 or type(max_pending) is not int or not 1 <= max_pending <= 256:
        raise ClaimGateError('factory_pump_configuration_invalid')
    path = Path(path).absolute()
    if path.parent.is_symlink() or not path.parent.is_dir():
        raise ClaimGateError('factory_private_directory_required')
    lock_fd = _private_fd(str(path)+'.lock')
    try:
        try:fcntl.flock(lock_fd, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
        except BlockingIOError:return {'busy': True, 'staged': 0}
        os.close(_private_fd(path))
        with closing(sqlite3.connect(path, timeout=5)) as db:
            _init(db, asset_id)
            result = {'busy': False, 'listed': 0, 'confirmed': 0, 'staged': 0, 'error': None}
            pending = db.execute("SELECT count(*) FROM tasks WHERE state NOT IN ('staged','elsewhere','rejected')").fetchone()[0]
            if pending < max_pending and _enabled(controls, 'poll'):
                limit = min(10, max_pending-pending)
                try:
                    raw = send(upstream, claim_form(service='ResInternalLLM', timestamp=int(time.time()), limit=limit))
                    tasks = parse_claim_response(_decode(raw), requested_limit=limit, allowed_res_types={1,6})
                except Exception:
                    # list is explicitly read-only; next scheduled cycle may list.
                    result['error'] = 'factory_list_failed'
                    return result
                db.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
                now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
                for task in tasks:
                    payload = json.dumps(asdict(task), sort_keys=True, separators=(',', ':'))
                    old = db.execute('SELECT payload_json FROM tasks WHERE task_id=?', (task.task_id,)).fetchone()
                    if old and old[0] != payload:
                        db.rollback()
                        result['error'] = 'factory_task_identity_conflict'
                        return result
                    if not old:db.execute("INSERT INTO tasks VALUES (?,?,'listed',?)", (task.task_id, payload, now))
                db.commit()
                result['listed'] = len(tasks)
            if _enabled(controls, 'confirm'):
                ids = [r[0] for r in db.execute("SELECT task_id FROM tasks WHERE state='listed' ORDER BY task_id LIMIT 100")]
                if ids:
                    db.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
                    attempt = db.execute("INSERT INTO confirmations(task_ids,state) VALUES (?,'sending')", (json.dumps(ids),)).lastrowid
                    db.executemany("UPDATE tasks SET state='sending' WHERE task_id=?", [(i,) for i in ids])
                    db.commit()
                    try:
                        raw = send(upstream, confirm_form(timestamp=int(time.time()), task_ids=ids))
                        # Preserve complete raw response before parsing. A crash
                        # here remains unknown, available for offline reconciliation.
                        if not isinstance(raw, bytes) or len(raw)>MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES:raise ClaimGateError('factory_response_limit')
                        db.execute('UPDATE confirmations SET response=? WHERE id=?', (raw, attempt));db.commit()
                        partition = parse_confirm_response(_decode(raw), requested_task_ids=ids)
                        db.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
                        for key,state in [('confirmed_task_ids','confirmed'),('already_claimed_task_ids','elsewhere'),('rejected_task_ids','rejected')]:
                            db.executemany('UPDATE tasks SET state=? WHERE task_id=?', [(state,i) for i in partition[key]])
                        db.execute("UPDATE confirmations SET state='received' WHERE id=?", (attempt,));db.commit()
                        result['confirmed'] = len(partition['confirmed_task_ids'])
                    except Exception:
                        db.rollback()
                        db.executemany("UPDATE tasks SET state='unknown' WHERE task_id=?", [(i,) for i in ids])
                        db.execute("UPDATE confirmations SET state='unknown' WHERE id=?", (attempt,));db.commit()
                        result['error'] = 'factory_confirmation_unknown'
            if _enabled(controls, 'stage'):
                db.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
                for ident,payload,first_seen in db.execute("SELECT task_id,payload_json,first_seen FROM tasks WHERE state='confirmed' ORDER BY task_id LIMIT 100").fetchall():
                    task = json.loads(payload)
                    kind,identity = resource_identity(task['res_type'], task['ll_id'], task['img_id'], url=upstream)
                    db.execute('INSERT INTO changes(task_id,content_id,source_version,category_key,event_type,asset_id,resource_kind,occurred_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)',
                        (ident,identity,version_prefix+str(ident),'model_3d_su','content.created',asset_id,kind,first_seen))
                    db.execute("UPDATE tasks SET state='staged' WHERE task_id=?", (ident,))
                    result['staged'] += 1
                db.commit()
            result['quarantined'] = db.execute("SELECT count(*) FROM tasks WHERE state IN ('unknown','elsewhere')").fetchone()[0]
            return result
    finally:
        os.close(lock_fd)

===== FILE backend/app/factory_submit_contract.py =====
"""Offline wire boundary for the factory result submit API. No network here.

Documented at https://s.apifox.cn/ef478d84-b0c0-4e83-bf3a-481c19e27c2e/514550602e0
(read 2026-09-15). Same host and signature scheme as claim/confirmClaim:
``service=ResInternalLLM action=submit time key=md5(reversed(service+action+time))``
with a JSON ``body``. The server owns idempotency: a repeated submit does not
overwrite an accepted result.

Required body fields for ``result_status=success``: task_id, title(<=10),
seo_title(<=28), category/style/cons/design(<=50 each), tags(>=4 non-empty),
score(int 0-100). Optional: grade(1-11), reason, hwreason.

Two places where the platform's frozen output and the API disagree are settled
here as explicit, single-constant policies rather than buried in the adapter:

* ``cons`` is unbounded in the prompt contract and 89% of real outputs exceed
  50 characters. Bounded strings are truncated to the API limit and the
  truncation is reported in ``notes``; a rejected 89% would only dead-letter.
* Short-circuited runs carry a final level but no final score by design, and
  the platform refuses to invent one. The API requires a score. Policy
  ``SCORE_FALLBACK`` chooses between prompt A's own reference score for the
  same image (a real model output, labelled as such in ``notes``) and failing
  closed. Never a synthetic number.
* A second write for a task the server already accepted is only honoured
  downstream when a person made it, so the body has to say which kind of write
  it is. Policy ``OVERRIDE_SOURCE_POLICY`` gates the extra fields until their
  names are agreed with the downstream owner; ``OVERRIDE_FIELDS`` holds the
  names themselves so agreeing on them is a one-line change here.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
from typing import Any, Mapping

from .factory_claim_contract import FactoryContractError, _positive_int
from .model_3d_su_legacy_projection import legacy_3d_su_projection

SERVICE = 'ResInternalLLM'
ACTION = 'submit'

# Operator decision 2026-09-15. Only L1-L4 exist for 3D/SU images.
GRADE_BY_LEVEL: dict[str, int] = {'L1': 7, 'L2': 9, 'L3': 6, 'L4': 2}
GRADE_LABELS: dict[int, str] = {1: '空', 2: '极差', 3: '不相关', 4: '推荐', 5: '违规', 6: '中差',
                                7: '好', 8: '极好', 9: '中等', 10: '保留', 11: '屏蔽'}

LIMITS: dict[str, int] = {'title': 10, 'seo_title': 28, 'category': 50, 'style': 50,
                          'cons': 50, 'design': 50, 'error_message': 500,
                          'override_actor': 120, 'override_reason': 200}
MIN_TAGS = 4

# 'prompt_a_reference' | 'fail_closed'
SCORE_FALLBACK = 'prompt_a_reference'

# 'emit' | 'off'. Field names agreed with the downstream owner (荣栋) on
# 2026-09-18; 'off' keeps the wire byte-identical to the thirteen-field body if
# the downstream ever needs the fields withdrawn without a code change elsewhere.
OVERRIDE_SOURCE_POLICY = 'emit'
OVERRIDE_FIELDS: dict[str, str] = {'source': 'override_source', 'actor': 'override_actor',
                                   'reason': 'override_reason'}
HUMAN_CORRECTION = 'human_correction'
HUMAN_OVERRIDE_SCHEMA = 'incremental-level-override-v1'


def submit_form(*, timestamp: int, body: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, str]:
    """Form field values only; caller sends multipart, never JSON."""
    if not _positive_int(timestamp):
        raise FactoryContractError('factory_submit_auth_input_invalid')
    if not isinstance(body, Mapping) or not _positive_int(body.get('task_id')):
        raise FactoryContractError('factory_submit_body_invalid')
    time = str(timestamp)
    key = hashlib.md5((SERVICE + ACTION + time)[::-1].encode('ascii')).hexdigest()
    return {'service': SERVICE, 'action': ACTION, 'time': time, 'key': key,
            'body': json.dumps(dict(body), ensure_ascii=False, separators=(',', ':'))}


def _clip(value: Any, field: str, notes: list[str]) -> str:
    text = value.strip() if isinstance(value, str) else ''
    limit = LIMITS[field]
    if len(text) > limit:
        notes.append(f'{field}:truncated:{len(text)}>{limit}')
        return text[:limit]
    return text


def _first_text(field: str, *sources: Any) -> str:
    for source in sources:
        if isinstance(source, Mapping):
            value = source.get(field)
            if isinstance(value, str) and value.strip():
                return value.strip()
    return ''


def _override_fields(output: Mapping[str, Any], change: Any,
                     notes: list[str]) -> dict[str, str]:
    """Who overwrote a task the server already accepted: a person, or the run.

    Two independent records of the same decision exist by the time a submit is
    built. A level correction freezes ``human_override`` into the result and
    writes ``human_correction`` into the outbox change record; a replay after an
    upstream correction writes only the change record, because the level it
    republishes is the mechanism's own; an automatic run writes neither. So a
    person made this write when either record says so. The one shape that cannot
    be sent honestly is a frozen human override under an automatic change
    record, which means the two disagree about what happened: that fails closed
    into a dead letter rather than labelling a mechanism write as a person's.
    """
    if OVERRIDE_SOURCE_POLICY != 'emit':
        return {}
    frozen = output.get('human_override')
    if not isinstance(frozen, Mapping) or frozen.get('schema_version') != HUMAN_OVERRIDE_SCHEMA:
        frozen = None
    recorded = change.get('trigger_kind') if isinstance(change, Mapping) else None
    recorded = recorded.strip() if isinstance(recorded, str) else ''
    if frozen is not None and recorded and recorded != HUMAN_CORRECTION:
        raise FactoryContractError('factory_submit_override_source_conflict')
    if frozen is None and recorded != HUMAN_CORRECTION:
        return {}
    notes.append('override:' + HUMAN_CORRECTION)
    return {
        OVERRIDE_FIELDS['source']: HUMAN_CORRECTION,
        OVERRIDE_FIELDS['actor']: _clip(_first_text('actor', frozen, change),
                                        'override_actor', notes),
        OVERRIDE_FIELDS['reason']: _clip(_first_text('reason', frozen, change),
                                         'override_reason', notes),
    }


def build_submit_body(output: Mapping[str, Any], *,
                      change: Any = None) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], list[str]]:
    """factory-result-v1 node output -> API body. Returns (body, notes).

    ``change`` is the outbox event's change record when the caller has one; it
    is the only place a replay-after-correction records that a person asked for
    the rewrite, so a caller that drops it silently downgrades those writes to
    look automatic.

    Raises FactoryContractError for anything that cannot be sent honestly:
    missing task identity, an unmapped level, fewer than four tags, a missing
    score under the fail-closed policy, or two records that disagree about who
    made the write.
    """
    if not isinstance(output, Mapping) or output.get('schema_version') != 'factory-result-v1':
        raise FactoryContractError('factory_submit_output_schema_invalid')
    task = output.get('factory_task')
    if not isinstance(task, Mapping) or not _positive_int(task.get('task_id')):
        raise FactoryContractError('factory_submit_task_identity_missing')
    level = output.get('final_level')
    if level not in GRADE_BY_LEVEL:
        raise FactoryContractError('factory_submit_level_unmapped')
    res_type = task.get('res_type') if type(task.get('res_type')) is int else None
    stage_a = output.get('prompt_a_fields')
    if not isinstance(stage_a, Mapping):
        raise FactoryContractError('factory_submit_prompt_a_missing')

    notes: list[str] = []
    final_score = output.get('final_score')
    score: int | None = final_score if type(final_score) is int and 0 <= final_score <= 100 else None
    if score is None:
        production = stage_a.get('production_fields')
        reference = production.get('score') if isinstance(production, Mapping) else None
        if SCORE_FALLBACK == 'prompt_a_reference' and type(reference) is int and 0 <= reference <= 100:
            score = reference
            notes.append('score:prompt_a_reference')
        else:
            raise FactoryContractError('factory_submit_score_missing')

    legacy = legacy_3d_su_projection(stage_a, score=score, res_type=res_type)
    tags = [t.strip() for t in legacy['tags'] if isinstance(t, str) and t.strip()]
    if len(tags) < MIN_TAGS:
        raise FactoryContractError('factory_submit_tags_insufficient')
    notes.extend('legacy:' + n for n in legacy.get('notes', []))

    body = {
        'task_id': task['task_id'],
        'result_status': 'success',
        'title': _clip(legacy['title'], 'title', notes),
        'seo_title': _clip(legacy['seotitle'], 'seo_title', notes),
        'category': _clip(legacy['category'], 'category', notes),
        'style': _clip(legacy['style'], 'style', notes),
        'cons': _clip(legacy['cons'], 'cons', notes),
        'design': _clip(legacy['design'], 'design', notes),
        'tags': tags,
        'score': score,
        'grade': GRADE_BY_LEVEL[level],
        'reason': legacy['reason'],
        'hwreason': legacy['hwreason'],
    }
    body.update(_override_fields(output, change, notes))
    return body, notes


MAX_DIAGNOSTIC_CHARS = 200


def submit_diagnostic(response: object) -> str:
    """工厂 envelope 里的 ``msg``，清洗后用于审计诊断。

    上面那条「不回显响应体」的纪律照旧：这里只取 ``msg`` 这一个字段，不碰 ``data``
    也不碰整包；去掉控制字符（含换行，免得污染日志行），按字符截断。它只进审计详情，
    不进错误码，也不进给人看的报错文案——错误码要可枚举，而这是上游随便写的文本。
    """

    if not isinstance(response, dict):
        return ""
    message = response.get("msg")
    if not isinstance(message, str):
        return ""
    cleaned = "".join(ch for ch in message if ch.isprintable()).strip()
    if len(cleaned) > MAX_DIAGNOSTIC_CHARS:
        cleaned = cleaned[:MAX_DIAGNOSTIC_CHARS] + "…"
    return cleaned


def parse_submit_response(response: object, *, task_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Validate the submit envelope; never echo the raw body into errors."""
    if not _positive_int(task_id):
        raise FactoryContractError('factory_submit_body_invalid')
    # Published example uses 1; the test factory has returned 200 for the same envelope.
    if not isinstance(response, dict) or type(response.get('code')) is not int:
        raise FactoryContractError('factory_submit_not_successful')
    if response['code'] not in {1, 200}:
        # 2026-09-20: 第一条人工纠偏覆盖被工厂拒了，而我们只记下「未通过」，
        # 连它回的业务码都没留，查不下去。带上这个数字——它是工厂自己定义的
        # 枚举，不是响应体，回显它不违反上面那条「不回显 body」。
        # 错误码列是 String(80)，所以只在数值短到能放下时才拼。
        code = response['code']
        if -99999 <= code <= 999999:
            raise FactoryContractError('factory_submit_not_successful_code_%d' % code)
        raise FactoryContractError('factory_submit_not_successful')
    request_id = response.get('request_id')
    data = response.get('data')
    if not isinstance(request_id, str) or not request_id.strip() or not isinstance(data, dict):
        raise FactoryContractError('factory_submit_envelope_invalid')
    if data.get('task_id') != task_id:
        raise FactoryContractError('factory_submit_task_mismatch')
    accepted = data.get('accepted')
    if type(accepted) is not bool:
        raise FactoryContractError('factory_submit_envelope_invalid')
    status = data.get('status')
    return {'task_id': task_id, 'accepted': accepted, 'request_id': request_id.strip(),
            'status': status if type(status) is int else None}

===== FILE backend/app/factory_submit_delivery.py =====
"""Downstream adapter that submits factory-result-v1 payloads over HTTPS.

Fits the same DownstreamAdapter protocol as the SQL envelope adapter, so the
outbox tick, the delivery loop, the publish ledger, pauses and dead letters all
work unchanged. Three deliberate differences from the SQL adapter:

* The physical endpoint is deployment-side, like a DSN: the contract carries a
  logical locator and ``LABEL_SYSTEM_ENDPOINT_<LOCATOR>`` names the URL, which
  must be one of the two allowlisted factory hosts. Nothing else is accepted.
* The API has no read endpoint. ``read_back`` therefore returns what this
  adapter itself recorded from the server's acceptance in the same round trip,
  keyed by business key, so the delivery loop's read-back match still holds.
* Transport failures are retryable; a status the server chose is classified by
  that status (4xx no, 408/429/5xx yes), and a response that fails validation or
  a body that cannot be built honestly is never retried.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import os
import re
import time
from collections.abc import Callable, Mapping, Sequence
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from threading import BoundedSemaphore
from typing import Any
from urllib.error import HTTPError

from .connector_delivery import (
    ConnectorDeliveryError, DeliveredRow, DeliveryAttempt, DeliveryBatch, DeliveryKey,
    DeliveryWriteResult, DownstreamSafetyEvidence,
)
from .factory_claim_contract import FactoryContractError
from .factory_submit_contract import (build_submit_body, parse_submit_response,
                                      submit_diagnostic, submit_form)
from .factory_test_claim import UPSTREAMS, ClaimGateError, send_upstream_request
from .models import ConnectorContractVersion

FACTORY_SUBMIT_ADAPTER_KEY = 'factory-submit-v1'
FACTORY_SUBMIT_SCHEMA_FINGERPRINT = hashlib.sha256(b'factory-submit-result-v1').hexdigest()
MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES = 1024 * 1024
# Request timeout and rate limiting are the only 4xx worth repeating.
_RETRYABLE_STATUS = frozenset({408, 429})
_ENDPOINT_ENV_PREFIX = 'LABEL_SYSTEM_ENDPOINT_'
FACTORY_SUBMIT_CONCURRENCY = 20
# Shared across adapter instances in the single delivery process.
_submit_slots = BoundedSemaphore(FACTORY_SUBMIT_CONCURRENCY)


def resolve_locator_endpoint(locator: str, *, allowed: Mapping = None) -> str:
    """Logical locator -> allowlisted factory URL from the deployment environment."""
    allowed = UPSTREAMS if allowed is None else allowed
    reference = (locator or '').strip()
    if not reference or '://' in reference:
        raise ConnectorDeliveryError('downstream_adapter_unavailable', '下游端点必须是逻辑引用')
    env_key = _ENDPOINT_ENV_PREFIX + re.sub(r'[^A-Za-z0-9]+', '_', reference).upper()
    url = (os.getenv(env_key) or '').strip()
    if not url:
        raise ConnectorDeliveryError('downstream_adapter_unavailable',
                                     f'逻辑引用 {reference} 没有对应的端点配置；请设置环境变量 {env_key}')
    if url not in allowed:
        raise ConnectorDeliveryError('downstream_adapter_unavailable', '下游端点不在允许的工厂域名列表内')
    return url


class FactorySubmitDownstreamAdapter:
    def __init__(self, url: str, *, send: Callable[[str, dict[str, str]], bytes] = send_upstream_request,
                 clock: Callable[[], float] = time.time) -> None:
        if url not in UPSTREAMS:
            raise ConnectorDeliveryError('downstream_adapter_unavailable', '下游端点不在允许的工厂域名列表内')
        self._url = url
        self._send = send
        self._clock = clock
        self._rows: dict[str, DeliveredRow] = {}
        self.notes: dict[str, list[str]] = {}

    @property
    def url(self) -> str:
        return self._url

    def verify_target(self) -> DownstreamSafetyEvidence:
        return DownstreamSafetyEvidence(least_privileged=True,
                                        schema_fingerprint=FACTORY_SUBMIT_SCHEMA_FINGERPRINT,
                                        verifier='factory-submit-https-allowlisted:' + self._url)

    def _submit_one(self, key: DeliveryKey, payload: Any) -> DeliveryWriteResult:
        result = payload.get('result') if isinstance(payload, dict) else None
        output = result.get('output') if isinstance(result, dict) else None
        # The change record travels with the event and is the only place a
        # replay-after-correction says a person asked for the rewrite, so it has
        # to reach the body builder alongside the frozen result.
        change = payload.get('change') if isinstance(payload, dict) else None
        try:
            body, notes = build_submit_body(output if isinstance(output, dict) else {}, change=change)
        except FactoryContractError as exc:
            return DeliveryWriteResult(key=key, succeeded=False, error_code=str(exc), retryable=False)
        try:
            raw = self._send(self._url, submit_form(timestamp=int(self._clock()), body=body))
        except HTTPError as exc:
            # A status the server chose is not a transport problem. Retrying a
            # 4xx just burns the attempt budget and dead-letters the material,
            # which is what happened to task 258 on 2026-09-15: five attempts in
            # thirty-one seconds against a deterministic 404. The status goes in
            # the error code because _failure fixes the message, so the code is
            # the only per-item channel into the ledger and the audit trail.
            status = exc.code if isinstance(exc.code, int) else 0
            retryable = status in _RETRYABLE_STATUS or 500 <= status < 600
            self.notes.setdefault(key.business_idempotency_key, []).append('http:%d' % status)
            return DeliveryWriteResult(key=key, succeeded=False,
                                       error_code='factory_submit_http_%d' % status, retryable=retryable)
        except (ClaimGateError, OSError, ValueError) as exc:
            # Genuinely no answer: DNS, connect, TLS, timeout, or a refused
            # redirect. The server dedupes a repeat, so retrying is safe.
            self.notes.setdefault(key.business_idempotency_key, []).append('transport:' + type(exc).__name__)
            return DeliveryWriteResult(key=key, succeeded=False,
                                       error_code='factory_submit_transport_failed', retryable=True)
        envelope: object = None
        try:
            if not isinstance(raw, bytes) or len(raw) > MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES:
                raise FactoryContractError('factory_submit_response_limit')
            envelope = json.loads(raw)
            parsed = parse_submit_response(envelope, task_id=body['task_id'])
        except (FactoryContractError, ValueError) as exc:
            code = str(exc) if isinstance(exc, FactoryContractError) else 'factory_submit_response_invalid'
            return DeliveryWriteResult(key=key, succeeded=False, error_code=code, retryable=False,
                                       diagnostic=submit_diagnostic(envelope))
        # accepted=false with code 1 is the server's own dedupe: the result is
        # already held server-side, which is delivered for our purposes.
        external = (f"factory-submit:task:{parsed['task_id']}:req:{parsed['request_id']}"
                    f":accepted:{str(parsed['accepted']).lower()}")
        self._rows[key.business_idempotency_key] = DeliveredRow(key=key, external_record_key=external)
        if notes:
            self.notes[key.business_idempotency_key] = notes
        return DeliveryWriteResult(key=key, succeeded=True, external_record_key=external)

    def deliver(self, batch: DeliveryBatch) -> DeliveryAttempt:
        def submit(item):
            with _submit_slots:
                try:
                    return self._submit_one(item.key, item.payload)
                except Exception:
                    # Preserve other items' accepted receipts on an unexpected
                    # per-request failure; the normal business-key retry applies.
                    return DeliveryWriteResult(key=item.key, succeeded=False,
                        error_code='downstream_adapter_unavailable', retryable=True)

        if len(batch.items) <= 1:
            return DeliveryAttempt(results=tuple(submit(item) for item in batch.items))
        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=min(FACTORY_SUBMIT_CONCURRENCY, len(batch.items)),
                                thread_name_prefix='factory-submit') as executor:
            return DeliveryAttempt(results=tuple(executor.map(submit, batch.items)))

    def read_back(self, keys: Sequence[DeliveryKey]) -> tuple[DeliveredRow, ...]:
        rows = []
        for key in keys:
            row = self._rows.get(key.business_idempotency_key)
            if row is not None and row.key == key:
                rows.append(row)
        return tuple(rows)


def resolve_factory_submit_adapter(target: ConnectorContractVersion, *,
                                   send: Callable[[str, dict[str, str]], bytes] = send_upstream_request
                                   ) -> FactorySubmitDownstreamAdapter:
    if target.direction != 'downstream' or target.adapter_key != FACTORY_SUBMIT_ADAPTER_KEY:
        raise ConnectorDeliveryError('downstream_adapter_unavailable', '该下游合同尚未配置可执行适配器')
    if target.schema_fingerprint != FACTORY_SUBMIT_SCHEMA_FINGERPRINT:
        raise ConnectorDeliveryError('downstream_schema_drift', '下游结构与冻结合同不一致')
    return FactorySubmitDownstreamAdapter(resolve_locator_endpoint(target.logical_locator), send=send)

===== FILE backend/app/factory_test_claim.py =====
"""One-shot test claim with a durable intent. Never a production poller.

No automatic retry, submit, image download, or model call. Any recorded attempt
blocks another POST, including a process crash. The response journal is private
runtime data and must not be committed. Receipt IDs are not source versions.
"""
from dataclasses import asdict
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import sqlite3
import ssl
import time
import uuid
from typing import NamedTuple
from urllib.parse import urlsplit
from urllib.request import Request, HTTPRedirectHandler, HTTPSHandler, build_opener
from urllib.error import HTTPError, URLError

from .factory_claim_contract import claim_form, parse_claim_response

TEST_URL = 'https://userapitest.3d66.com/api/factory'
PRODUCTION_URL = 'https://userapi.3d66.com/api/factory'
# Each target is paired with the prefixes its content keys must carry. The two
# environments number tasks independently, so a shared prefix would let a
# production task id collide with an already seen test task id in the journal
# and in the downstream idempotency key. Never add a target without its pair.
class ClaimGateError(ValueError):
    pass


class Upstream(NamedTuple):
    identity: str       # content id prefix
    version: str        # content version prefix
    submission: str     # IncrementalMaterialJob.submission_id prefix


# Every prefix is namespaced by target. The test submission prefix was briefly
# the bare 'task:<id>' to keep already recorded jobs addressable; production
# tasks 1-5 then landed in that namespace through a forwarding bug, so the two
# sequences could dedupe each other. The namespaces are now disjoint, and the
# four genuine test tasks are held by the journal and by an explicit exclusion
# list rather than by sharing a namespace with production.
#: 测试环境（.137）用的 mock 工厂：只在非生产部署环境且显式配置了这个变量时才成为合法目标。
#: 生产环境无论怎么配都不会把它加进 allowlist——正式进料/投递只认上面两个域名。
MOCK_URL_ENV = 'LABEL_SYSTEM_FACTORY_MOCK_URL'
_NON_PRODUCTION_ENVS = frozenset({'local', 'dev', 'test', 'ci'})


def mock_upstream_target(environ=None) -> str | None:
    """Return the configured mock factory URL, or None outside non-production deployments."""
    env = os.environ if environ is None else environ
    deploy_env = (env.get('LABEL_SYSTEM_DEPLOY_ENV') or 'production').strip().lower()
    url = (env.get(MOCK_URL_ENV) or '').strip()
    if deploy_env not in _NON_PRODUCTION_ENVS or not url:
        return None
    parts = urlsplit(url)
    host = (parts.hostname or '').lower()
    if (parts.scheme not in ('http', 'https') or not host or host == '3d66.com' or host.endswith('.3d66.com')
            or parts.username or parts.password or parts.query or parts.fragment or url.endswith('/')):
        raise ClaimGateError('factory_mock_target_invalid')
    return url


def build_upstreams(environ=None) -> dict:
    targets = {
        TEST_URL: Upstream('factory-test', 'test-task:', 'test-task:'),
        PRODUCTION_URL: Upstream('factory-prod', 'prod-task:', 'prod-task:'),
    }
    mock = mock_upstream_target(environ)
    if mock:
        # 第三套前缀：mock 任务号从 1 起，和 test/prod 的任务号一样会撞，所以命名空间也分开。
        targets[mock] = Upstream('factory-mock', 'mock-task:', 'mock-task:')
    return targets


UPSTREAMS = build_upstreams()
MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES = 1024 * 1024

#: 灵感图（LgtInternalLLM）专用测试域名：研发 09-28 确认 userapitest 只路由 ResInternalLLM，
#: 灵感图测试要走 factory-api-test。它不进 3DSU 的 UPSTREAMS，只进灵感图自己的允许列表，
#: 任务号同样从 1 起，所以前缀也单独一套。
INSPIRATION_TEST_URL = 'https://factory-api-test.3d66.com/api/factory'
#: 灵感图正式域名（老大 09-28 转荣栋口径）。只对灵感图开放，且只有 ENABLED=1 才能确认/提交。
INSPIRATION_PRODUCTION_URL = 'https://factory-api.3d66.com/api/factory'


def build_inspiration_upstreams() -> dict:
    """灵感图只认自己的两个域名。

    不继承 3DSU 的 userapi / userapitest / mock（评审 T1）：灵感图端点变量一旦误配成 3DSU 域名，
    投递要在这里就被拒绝，而不是把灵感图结果打到 3DSU 工厂。两套任务号各自从 1 起，前缀也各自一套。
    """
    return {
        INSPIRATION_TEST_URL: Upstream('factory-lgt-test', 'lgt-test-task:', 'lgt-test-task:'),
        INSPIRATION_PRODUCTION_URL: Upstream('factory-lgt-prod', 'lgt-prod-task:', 'lgt-prod-task:'),
    }


INSPIRATION_UPSTREAMS = build_inspiration_upstreams()



def upstream_prefixes(url) -> Upstream:
    """Prefixes for an allowed target; refuse anything else."""
    if not isinstance(url, str) or url not in UPSTREAMS:
        raise ClaimGateError('factory_upstream_target_not_allowed')
    return UPSTREAMS[url]


def inspiration_upstream_prefixes(url) -> Upstream:
    """Prefixes for an inspiration-only target (test or production); refuse anything else."""
    if not isinstance(url, str) or url not in INSPIRATION_UPSTREAMS:
        raise ClaimGateError('factory_upstream_target_not_allowed')
    return INSPIRATION_UPSTREAMS[url]


def resource_identity(res_type, ll_id, img_id, *, url=TEST_URL):
    identity_prefix = upstream_prefixes(url).identity
    if type(res_type) is not int or res_type not in (1, 6):
        raise ClaimGateError('factory_resource_type_unmapped')
    if any(type(v) is not int or v <= 0 for v in (ll_id, img_id)):
        raise ClaimGateError('factory_identity_invalid')
    # Image-specific identity; not an assertion of upstream revision order.
    return {1: '3d', 6: 'su'}[res_type], f'{identity_prefix}:{res_type}:{ll_id}:{img_id}'


class NoRedirect(HTTPRedirectHandler):
    def redirect_request(self, req, fp, code, msg, headers, newurl):
        raise ClaimGateError('factory_redirect_refused')


def verified_tls_context():
    # Use the project's existing CA bundle; never disable TLS verification.
    import certifi
    return ssl.create_default_context(cafile=certifi.where())


def send_upstream_request(url, fields):
    # Allowlisted targets only; the response must come back from the same one.
    upstream_prefixes(url)
    boundary = '----FactoryTest-' + uuid.uuid4().hex
    parts = []
    for name, value in fields.items():
        parts.extend([f'--{boundary}', f'Content-Disposition: form-data; name="{name}"', '', value])
    parts.extend([f'--{boundary}--', ''])
    req = Request(url, data='\r\n'.join(parts).encode(), method='POST', headers={
        'Content-Type': f'multipart/form-data; boundary={boundary}',
        'Accept': 'application/json', 'User-Agent': '3d66-test-claim/1.0'})
    with build_opener(NoRedirect(), HTTPSHandler(context=verified_tls_context())).open(req, timeout=30) as response:
        if response.status != 200 or response.geturl() != url:
            raise ClaimGateError('factory_http_result_unknown')
        return response.read(MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES + 1)


# Retained so existing callers keep working; the target is no longer test-only.
send_test_request = send_upstream_request


def claim_once(path: Path, *, enabled=False, url=TEST_URL, send=send_upstream_request):
    if enabled is not True:
        raise ClaimGateError('factory_claim_disabled_or_wrong_target')
    upstream_prefixes(url)
    path = Path(path)
    # Parent is deliberately caller-owned; never silently create a broad path.
    fd = os.open(path, os.O_CREAT | os.O_RDWR | os.O_NOFOLLOW, 0o600)
    os.close(fd)
    os.chmod(path, 0o600)
    with sqlite3.connect(path, timeout=10) as db:
        db.execute('PRAGMA synchronous=FULL')
        db.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
        tables = {r[0] for r in db.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'")}
        if tables and tables != {'attempts', 'tasks'}:
            raise ClaimGateError('factory_journal_not_dedicated')
        db.execute('CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS attempts (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK(id=1), state TEXT NOT NULL, started_at INTEGER NOT NULL, response BLOB, error_code TEXT)')
        db.execute('CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tasks (task_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, resource_kind TEXT NOT NULL, content_id TEXT NOT NULL, payload_json TEXT NOT NULL)')
        if db.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM attempts').fetchone()[0]:
            raise ClaimGateError('factory_prior_claim_requires_reconciliation')
        timestamp = int(time.time())
        db.execute("INSERT INTO attempts(id,state,started_at) VALUES (1,'sending',?)", (timestamp,))
        db.commit()  # durable before bytes leave this process
        try:
            raw = send(url, claim_form(service='ResInternalLLM', timestamp=timestamp, limit=1))
            if not isinstance(raw, bytes) or len(raw) > MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES:
                raise ClaimGateError('factory_response_limit')
            db.execute('UPDATE attempts SET response=? WHERE id=1', (raw,))
            db.commit()  # retain response even when semantic parsing refuses it
            tasks = parse_claim_response(json.loads(raw), requested_limit=1, allowed_res_types={1, 6})
            for task in tasks:
                kind, identity = resource_identity(task.res_type, task.ll_id, task.img_id, url=url)
                db.execute('INSERT INTO tasks VALUES (?,?,?,?)',
                    (task.task_id, kind, identity, json.dumps(asdict(task), ensure_ascii=False)))
            db.execute("UPDATE attempts SET state='received' WHERE id=1")
            db.commit()
        except Exception as error:
            db.rollback()
            if isinstance(error, HTTPError):
                classification = 'http:' + str(error.code)
            else:
                cause = error.reason if isinstance(error, URLError) else error
                kind = type(cause).__name__
                classification = 'transport:' + (kind if kind in {
                    'TimeoutError','SSLCertVerificationError','ConnectionResetError',
                    'ConnectionRefusedError','gaierror'} else 'unknown')
            db.execute("UPDATE attempts SET state='unknown', error_code=? WHERE id=1", (classification,))
            db.commit()
            raise ClaimGateError('factory_claim_requires_reconciliation') from None
    return {'state': 'received', 'tasks': len(tasks), 'environment': 'test',
            'submit_calls': 0, 'model_calls': 0, 'image_downloads': 0,
            'automatic_claim': False, 'upstream_acknowledged': False}


def reconcile_saved_listing(path: Path, *, url=TEST_URL):
    """Reparse a durably saved response after a parser fix; zero network I/O."""
    path = Path(path)
    if path.is_symlink() or not path.is_file():
        raise ClaimGateError('factory_received_journal_required')
    with sqlite3.connect(path.resolve().as_uri()+'?mode=rw', uri=True, timeout=10) as db:
        db.execute('PRAGMA synchronous=FULL')
        db.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
        tables = {r[0] for r in db.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'")}
        if tables != {'attempts', 'tasks'}:
            raise ClaimGateError('factory_journal_not_dedicated')
        row = db.execute('SELECT state,response FROM attempts WHERE id=1').fetchone()
        if not row or row[0] != 'unknown' or not isinstance(row[1], bytes) or len(row[1]) > MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES:
            raise ClaimGateError('factory_saved_response_required')
        tasks = parse_claim_response(json.loads(row[1]), requested_limit=1, allowed_res_types={1,6})
        for task in tasks:
            kind, identity = resource_identity(task.res_type, task.ll_id, task.img_id, url=url)
            values = (task.task_id, kind, identity, json.dumps(asdict(task), ensure_ascii=False))
            old = db.execute('SELECT task_id,resource_kind,content_id,payload_json FROM tasks WHERE task_id=?', (task.task_id,)).fetchone()
            if old is not None and old != values:
                raise ClaimGateError('factory_staged_identity_conflict')
            if old is None:
                db.execute('INSERT INTO tasks VALUES (?,?,?,?)', values)
        if db.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM tasks').fetchone()[0] != len(tasks):
            raise ClaimGateError('factory_staged_identity_conflict')
        db.execute("UPDATE attempts SET state='received',error_code=NULL WHERE id=1")
        db.commit()
    return dict(state='received', tasks=len(tasks), network_calls=0, upstream_acknowledged=False)


def confirm_received_once(path: Path, *, enabled=False, url=TEST_URL, send=send_upstream_request):
    """Explicit one-shot TEST confirmation; unknown/claimed elsewhere never retries.

    Does not dispatch inference or submit results. A listed payload must already
    exist in this private journal before any confirmation request is sent.
    """
    from .factory_claim_contract import confirm_form, parse_confirm_response
    if enabled is not True:
        raise ClaimGateError('factory_confirm_disabled_or_wrong_target')
    upstream_prefixes(url)
    path = Path(path)
    if path.is_symlink() or not path.is_file():
        raise ClaimGateError('factory_received_journal_required')
    with sqlite3.connect(path.resolve().as_uri() + '?mode=rw', uri=True, timeout=10) as db:
        db.execute('PRAGMA synchronous=FULL')
        db.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
        tables = {r[0] for r in db.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'")}
        if tables not in ({'attempts', 'tasks'}, {'attempts', 'tasks', 'confirmations'}):
            raise ClaimGateError('factory_journal_not_dedicated')
        if db.execute('SELECT state FROM attempts WHERE id=1').fetchone() != ('received',):
            raise ClaimGateError('factory_received_response_required')
        ids = [r[0] for r in db.execute('SELECT task_id FROM tasks ORDER BY task_id')]
        form = confirm_form(timestamp=int(time.time()), task_ids=ids)
        db.execute('CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS confirmations (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK(id=1), state TEXT NOT NULL, task_ids TEXT NOT NULL, response BLOB)')
        if db.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM confirmations').fetchone()[0]:
            raise ClaimGateError('factory_prior_confirmation_requires_reconciliation')
        db.execute("INSERT INTO confirmations VALUES (1,'sending',?,NULL)", (json.dumps(ids),))
        db.commit()
        try:
            raw = send(url, form)
            if not isinstance(raw, bytes) or len(raw) > MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES:
                raise ClaimGateError('factory_response_limit')
            db.execute('UPDATE confirmations SET response=? WHERE id=1', (raw,))
            db.commit()
            result = parse_confirm_response(json.loads(raw), requested_task_ids=ids)
            state = ('confirmed' if len(result['confirmed_task_ids']) == len(ids)
                     else 'requires_reconciliation')
            db.execute('UPDATE confirmations SET state=? WHERE id=1', (state,))
            db.commit()
        except Exception:
            db.rollback()
            db.execute("UPDATE confirmations SET state='unknown' WHERE id=1")
            db.commit()
            raise ClaimGateError('factory_confirmation_requires_reconciliation') from None
    return dict(state=state, environment='test', automatic_retry=False,
                model_calls=0, submit_calls=0, upstream_acknowledged=state == 'confirmed')


def stage_confirmed(journal: Path, target: Path, *, local_asset_id: int, enabled=False,
                    url=TEST_URL):
    """Live-test bridge: confirmation first, then the existing LOCAL staging path."""
    if enabled is not True:
        return {'staged': 0, 'paused': True}
    path = Path(journal)
    if path.is_symlink() or not path.is_file():
        raise ClaimGateError('factory_confirmed_journal_required')
    with sqlite3.connect(path.resolve().as_uri() + '?mode=ro', uri=True) as db:
        exists = db.execute("SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name='confirmations'").fetchone()
        if not exists or db.execute('SELECT state FROM confirmations WHERE id=1').fetchone() != ('confirmed',):
            raise ClaimGateError('factory_confirmed_journal_required')
        ids = [r[0] for r in db.execute('SELECT task_id FROM tasks ORDER BY task_id')]
        frozen = json.loads(db.execute('SELECT task_ids FROM confirmations WHERE id=1').fetchone()[0])
        if ids != frozen:
            raise ClaimGateError('factory_confirmation_identity_conflict')
    result = stage_received(journal, target, local_asset_id=local_asset_id, enabled=True, url=url)
    result['upstream_acknowledged'] = True
    return result


def stage_received(journal: Path, target: Path, *, local_asset_id: int, enabled=False,
                   url=TEST_URL):
    """Feed a dedicated test source DB, using an explicit local placeholder asset.

    Native dispatch must remain simulated; this function does not dispatch work.
    Version is a task identity, not an upstream modification sequence. A replay
    with changed identity/asset is rejected instead of overwriting an event.
    """
    if enabled is not True:
        return {'staged': 0, 'paused': True}
    if type(local_asset_id) is not int or local_asset_id <= 0:
        raise ClaimGateError('factory_local_placeholder_required')
    journal, target = Path(journal), Path(target)
    if journal.resolve() == target.resolve():
        raise ClaimGateError('factory_source_must_be_separate')
    with sqlite3.connect(journal.resolve().as_uri() + '?mode=ro', uri=True) as db:
        state=db.execute('SELECT state FROM attempts WHERE id=1').fetchone()
        if state != ('received',):
            raise ClaimGateError('factory_received_response_required')
        payloads=[json.loads(r[0]) for r in db.execute('SELECT payload_json FROM tasks ORDER BY task_id')]
    prepared=[]
    for payload in payloads:
        envelope={'code':1,'request_id':'stored-response','data':[payload]}
        task,=parse_claim_response(envelope,requested_limit=1,allowed_res_types={1,6})
        version_prefix = upstream_prefixes(url).version
        kind, identity=resource_identity(task.res_type,task.ll_id,task.img_id,url=url)
        prepared.append((task.task_id,identity,version_prefix+str(task.task_id),
                         'model_3d_su','content.created',local_asset_id,kind))
    fd=os.open(target,os.O_CREAT|os.O_RDWR|os.O_NOFOLLOW,0o600);os.close(fd)
    inserted=0
