    with sqlite3.connect(target,timeout=10) as db:
        db.execute('PRAGMA synchronous=FULL');db.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
        tables={r[0] for r in db.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'")}
        if tables and tables != {'changes'}:
            raise ClaimGateError('factory_source_not_dedicated')
        db.execute('CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS changes (seq INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, task_id INTEGER NOT NULL UNIQUE, content_id TEXT NOT NULL, source_version TEXT NOT NULL, category_key TEXT NOT NULL, event_type TEXT NOT NULL, asset_id INTEGER NOT NULL, resource_kind TEXT NOT NULL, occurred_at TEXT NOT NULL)')
        for row in prepared:
            old=db.execute('SELECT task_id,content_id,source_version,category_key,event_type,asset_id,resource_kind FROM changes WHERE task_id=?',(row[0],)).fetchone()
            if old is not None:
                if old != row:raise ClaimGateError('factory_staged_identity_conflict')
                continue
            db.execute('INSERT INTO changes(task_id,content_id,source_version,category_key,event_type,asset_id,resource_kind,occurred_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)',
                       (*row,datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()))
            inserted+=1
    return {'staged':inserted,'paused':False,'upstream_acknowledged':False,
            'source_time_basis':'local_staging_only','model_execution_mode_required':'simulated'}

===== FILE backend/app/failure_console.py =====
"""失败处理：跑挂或投挂的素材，逐条摆给人看，人来决定重试还是直接给结论。

运行中心那张卡管的是「调度条目」和「投递事件」，运营认的是**素材**。所以这里一行一条
素材，带全它的身份（run / task / img / ll / 分类）、卡在哪一步、错误码与工厂原话、
试了几次、投没投过，以及这条现在能做什么。

人工结论不碰 DAG（老大 2026-09-20 的选择）：节点输出是证据，模型跑出来什么就是什么，
人填的东西不伪装成节点输出。结论单独落 ``manual_failure_resolutions``，投下游的那一份
payload 里 ``prompt_a_status='manual'`` 自证来源。

3DSU 从 ``production_fields`` 投影；灵感图从 ``a_fields`` 投影。两者分别按目标合同
校验人工字段，调用 A 的草稿只用于预填，不成为通过校验的模型事实。
"""

from __future__ import annotations
from .workflow_snapshot_store import read_run_snapshot_text

import hashlib
import json
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any, Mapping, Sequence

from sqlalchemy import func, or_, select, String
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, aliased

from .api_datetime_encoding import platform_isoformat
from .audit import append_audit_event
from .failure_resolution_models import ManualFailureResolution
from .factory_submit_contract import GRADE_BY_LEVEL, LIMITS, MIN_TAGS
from .models import (
    Asset,
    AuditEvent,
    ContentIngressEvent,
    DownstreamPublishLedger,
    ProductionRun,
    ProductionStepAttempt,
    RuntimeOutboxEvent,
    WorkflowModelInvocation,
)

#: 跑挂的素材：终态失败，和还在自动重试里打转的。canceled 不算——那是发布窗口
#: 或进料自己取消的，不是失败（09-15~09-16 留下 153 条，混进来只会淹没真问题）。
RUNTIME_FAILURE_STATUSES = ("failed", "retryable")
#: 人填的调用 A 字段。键名跟 production_fields 对齐，投影函数直接吃。
MANUAL_TEXT_FIELDS = (
    ("title", "title"),
    ("seotitle", "seo_title"),
    ("category", "category"),
    ("style", "style"),
    ("cons", "cons"),
    ("design", "design"),
)


class FailureConsoleError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str, *, status_code: int = 409) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code
        self.status_code = status_code


def _json_object(raw: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if isinstance(raw, Mapping):
        return dict(raw)
    if isinstance(raw, str) and raw.strip():
        try:
            parsed = json.loads(raw)
        except ValueError:
            return {}
        return dict(parsed) if isinstance(parsed, Mapping) else {}
    return {}


def _manifest(run: ProductionRun) -> dict[str, Any]:
    snapshot = _json_object(read_run_snapshot_text(run))
    workflow = snapshot.get("workflow")
    manifest = workflow.get("manifest") if isinstance(workflow, Mapping) else None
    return dict(manifest) if isinstance(manifest, Mapping) else {}


def _downstream_node_id(manifest: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str | None:
    for node in manifest.get("nodes", []) or []:
        if isinstance(node, Mapping) and node.get("kind") == "downstream_projection":
            return str(node.get("node_instance_id") or "") or None
    return None


def _frozen_levels(run: ProductionRun) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    from .workflow_short_circuit import enabled_levels_from_manifest

    try:
        return tuple(enabled_levels_from_manifest(_manifest(run)))
    except Exception:  # pragma: no cover - 快照损坏时不让页面整个挂掉
        return ()


def submittable_levels(run: ProductionRun) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    """冻结启用等级与目标映射的交集：3DSU 为 L1–L4，灵感图按绑定合同。"""

    if run.category_key == "inspiration_image":
        from .failure_console_inspiration import target_mapping
        mapping = target_mapping(run)
        return tuple(level for level in _frozen_levels(run) if level in mapping)
    return tuple(level for level in _frozen_levels(run) if level in GRADE_BY_LEVEL)


def _last_attempts(db: Session, run_ids: Sequence[int]) -> dict[int, ProductionStepAttempt]:
    """每个 run 最后一次失败的那一步；没有失败步就取最后一步。"""

    if not run_ids:
        return {}
    rows = db.scalars(
        select(ProductionStepAttempt)
        .where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id.in_(list(run_ids)))
        .order_by(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id, ProductionStepAttempt.sequence, ProductionStepAttempt.attempt_no)
    ).all()
    chosen: dict[int, ProductionStepAttempt] = {}
    for row in rows:
        run_id = int(row.run_id)
        current = chosen.get(run_id)
        if current is None:
            chosen[run_id] = row
            continue
        # 失败的那步最说明问题；都失败就取靠后的。
        if row.status in ("failed", "retryable") or current.status not in ("failed", "retryable"):
            chosen[run_id] = row
    return chosen


def _stage_a_output(db: Session, run_id: int) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    """调用 A 跑成过就把它的输出拿回来，用于预填人工结论表单。"""

    row = db.scalar(
        select(ProductionStepAttempt.output_manifest_json)
        .where(
            ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run_id,
            ProductionStepAttempt.step_key == "prompt_a",
            ProductionStepAttempt.status == "succeeded",
        )
        .order_by(ProductionStepAttempt.attempt_no.desc())
        .limit(1)
    )
    if not row:
        return None
    output = _json_object(row).get("output")
    return dict(output) if isinstance(output, Mapping) else None


def _resolutions(db: Session, run_ids: Sequence[int]) -> dict[int, ManualFailureResolution]:
    if not run_ids:
        return {}
    rows = db.scalars(
        select(ManualFailureResolution).where(
            ManualFailureResolution.production_run_id.in_(list(run_ids)),
            ManualFailureResolution.status == "published",
        )
    ).all()
    return {int(row.production_run_id): row for row in rows}


def _row(
    run: ProductionRun,
    *,
    attempt: ProductionStepAttempt | None,
    asset: Asset | None,
    stage_a: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
    resolution: ManualFailureResolution | None,
    published: bool,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    upstream_class_label = None
    from .incremental_correction import upstream_class_label as _label

    upstream_class_label = _label(run.upstream_class_id)
    return {
        "kind": "runtime",
        "production_run_id": int(run.id),
        "run_key": run.run_key,
        "status": run.status,
        "attempt_count": int(run.attempt_count or 0),
        "category_key": run.category_key or "",
        "asset_id": int(asset.id) if asset is not None else None,
        "asset_name": asset.original_name if asset is not None else "",
        "upstream": {
            "task_id": run.upstream_task_id,
            "img_id": run.upstream_img_id,
            "ll_id": run.upstream_ll_id,
            "class_id": run.upstream_class_id,
            "class_label": upstream_class_label,
        },
        "failed_step": attempt.step_key if attempt is not None else "",
        "failed_step_status": attempt.status if attempt is not None else "",
        "error_code": (attempt.last_error_code or "") if attempt is not None else "",
        "error_message": (attempt.last_error_message or "") if attempt is not None else "",
        "created_at": platform_isoformat(run.created_at) if run.created_at else None,
        "updated_at": platform_isoformat(run.updated_at) if run.updated_at else None,
        #: 调用 A 跑成过就能预填字段；没跑成人工得自己填全。
        "stage_a_available": stage_a is not None,
        "downstream_published": published,
        "submittable_levels": list(submittable_levels(run)),
        "resolution": _resolution_payload(resolution) if resolution is not None else None,
    }


def _resolution_payload(row: ManualFailureResolution) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "id": int(row.id),
        "level": row.level,
        "score": int(row.score),
        "fields": _json_object(row.fields_json),
        "reason": row.reason,
        "actor": row.actor,
        "outbox_event_id": row.outbox_event_id,
        "created_at": platform_isoformat(row.created_at) if row.created_at else None,
    }


def _superseded_by_success():
    """同一素材（content_record）之后又有一条增量运行成功：失败已被后续运行补上。

    09-30 复盘：失败处理页只把「人工结论」「重新评估已结算」算作已处理，素材后来被重新接入
    （缩图重进、上游重发、换机制重跑）并成功投递的失败仍挂在未处理里——7 条里 6 条都是这种。
    """
    failed_event = aliased(ContentIngressEvent)
    later_event = aliased(ContentIngressEvent)
    later_run = aliased(ProductionRun)
    return (
        select(later_run.id)
        .join(later_event, later_event.id == later_run.source_id)
        .join(failed_event, failed_event.content_record_id == later_event.content_record_id)
        .where(
            failed_event.id == ProductionRun.source_id,
            later_run.source_type == "content_ingress_event",
            later_run.status == "succeeded",
            later_run.id > ProductionRun.id,
        )
    )


def list_failures(
    db: Session,
    *,
    limit: int = 50,
    offset: int = 0,
    resolved: bool | None = None,
    search: str | None = None,
    category_key: str | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """跑挂的素材，一行一条。``resolved`` 为 None 时两种都给。"""

    limit = max(1, min(int(limit or 50), 200))
    offset = max(0, int(offset or 0))
    statement = select(ProductionRun).where(
        ProductionRun.source_type == "content_ingress_event",
        ProductionRun.status.in_(RUNTIME_FAILURE_STATUSES),
    )
    if category_key:
        statement = statement.where(ProductionRun.category_key == category_key)
    if resolved is not None:
        has_resolution = select(ManualFailureResolution.id).where(
            ManualFailureResolution.production_run_id == ProductionRun.id,
            ManualFailureResolution.status == "published",
        ).exists()
        has_reassessment = select(AuditEvent.id).where(
            AuditEvent.action == 'incremental_reassessment_settled',
            AuditEvent.subject_type == 'production_run',
            AuditEvent.subject_id == func.cast(ProductionRun.id, String),
        ).exists()
        handled = or_(has_resolution, has_reassessment, _superseded_by_success().exists())
        statement = statement.where(handled if resolved else ~handled)
    if search and str(search).strip():
        token = str(search).strip()
        conditions = [ProductionRun.run_key.like(f"%{token}%")]
        if token.isdigit():
            number = int(token)
            conditions.extend([
                ProductionRun.id == number,
                ProductionRun.upstream_task_id == number,
                ProductionRun.upstream_img_id == number,
                ProductionRun.upstream_ll_id == number,
            ])
        statement = statement.where(or_(*conditions))
    total = db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(statement.subquery())) or 0
    if offset >= int(total):
        # MySQL may otherwise scan the large frozen payloads in id order even
        # after the count has proved this page cannot contain a row.
        return {"items": [], "total": int(total), "limit": limit,
                "offset": offset, "has_more": False}
    runs = db.scalars(
        statement.order_by(ProductionRun.id.desc()).limit(limit).offset(offset)
    ).all()
    run_ids = [int(run.id) for run in runs]
    attempts = _last_attempts(db, run_ids)
    resolutions = _resolutions(db, run_ids)
    published_keys = set()
    if runs:
        published_keys = {
            str(key)
            for (key,) in db.execute(
                select(DownstreamPublishLedger.origin_run_key).where(
                    DownstreamPublishLedger.origin_run_key.in_([run.run_key for run in runs])
                )
            )
        }
    reassessments = {int(child.source_run_id): {'production_run_id':child.id,'status':child.status,
        'delivered': bool(db.scalar(select(AuditEvent.id).where(AuditEvent.event_key==f'incremental-reassessment-settled:{child.source_run_id}')))}
        for child in db.scalars(select(ProductionRun).where(ProductionRun.source_run_id.in_(run_ids), ProductionRun.source_type=='content_ingress_event'))}
    superseded = {}
    if runs:
        failed_event = aliased(ContentIngressEvent)
        later_event = aliased(ContentIngressEvent)
        later_run = aliased(ProductionRun)
        for failed_id, later_id in db.execute(
            select(ProductionRun.id, func.min(later_run.id))
            .join(failed_event, failed_event.id == ProductionRun.source_id)
            .join(later_event, later_event.content_record_id == failed_event.content_record_id)
            .join(later_run, later_run.source_id == later_event.id)
            .where(ProductionRun.id.in_(run_ids), later_run.source_type == "content_ingress_event",
                   later_run.status == "succeeded", later_run.id > ProductionRun.id)
            .group_by(ProductionRun.id)
        ):
            superseded[int(failed_id)] = {"production_run_id": int(later_id), "status": "succeeded"}
    items = []
    for run in runs:
        resolution = resolutions.get(int(run.id))
        asset_id = run.upstream_img_id
        asset = None
        frozen = _json_object(_json_object(read_run_snapshot_text(run)).get("runtime_context")).get("input_manifest")
        if isinstance(frozen, Mapping) and frozen.get("asset_id") is not None:
            asset = db.get(Asset, int(frozen["asset_id"]))
        items.append(
            {**_row(
                run,
                attempt=attempts.get(int(run.id)),
                asset=asset,
                stage_a=_stage_a_output(db, int(run.id)),
                resolution=resolution,
                published=run.run_key in published_keys,
            ), "reassessment": reassessments.get(run.id),
            # 已被同一素材后续成功的运行补上（界面显示为已处理，并链到那条运行）
            "superseded_by": superseded.get(int(run.id))}
        )
    return {"items": items, "total": int(total), "limit": limit, "offset": offset,
            "has_more": offset + len(runs) < int(total)}


def resolution_form(db: Session, run_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """人工结论表单：该填什么、限制多少、能不能预填。"""

    run = db.scalar(select(ProductionRun).where(ProductionRun.id == int(run_id)).with_for_update().execution_options(populate_existing=True))
    if run is None:
        raise FailureConsoleError("failure_run_not_found", "运行记录不存在", status_code=404)
    stage_a = _stage_a_output(db, int(run.id))
    if run.category_key == "inspiration_image":
        from .failure_console_inspiration import form_fields
        return {"production_run_id": int(run.id), "stage_a_available": stage_a is not None,
                "submittable_levels": list(submittable_levels(run)),
                **form_fields(db, run, stage_a)}
    production = _json_object((stage_a or {}).get("production_fields"))
    candidate_id = None
    validation_error = ""
    if not production:
        rejected = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation).where(
            WorkflowModelInvocation.production_run_id == run.id,
            WorkflowModelInvocation.node_instance_id == "prompt_a",
            WorkflowModelInvocation.error_code == "structured_output_schema_mismatch",
        ).order_by(WorkflowModelInvocation.id.desc()).limit(1))
        if rejected is not None:
            evidence = _json_object(_json_object(rejected.evidence_json).get("failure"))
            failure = _json_object(evidence.get("failure_evidence"))
            production = _json_object(failure.get("candidate_production_fields"))
            if production:
                candidate_id = rejected.id
                validation_error = str(failure.get("validation_error") or rejected.error_message)
    prefill = {name: production.get(name, "") for name, _ in MANUAL_TEXT_FIELDS}
    tags = production.get("tags")
    prefill["tags"] = [str(item) for item in tags] if isinstance(tags, list) else []
    return {
        "production_run_id": int(run.id),
        "field_profile": "model_3d_su",
        "stage_a_available": stage_a is not None,
        "candidate_invocation_id": candidate_id,
        "validation_error": validation_error,
        "submittable_levels": list(submittable_levels(run)),
        "limits": {field: LIMITS[limit_key] for field, limit_key in MANUAL_TEXT_FIELDS},
        "min_tags": MIN_TAGS,
        "prefill": prefill,
    }


def _validate_fields(fields: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """逐字段按下游合同校验。**不截断**——运营得知道自己写超了，而不是被悄悄砍掉。"""

    cleaned: dict[str, Any] = {}
    for name, limit_key in MANUAL_TEXT_FIELDS:
        value = fields.get(name)
        text = value.strip() if isinstance(value, str) else ""
        if not text:
            raise FailureConsoleError(
                "failure_resolution_field_required", f"{name} 必填", status_code=422
            )
        limit = LIMITS[limit_key]
        if len(text) > limit:
            raise FailureConsoleError(
                "failure_resolution_field_too_long",
                f"{name} 最多 {limit} 个字，现在是 {len(text)} 个",
                status_code=422,
            )
        cleaned[name] = text
    raw_tags = fields.get("tags")
    tags = [str(item).strip() for item in raw_tags if str(item).strip()] if isinstance(raw_tags, list) else []
    if len(tags) < MIN_TAGS:
        raise FailureConsoleError(
            "failure_resolution_tags_insufficient",
            f"标签至少 {MIN_TAGS} 个，现在是 {len(tags)} 个",
            status_code=422,
        )
    cleaned["tags"] = tags
    return cleaned


def resolve_failure(
    db: Session,
    *,
    run_id: int,
    level: str,
    score: Any,
    fields: Mapping[str, Any],
    reason: str,
    actor: str,
    idempotency_key: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """人给这条跑挂的素材一个最终结论，并按这个结论投一版下游。

    不写节点输出、不改 run 状态：DAG 上它就是挂了，这是事实。结论单独落表，
    投出去的 payload 里 ``prompt_a_status='manual'`` 与 ``human_override`` 一起
    说明这一版是人填的。
    """

    from .downstream_publish_ledger import record_downstream_publish
    from .runtime_outbox import enqueue_runtime_outbox
    from .workflow_runtime import build_incremental_projection_outbox_request

    reason = (reason or "").strip()
    if not reason:
        raise FailureConsoleError("failure_resolution_reason_required", "必须填写理由", status_code=422)
    if not (idempotency_key or "").strip():
        raise FailureConsoleError("failure_resolution_idempotency_required", "缺少幂等键", status_code=422)

    existing = db.scalar(
        select(ManualFailureResolution).where(
            ManualFailureResolution.idempotency_key == idempotency_key.strip()
        )
    )
    if existing is not None:
        # 网络重试送来同一个键：原样还回去，不重复投递。
        return _resolution_payload(existing)
    replayed = db.scalar(
        select(AuditEvent).where(AuditEvent.event_key == _resolution_audit_key(idempotency_key))
    )
    if replayed is not None:
        # 这个键的结论已被后来的人工结论覆盖：还回当前结论，不能把旧值再投一遍。
        current = db.get(ManualFailureResolution, int(_json_object(replayed.payload_json).get("resolution_id") or 0))
        if current is not None:
            return _resolution_payload(current)

    run = db.scalar(select(ProductionRun).where(ProductionRun.id == int(run_id)).with_for_update().execution_options(populate_existing=True))
    if run is None:
        raise FailureConsoleError("failure_run_not_found", "运行记录不存在", status_code=404)
    from .incremental_reassessment import assert_no_replacement
    assert_no_replacement(db, run)
    if run.status not in RUNTIME_FAILURE_STATUSES:
        raise FailureConsoleError(
            "failure_run_not_failed",
            "只有失败或仍在重试的素材需要人工结论",
        )
    # 人工结论是最高优先级，人与人之间后提交的覆盖先提交的（2026-09-26 老大裁决）。
    previous = db.scalar(
        select(ManualFailureResolution).where(
            ManualFailureResolution.production_run_id == int(run.id),
            ManualFailureResolution.status == "published",
        ).with_for_update()
    )
    allowed = submittable_levels(run)
    if not allowed:
        raise FailureConsoleError(
            "failure_resolution_levels_unavailable",
            "该工作流没有可投递的等级，无法给结论",
        )
    if level not in allowed:
        raise FailureConsoleError(
            "failure_resolution_level_invalid",
            "等级只能是：" + "、".join(allowed),
            status_code=422,
        )
    if isinstance(score, bool) or not isinstance(score, int) or not 0 <= score <= 100:
        raise FailureConsoleError(
            "failure_resolution_score_invalid", "分数必须是 0-100 的整数", status_code=422
        )
    inspiration = run.category_key == "inspiration_image"
    if inspiration:
        from .failure_console_inspiration import validate_fields
        cleaned = validate_fields(fields)
    else:
        cleaned = _validate_fields(fields)

    manifest = _manifest(run)
    node_id = _downstream_node_id(manifest)
    if node_id is None:
        raise FailureConsoleError(
            "failure_resolution_no_downstream", "该工作流没有写入下游节点，结论无处可投"
        )
    stage_a = _stage_a_output(db, int(run.id)) or {}
    # 任务身份来自冻结输入（进料时就钉住了），调用 A 跑成过的话它的输出里也有一份。
    frozen = _json_object(_json_object(read_run_snapshot_text(run)).get("runtime_context")).get("input_manifest")
    if inspiration:
        from .failure_console_inspiration import frozen_task
        factory_task = frozen_task(_json_object(frozen))
    else:
        factory_task = _json_object(stage_a.get("factory_task")) or _json_object(_json_object(frozen).get("factory_task"))
    task_id = factory_task.get("task_id")
    if isinstance(task_id, bool) or not isinstance(task_id, int) or task_id < 1:
        # 没有任务号就投不出去。当场说清楚，别让运营填完一整张表单再被投递器拒掉。
        raise FailureConsoleError(
            "failure_resolution_task_identity_missing",
            "这条素材的冻结输入里没有工厂任务号，人工结论无法投递",
        )
    correction_event_id = "manual-failure:" + hashlib.sha256(
        json.dumps({"run": int(run.id), "key": idempotency_key.strip()}, sort_keys=True).encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()[:40]
    human_override = {
        "schema_version": "manual-failure-resolution-v1",
        "field": "final_level",
        "previous_level": None,
        "level": level,
        "actor": actor,
        "reason": reason,
        "correction_event_id": correction_event_id,
    }
    output = {
        "schema_version": "factory-result-v1",
        "factory_task": factory_task,
        "final_level": level,
        "final_score": score,
        # 调用 A 没有跑成（或跑成了但后面挂了）：这一份字段是人填的，如实标明。
        "prompt_a_fields": {"production_fields": dict(cleaned), "model_3d_su_fields": {}},
        "prompt_a_status": "manual",
        "prompt_b_status": "manual",
        "prompt_b_aesthetic_score": None,
        "normalized_result": {"level": level, "score": score, "source": "manual_failure_resolution"},
        "human_override": human_override,
        "external_io_performed": False,
    }
    if inspiration:
        output["schema_version"] = "inspiration-factory-result-v1"
        output["inspiration_task"] = output.pop("factory_task")
        output["a_fields"] = dict(cleaned)
        output.pop("prompt_a_fields")
        output["score_source"] = "manual_failure_resolution"
        from .failure_console_inspiration import target_mapping
        from .inspiration_factory_contract import build_submit_body, InspirationFactoryContractError
        try:
            build_submit_body(output, level_mapping=target_mapping(run),
                              human_override={"override_source": "human_correction"})
        except InspirationFactoryContractError as exc:
            raise FailureConsoleError("failure_resolution_contract_invalid",
                                      "灵感图结论不符合目标合同", status_code=422) from exc
    node_result = {
        "schema_version": "workflow-node-result-v1",
        "node_instance_id": node_id,
        "node_kind": "downstream_projection",
        # 这份清单只描述「投出去的东西」，不是节点跑过的证据——DAG 上这条 run 仍是失败。
        "status": "manual_resolution",
        "attempt": 0,
        "manual_resolution": {"actor": actor, "reason": reason, "correction_event_id": correction_event_id},
        "output": output,
    }
    request = build_incremental_projection_outbox_request(
        db,
        run,
        step_key=node_id,
        output_manifest=node_result,
        review_state="reviewed",
        change={
            "trigger_kind": "human_correction",
            "actor": actor,
            "reason": reason,
            "correction_event_id": correction_event_id,
        },
    )
    event_id = None
    payload: Mapping[str, Any] = {}
    if request is not None:
        event = enqueue_runtime_outbox(db, request)
        record_downstream_publish(db, event=event, payload=request.payload)
        event_id = int(event.id)
        payload = request.payload
    previous_summary = None
    if previous is not None:
        previous_summary = {
            "level": previous.level, "score": int(previous.score), "actor": previous.actor,
            "idempotency_key": previous.idempotency_key, "outbox_event_id": previous.outbox_event_id,
        }
        # 一条素材只有一行结论（唯一约束）：原地改成新结论，旧值留在审计里。
        previous.idempotency_key = idempotency_key.strip()
        previous.level = level
        previous.score = score
        previous.fields_json = json.dumps(cleaned, ensure_ascii=False)
        previous.payload_json = json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, default=str)
        previous.reason = reason
        previous.actor = actor[:80]
        previous.outbox_event_id = event_id
    row = previous if previous is not None else ManualFailureResolution(
        idempotency_key=idempotency_key.strip(),
        production_run_id=int(run.id),
        run_key=run.run_key or "",
        category_key=run.category_key or "",
        upstream_task_id=run.upstream_task_id,
        level=level,
        score=score,
        fields_json=json.dumps(cleaned, ensure_ascii=False),
        payload_json=json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, default=str),
        reason=reason,
        actor=actor[:80],
        outbox_event_id=event_id,
    )
    if previous is None:
        db.add(row)
    db.flush()
    if run.status == "retryable":
        # The scheduler would otherwise keep retrying this run on its own and
        # could publish a model result over the human conclusion.
        from .workflow_runtime import terminalize_runtime_run

        terminalize_runtime_run(
            db,
            run,
            status="failed",
            error_code="manual_failure_resolved",
            error_message="已有人工结论，停止自动重试",
        )
    append_audit_event(
        db,
        category="failure_console",
        action="manual_resolution",
        subject_type="production_run",
        subject_id=int(run.id),
        actor=actor,
        payload={
            "resolution_id": int(row.id),
            "level": level,
            "score": score,
            "reason": reason,
            "outbox_event_id": event_id,
            "stage_a_available": bool(stage_a),
            "correction_event_id": correction_event_id,
            "superseded": previous_summary,
        },
        event_key=_resolution_audit_key(idempotency_key),
    )
    # 素材大宽表（ADR-0111）：人工结论落表后同事务刷新；失败只记日志。
    from .wide_material import refresh_for_run

    refresh_for_run(db, run, event_kind="manual_resolution")
    return _resolution_payload(row)


def _resolution_audit_key(idempotency_key: str) -> str:
    return "failure-resolution:" + hashlib.sha256(idempotency_key.strip().encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


def retry_failure(db: Session, *, run_id: int, actor: str, reason: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """按运行重试：只重跑失败的那一步，成功节点不重放，也重开传输窗口。"""

    from .workflow_runtime import WorkflowRuntimeError, retry_failed_run_from_checkpoint

    reason = (reason or "").strip()
    if not reason:
        raise FailureConsoleError("failure_retry_reason_required", "重试必须填写理由", status_code=422)
    run = db.get(ProductionRun, int(run_id))
    if run is None:
        raise FailureConsoleError("failure_run_not_found", "运行记录不存在", status_code=404)
    try:
        updated = retry_failed_run_from_checkpoint(db, int(run.id), actor=actor)
    except WorkflowRuntimeError as exc:
        raise FailureConsoleError(exc.code, str(exc), status_code=getattr(exc, "status_code", 409)) from exc
    append_audit_event(
        db,
        category="failure_console",
        action="retry",
        subject_type="production_run",
        subject_id=int(run.id),
        actor=actor,
        payload={"reason": reason, "status": updated.status},
    )
    return {"production_run_id": int(updated.id), "status": updated.status}

===== FILE backend/app/failure_console_api.py =====
"""失败处理页的读写接口。

三个动作：看列表、按运行重试、给人工结论。结论要带一整份调用 A 的字段——
校验在 ``failure_console`` 里做，这里只负责形状和权限。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Callable

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query
from fastapi.exceptions import RequestValidationError
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field, ValidationError, field_validator
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .database import get_db
from .failure_console import (
    FailureConsoleError,
    list_failures,
    resolution_form,
    resolve_failure,
    retry_failure,
)


def _http(exc: FailureConsoleError) -> HTTPException:
    return HTTPException(status_code=exc.status_code, detail={"code": exc.code, "message": str(exc)})


def _actor(user: Any) -> str:
    return str(getattr(user, "username", None) or getattr(user, "display_name", None) or "unknown")


class ResolutionFields(BaseModel):
    title: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=40)
    seotitle: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=80)
    category: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=80)
    style: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=80)
    cons: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=120)
    design: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=120)
    #: 下限 4 个由合同定，这里只挡住明显离谱的量。
    tags: list[str] = Field(min_length=1, max_length=20)


class InspirationResolutionFields(BaseModel):
    title: str
    seo_title: str
    class_text: str
    style: str
    tags: list[str]
    cons: str
    design: str
    reason: str
    trait: str
    image_defects: str


class ResolutionRequest(BaseModel):
    level: str = Field(min_length=2, max_length=10)
    score: int = Field(ge=0, le=100)
    fields: dict[str, Any]
    reason: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=500)
    idempotency_key: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=120)

    @field_validator("reason")
    @classmethod
    def _reason_not_blank(cls, value: str) -> str:
        if not value.strip():
            raise ValueError("理由不能为空")
        return value


class RetryRequest(BaseModel):
    reason: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=500)

    @field_validator("reason")
    @classmethod
    def _reason_not_blank(cls, value: str) -> str:
        if not value.strip():
            raise ValueError("理由不能为空")
        return value


class RecoveryRequest(BaseModel):
    quote_hash: str = Field(min_length=64, max_length=64)
    requests: int = Field(ge=1, le=2, strict=True)
    reason: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=500)
    confirmed: bool = Field(strict=True)


class InvocationReviewRequest(BaseModel):
    quote_hash: str = Field(pattern=r"^[0-9a-f]{64}$")
    evidence_reference: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=1000)
    reason: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=1000)
    execution_ended_confirmed: bool = Field(strict=True)
    liability_retained_confirmed: bool = Field(strict=True)


def build_failure_console_router(
    require_user: Callable[..., Any],
    require_reviewer: Callable[..., Any],
) -> APIRouter:
    router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/failures", tags=["failure-console"])

    @router.get("/invocations")
    def list_invocations(category_key: str | None = Query(default=None, max_length=80),
        reviewed: bool = False, limit: int = Query(default=25, ge=1, le=50),
        offset: int = Query(default=0, ge=0), user: Any = Depends(require_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        from .invocation_review import list_unknown_invocations
        result = list_unknown_invocations(db,category_key=category_key,reviewed=reviewed,limit=limit,offset=offset)
        result["review_allowed"] = bool(getattr(user,"is_admin",False))
        return result

    def require_review_admin(user: Any = Depends(require_user)):
        if not getattr(user, "is_admin", False):
            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="历史调用与费用责任核验仅管理员可登记")
        return user

    @router.post("/invocations/{invocation_id}/review")
    def review_invocation(invocation_id: int, payload: InvocationReviewRequest,
        user: Any = Depends(require_review_admin), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        from .invocation_review import review_unknown_invocation
        try:
            result = review_unknown_invocation(db,invocation_id=invocation_id,actor=_actor(user),**payload.model_dump())
            db.commit()
            return result
        except FailureConsoleError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _http(exc) from exc

    @router.get("")
    def list_failed_materials(
        resolved: bool | None = None,
        search: str | None = Query(default=None, max_length=160),
        category_key: str | None = Query(default=None, max_length=80),
        limit: int = Query(default=50, ge=1, le=200),
        offset: int = Query(default=0, ge=0),
        _user: Any = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            return list_failures(db, limit=limit, offset=offset, resolved=resolved, search=search, category_key=category_key)
        except FailureConsoleError as exc:
            raise _http(exc) from exc

    @router.get("/{run_id}/resolution-form")
    def get_resolution_form(
        run_id: int,
        _user: Any = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            return resolution_form(db, run_id)
        except FailureConsoleError as exc:
            raise _http(exc) from exc

    @router.post("/{run_id}/retry")
    def retry(
        run_id: int,
        payload: RetryRequest,
        user: Any = Depends(require_reviewer),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            result = retry_failure(db, run_id=run_id, actor=_actor(user), reason=payload.reason.strip())
        except FailureConsoleError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _http(exc) from exc
        db.commit()
        return result

    @router.get("/{run_id}/recovery-preview")
    def preview_recovery(run_id: int, requests: int = Query(default=1, ge=1, le=2),
        _user: Any = Depends(require_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        from .failure_recovery import recovery_preview
        try:
            return recovery_preview(db, run_id, requests)
        except FailureConsoleError as exc:
            raise _http(exc) from exc

    @router.post("/{run_id}/recover")
    def recover(run_id: int, payload: RecoveryRequest, user: Any = Depends(require_reviewer), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        from .failure_recovery import recover_failure
        from .workflow_runtime import WorkflowRuntimeError
        try:
            result = recover_failure(db, run_id=run_id, actor=_actor(user), **payload.model_dump())
            db.commit()
            return result
        except (FailureConsoleError, WorkflowRuntimeError) as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise HTTPException(status_code=getattr(exc,'status_code',409),detail={'code':exc.code,'message':str(exc)}) from exc

    @router.post("/{run_id}/resolution")
    def resolve(
        run_id: int,
        payload: ResolutionRequest,
        user: Any = Depends(require_reviewer),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            from .models import ProductionRun
            run = db.get(ProductionRun, run_id)
            fields_model = (InspirationResolutionFields
                            if run is not None and run.category_key == "inspiration_image"
                            else ResolutionFields)
            try:
                fields = fields_model.model_validate(payload.fields).model_dump()
            except ValidationError as exc:
                raise RequestValidationError([
                    {**error, "loc": ("body", "fields", *error["loc"])}
                    for error in exc.errors()
                ]) from exc
            result = resolve_failure(
                db,
                run_id=run_id,
                level=payload.level,
                score=payload.score,
                fields=fields,
                reason=payload.reason.strip(),
                actor=_actor(user),
                idempotency_key=payload.idempotency_key,
            )
        except FailureConsoleError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _http(exc) from exc
        db.commit()
        return result

    return router

===== FILE backend/app/failure_console_inspiration.py =====
"""Inspiration-only form and payload boundary; no model calls or transport."""
from collections.abc import Mapping

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import object_session

from .inspiration_factory_contract import TEXT_FIELDS, task_identity
from .models import ConnectorContractVersion, WorkflowModelInvocation
from .workflow_snapshot_store import read_run_snapshot_document


def target_mapping(run):
    """Read the immutable contract version selected by this run, never latest."""
    from .failure_console import _manifest, _json_object
    from .inspiration_factory_delivery import _mapping_config, INSPIRATION_FACTORY_ADAPTER_KEY
    from .connector_delivery import ConnectorDeliveryError

    db = object_session(run)
    for node in _manifest(run).get("nodes", []):
        if not isinstance(node, Mapping) or node.get("kind") != "downstream_projection":
            continue
        config = _json_object(node.get("config"))
        frozen = _json_object(config.get("connector_snapshot"))
        contract_id = frozen.get("connector_contract_id")
        if db is None or type(contract_id) is not int or contract_id < 1:
            return {}
        target = db.get(ConnectorContractVersion, contract_id)
        if (target is None or target.contract_key != config.get("connector_key")
                or frozen.get("contract_key") != target.contract_key
                or frozen.get("direction") != "downstream" or target.direction != "downstream"
                or target.category_key != "inspiration_image"
                or target.adapter_key != INSPIRATION_FACTORY_ADAPTER_KEY):
            return {}
        try:
            return _mapping_config(target.field_mapping_json)[0]
        except ConnectorDeliveryError:
            return {}
    return {}


def form_fields(db, run, stage_a):
    from .failure_console import _json_object
    from .inspiration_factory_projection import project_a_fields
    from .inspiration_semantic_consistency import (
        frozen_inspiration_contract_profile, inspiration_semantic_conflicts,
    )

    raw = stage_a
    candidate_id = None
    reasons = []
    validation_error = ""
    source = "validated_stage_a" if stage_a is not None else "empty"
    if stage_a is None:
        # A semantic rejection can have status=succeeded: usage was settled before
        # the step rejected the answer. Do not filter by invocation status/code.
        call = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation).where(
            WorkflowModelInvocation.production_run_id == run.id,
            WorkflowModelInvocation.node_instance_id == "prompt_a",
        ).order_by(WorkflowModelInvocation.id.desc()).limit(1))
        if call is not None:
            # 调用账本把模型调用证据存在 result 下（与 pricing_settlement、reservation 并列）。
            evidence = _json_object(_json_object(call.evidence_json).get("result"))
            parsed = evidence.get("parsed_output")
            if isinstance(parsed, Mapping):
                raw = dict(parsed)
                candidate_id = call.id
                source = "rejected_model_draft"
                stored = _json_object(evidence.get("semantic_validation")).get("reasons")
                reasons = [r for r in stored if isinstance(r, str)] if isinstance(stored, list) else []
                if not reasons:
                    snapshot = read_run_snapshot_document(run)
                    manifest = _json_object(snapshot.get("workflow")).get("manifest", {})
                    node = next((n for n in manifest.get("nodes", [])
                                 if n.get("node_instance_id") == "prompt_a"), {})
                    config = _json_object(node.get("config"))
                    contract = _json_object(config.get("output_contract"))
                    try:
                        reasons = list(inspiration_semantic_conflicts(
                            str(contract.get("name") or "inspiration_call_a"), raw,
                            contract_profile=frozen_inspiration_contract_profile(snapshot),
                            output_schema=node.get("output_schema", {}),
                            run_snapshot=snapshot, node=node))
                    except (ValueError, TypeError, KeyError, AttributeError):
                        reasons = ["semantic_validation_unavailable"]
                validation_error = "；".join(reasons) or call.error_message or "模型草稿未通过校验，请人工核验"
    prefill = ({**{name: "" for name in TEXT_FIELDS}, "tags": []} if raw is None
               else project_a_fields(dict(raw), draft=True))
    return dict(field_profile="inspiration_image", draft_source=source,
                candidate_invocation_id=candidate_id, validation_error=validation_error,
                validation_reasons=reasons, limits={}, min_tags=0,
                required_fields=[], prefill=prefill)


def validate_fields(fields):
    """The submit contract requires every key/type, permits empty text/tags,
    and has no text-length bound. Preserve human text byte-for-byte.
    """
    from .failure_console import FailureConsoleError
    for name in TEXT_FIELDS:
        if not isinstance(fields.get(name), str):
            raise FailureConsoleError("failure_resolution_field_invalid", f"{name} 必须是文本", status_code=422)
    tags = fields.get("tags")
    if not isinstance(tags, list) or any(not isinstance(tag, str) for tag in tags):
        raise FailureConsoleError("failure_resolution_field_invalid", "tags 必须是文本数组", status_code=422)
    return {**{name: fields[name] for name in TEXT_FIELDS}, "tags": list(tags)}


def frozen_task(frozen):
    from .failure_console import FailureConsoleError
    try:
        return task_identity(frozen.get("inspiration_task"))
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise FailureConsoleError("failure_resolution_task_identity_missing",
            "冻结输入中的灵感图任务身份缺失或无效，人工结论无法投递") from exc

===== FILE backend/app/failure_recovery.py =====
"""Explicit, bounded operator recovery; immutable snapshots and charges stay intact."""
from __future__ import annotations
from .workflow_snapshot_store import read_run_snapshot_document

import hashlib
import json
from sqlalchemy import select
from .audit import append_audit_event, canonical_json
from .models import AuditEvent, ProductionRun, ProductionStepAttempt, WorkflowModelInvocation
from .failure_resolution_models import ManualFailureResolution

ACTION = 'failure_recovery_budget'
BUDGET_KEYS = ('max_requests', 'max_input_tokens', 'max_output_tokens', 'max_cost_micros')
PERMANENT_BUDGET_CODES = frozenset({'model_budget_requests_exhausted', 'model_budget_input_exhausted',
    'model_budget_output_exhausted', 'model_budget_cost_exhausted'})


def recovery_authority(db, run):
    if run.source_type != 'content_ingress_event' or run.model_execution_mode != 'real':
        return None
    row = db.scalar(select(AuditEvent).where(AuditEvent.subject_type == 'production_run',
        AuditEvent.subject_id == str(run.id), AuditEvent.action == ACTION).order_by(AuditEvent.id.desc()).limit(1))
    if row is None:
        return None
    payload = json.loads(row.payload_json)
    if payload['snapshot_hash'] != run.snapshot_hash:
        raise ValueError('恢复授权与运行快照不一致')
    return payload


def classify_failure(code):
    if code in PERMANENT_BUDGET_CODES or code == 'model_budget_wait':
        return {'kind':'budget', 'label':'预算待处置', 'advice':'额度不会因等待而增加。核对调用账本后，可明确增加有限次续跑额度。'}
    if code == 'structured_output_schema_mismatch':
        return {'kind':'schema', 'label':'返回字段不合规', 'advice':'查看错误字段和候选内容，人工修复后提交独立结论；无需重填已有内容。'}
    if code in {'model_invocation_outcome_unknown','model_failure_streak_exhausted','model_transport_attempts_exhausted'}:
        return {'kind':'unknown', 'label':'模型调用需核验', 'advice':'先按请求号核验供应商结果。结果未知仍可能产生费用；确认后才可限次续跑。'}
    return {'kind':'other','label':'其他运行异常','advice':'核对失败步骤与错误原因，保留成功步骤后处置。'}


def recovery_preview(db, run_id, requests=1):
    from .failure_console import FailureConsoleError
    from .workflow_model_budget import _invocation_budget_usage, real_execution_runtime_kind
    from .batch_endpoint_recovery import is_resource_rejection
    if type(requests) is not int or not 1 <= requests <= 2:
        raise FailureConsoleError('recovery_limit_invalid','每次仅可授权 1 或 2 次模型调用',status_code=422)
    run = db.get(ProductionRun, run_id)
    if run is None:
        raise FailureConsoleError('failure_run_not_found','运行不存在',status_code=404)
    from .incremental_reassessment import assert_no_replacement
    assert_no_replacement(db,run)
    calls = db.scalars(select(WorkflowModelInvocation).where(WorkflowModelInvocation.production_run_id==run.id).order_by(WorkflowModelInvocation.id)).all()
    attempts = db.scalars(select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id==run.id).order_by(ProductionStepAttempt.id)).all()
    latest = {a.step_key:a for a in attempts}
    failed = [a for a in latest.values() if a.status in {'failed','retryable'}]
    blockers=[]
    if run.status not in {'failed','retryable'}: blockers.append('运行不在可恢复状态')
    if len(failed)!=1: blockers.append('没有唯一失败步骤，无法安全续跑')
    if any(a.status in {'running','leased'} for a in latest.values()) or any(c.status=='reserved' for c in calls):
        blockers.append('仍有在途步骤或未结算请求，请先核验')
    if db.scalar(select(ManualFailureResolution.id).where(ManualFailureResolution.production_run_id==run.id,ManualFailureResolution.status=='published')):
        blockers.append('已有人工结论，不能同时重跑产生第二份结果')
    runtime=read_run_snapshot_document(run).get('runtime_context',{})
    try:
        if real_execution_runtime_kind(run,runtime)!='incremental': blockers.append('仅支持真实增量运行')
    except Exception:
        blockers.append('缺少有效的冻结增量授权')
    if run.status=='retryable' and (not failed or failed[0].last_error_code!='model_budget_wait'):
        blockers.append('非预算等待应由现有调度继续，不可抢占')
    limits=[]
    for value in runtime.get('real_execution_node_limits',{}).values():
        if isinstance(value,dict):
            limits.append(value)
            if isinstance(value.get('online_fallback_limits'),dict): limits.append(value['online_fallback_limits'])
    keys=('max_input_tokens','max_output_tokens','estimated_cost_micros')
    if not limits or any(type(v.get(k)) is not int or v[k]<0 for v in limits for k in keys):
        blockers.append('冻结节点额度缺失或损坏'); limits=[]
    charged=[c for c in calls if c.status in {'reserved','succeeded','failed'} and not is_resource_rejection(c)]
    usage=[_invocation_budget_usage(c) for c in charged]
    amounts=[sum(v[i] for v in usage) for i in range(3)]
    extra=[requests*max((v[k] for v in limits),default=0) for k in keys]
    budget=dict(zip(BUDGET_KEYS,[len(charged)+requests]+[a+b for a,b in zip(amounts,extra)]))
    basis={'run_id':run.id,'snapshot_hash':run.snapshot_hash,'requests':requests,'budget':budget,
        'invocation_watermark':max((c.id for c in calls),default=0),
        'attempts':[(a.id,a.status) for a in latest.values()], 'run_status':run.status,
        'authority_id':db.scalar(select(AuditEvent.id).where(AuditEvent.subject_type=='production_run',AuditEvent.subject_id==str(run.id),AuditEvent.action==ACTION).order_by(AuditEvent.id.desc()).limit(1))}
    quote=hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(basis).encode()).hexdigest()
    error=failed[0].last_error_code if len(failed)==1 else run.error_code
    return {'production_run_id':run.id,'quote_hash':quote,'snapshot_hash':run.snapshot_hash,
        'classification':classify_failure(error),'requests':requests,'max_additional_cost_micros':extra[2],
        'budget':budget,'invocation_watermark':basis['invocation_watermark'],
        'failed_step':failed[0].step_key if len(failed)==1 else None,
        'blockers':blockers,'scheduler_attempts':run.attempt_count or 0,
        'model_calls':len(calls),'charged_calls':len(charged),
        'calls':[{'id':c.id,'step':c.node_instance_id,'status':c.status,'error_code':c.error_code,
            'request_id':c.request_correlation_id,'estimated_cost_micros':c.estimated_cost_micros,
            'actual_cost_micros':c.actual_cost_micros} for c in calls[-20:]]}


def recover_failure(db, *, run_id, quote_hash, requests, reason, confirmed, actor):
    from .failure_console import FailureConsoleError
    from .workflow_runtime import terminalize_runtime_run, retry_failed_run_from_checkpoint
    if confirmed is not True or not reason.strip():
        raise FailureConsoleError('recovery_confirmation_required','请核验请求并确认有限次续跑及费用',status_code=422)
    run=db.scalar(select(ProductionRun).where(ProductionRun.id==run_id).with_for_update().execution_options(populate_existing=True))
    if run is None: raise FailureConsoleError('failure_run_not_found','运行不存在',status_code=404)
    key=f'failure-recovery:{run_id}:{quote_hash}'
    existing=db.scalar(select(AuditEvent).where(AuditEvent.event_key==key))
    if existing:
        previous=json.loads(existing.payload_json)
        if previous['requests'] != requests or previous['reason'] != reason.strip():
            raise FailureConsoleError('recovery_idempotency_conflict','同一次预览已用于不同的恢复内容',status_code=409)
        return {'production_run_id':run_id,'status':run.status,'idempotent_replay':True}
    preview=recovery_preview(db,run_id,requests)
    if preview['blockers'] or preview['quote_hash']!=quote_hash:
        raise FailureConsoleError('recovery_stale_or_blocked','；'.join(preview['blockers']) or '状态或预算已变化，请重新预览',status_code=409)
    append_audit_event(db,category='failure_console',action=ACTION,subject_type='production_run',subject_id=run.id,
        actor=actor,event_key=key,payload={**preview,'reason':reason.strip(),'confirmed':True})
    if run.status=='retryable':
        # No running/reserved work is allowed above. Preserve the failed attempt
        # while cancelling stale dispatch entries before creating a fresh attempt.
        attempt=db.scalar(select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id==run.id,
            ProductionStepAttempt.step_key==preview['failed_step']).order_by(ProductionStepAttempt.id.desc()).limit(1))
        attempt.status='failed'
        terminalize_runtime_run(db,run,status='failed',error_code='model_budget_wait',error_message='人工确认预算后续跑')
    updated=retry_failed_run_from_checkpoint(db,run.id,actor=actor)
    return {'production_run_id':updated.id,'status':updated.status,'idempotent_replay':False}

===== FILE backend/app/failure_resolution_models.py =====
"""人工结论：一条跑挂的素材，由人给出最终结果。

**不碰 DAG。** 节点输出是证据——模型跑出来什么就是什么。跑挂的素材没有节点输出，
人工给的结论也不该伪装成节点输出：那样以后就分不清哪条是模型算的、哪条是人填的。
所以人工结论单独一张表，投下游时 payload 里 ``prompt_a_status='manual'`` 自证来源
（老大 2026-09-20 在两个方案之间选的这个）。

为什么不复用 ``IncrementalCorrectionCase``：它的 ``evaluation_id`` 非空、外键指向
``evaluation_results``，而跑挂的素材**根本没有结果行**（实测 failed/canceled/retryable
全都没有）。纠偏是「改模型给的结论」，这里是「模型没给出结论，人来给」，两件事。

十三字段为什么必须人填全：下游提交体由 ``legacy_3d_su_projection`` 从调用 A 的
``production_fields`` 投影而来，A 段挂了就一个字段都没有。只给等级投不出去——
投递器会按合同判错并死信。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone

from sqlalchemy import (
    CheckConstraint,
    DateTime,
    Integer,
    String,
    Text,
    UniqueConstraint,
)
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column

from .database import Base


def utcnow() -> datetime:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc)


class ManualFailureResolution(Base):
    """一条跑挂素材的人工结论。一条素材最多一条有效结论。"""

    __tablename__ = "manual_failure_resolutions"
    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint("production_run_id", name="uq_manual_failure_resolution_run"),
        UniqueConstraint("idempotency_key", name="uq_manual_failure_resolution_key"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status IN ('published','superseded')",
            name="ck_manual_failure_resolution_status",
        ),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    #: 一次提交一个键；网络重试送来同一个键时原样把上次的结果还回去。
    idempotency_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120), index=True)
    production_run_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, index=True)
    run_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120), default="", server_default="", index=True)
    category_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), default="", server_default="", index=True)
    #: 上游身份，便于在页面上按 task 找回来。
    upstream_task_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True, index=True)
    #: 人给的最终结论。
    level: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(8))
    score: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer)
    #: 人填的调用 A 字段，原样存 JSON（title/seotitle/category/style/tags/cons/design…）。
    fields_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="{}", server_default="{}")
    #: 这条结论投出去的那一版 payload，事后对账用。
    payload_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="{}", server_default="{}")
    reason: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="", server_default="")
    actor: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), default="", server_default="", index=True)
    #: 投出去的 outbox 事件；没投成也留着，便于重投。
    outbox_event_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True, index=True)
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(20), default="published", server_default="published", index=True
    )
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow, index=True)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow, onupdate=utcnow
    )

===== FILE backend/app/field_demand_contracts.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import re
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any, Mapping, Sequence

from sqlalchemy import func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .audit import canonical_json
from .models import Asset, AssetVersion, FieldDemandContract


_CONTRACT_KEY_PATTERN = re.compile(r"^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_-]*$")
_FIELD_KEY_PATTERN = re.compile(r"^[a-z][a-z0-9_.-]*$")
_ALLOWED_CANONICAL_ROOTS = {"semantic", "quality", "governance"}
_FORBIDDEN_PATH_PARTS = {
    "candidate",
    "raw",
    "raw_response",
    "manual",
    "manual_process",
    "credential",
    "credentials",
    "secret",
    "token",
    "password",
    "api_key",
}
_STATUSES = {"draft", "active", "retired"}


class FieldDemandContractError(ValueError):
    pass


def _required_text(value: str, *, label: str) -> str:
    normalized = value.strip()
    if not normalized:
        raise FieldDemandContractError(f"{label}不能为空")
    return normalized


def _normalize_occurred_at(value: datetime) -> datetime:
    if value.tzinfo is None:
        return value.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    return value.astimezone(timezone.utc)


def _hash(value: Any) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(value).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


def _normalize_fields(fields: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    if not fields:
        raise FieldDemandContractError("字段需求合同至少包含一个 Canonical 字段")
    normalized: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    seen: set[str] = set()
    for field in fields:
        item = dict(field)
        field_key = _required_text(str(item.get("field_key") or ""), label="field_key")
        if not _FIELD_KEY_PATTERN.fullmatch(field_key):
            raise FieldDemandContractError(f"field_key 格式无效：{field_key}")
        if field_key in seen:
            raise FieldDemandContractError(f"字段键重复：{field_key}")
        seen.add(field_key)

        source_path = _required_text(
            str(item.get("source_path") or ""), label="source_path"
        )
        path_parts = [part.lower() for part in source_path.split(".") if part]
        if (
            len(path_parts) < 2
            or path_parts[0] not in _ALLOWED_CANONICAL_ROOTS
            or any(part in _FORBIDDEN_PATH_PARTS for part in path_parts)
        ):
            raise FieldDemandContractError(
                "字段来源必须是 semantic.*、quality.* 或 governance.* Canonical 路径"
            )
        data_type = _required_text(str(item.get("data_type") or ""), label="data_type")
        required = item.get("required")
        if not isinstance(required, bool):
            raise FieldDemandContractError(f"字段 {field_key} 的 required 必须是布尔值")
        item.update(
            {
                "field_key": field_key,
                "source_path": source_path,
                "data_type": data_type,
                "required": required,
            }
        )
        try:
            canonical_json(item)
        except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
            raise FieldDemandContractError(f"字段 {field_key} 不是合法 JSON 定义") from exc
        normalized.append(item)
    return sorted(normalized, key=lambda item: str(item["field_key"]))


def _normalize_thresholds(thresholds: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, float]:
    if not {"accuracy", "recall"} <= set(thresholds):
        raise FieldDemandContractError("阈值必须包含 accuracy 和 recall")
    normalized: dict[str, float] = {}
    for key, value in thresholds.items():
        if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, (int, float)):
            raise FieldDemandContractError(f"阈值 {key} 必须是 0 到 1 的数字")
        numeric = float(value)
        if numeric < 0 or numeric > 1:
            raise FieldDemandContractError(f"阈值 {key} 必须在 0 到 1 之间")
        normalized[str(key)] = numeric
    return dict(sorted(normalized.items()))


def create_field_demand_contract(
    db: Session,
    *,
    contract_key: str,
    category_key: str,
    consumer_key: str,
    owner: str,
    fields: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
    thresholds: Mapping[str, Any],
    status: str,
    created_by: str,
) -> FieldDemandContract:
    normalized_key = _required_text(contract_key, label="contract_key")
    if not _CONTRACT_KEY_PATTERN.fullmatch(normalized_key):
        raise FieldDemandContractError("contract_key 格式无效")
    normalized_category = _required_text(category_key, label="category_key")
    normalized_consumer = _required_text(consumer_key, label="consumer_key")
    normalized_owner = _required_text(owner, label="owner")
    normalized_creator = _required_text(created_by, label="created_by")
    normalized_status = status.strip().lower()
    if normalized_status not in _STATUSES:
        raise FieldDemandContractError("status 必须是 draft、active 或 retired")
    normalized_fields = _normalize_fields(fields)
    normalized_thresholds = _normalize_thresholds(thresholds)
    definition = {
        "contract_key": normalized_key,
        "category_key": normalized_category,
        "consumer_key": normalized_consumer,
        "owner": normalized_owner,
        "fields": normalized_fields,
        "thresholds": normalized_thresholds,
        "status": normalized_status,
    }
    contract_hash = _hash(definition)
    existing = db.scalar(
        select(FieldDemandContract).where(
            FieldDemandContract.contract_key == normalized_key,
            FieldDemandContract.contract_hash == contract_hash,
        )
    )
    if existing is not None:
        return existing
    latest_version = db.scalar(
        select(func.max(FieldDemandContract.version)).where(
            FieldDemandContract.contract_key == normalized_key
        )
    )
    contract = FieldDemandContract(
        contract_key=normalized_key,
        version=int(latest_version or 0) + 1,
        category_key=normalized_category,
        consumer_key=normalized_consumer,
        owner=normalized_owner,
        fields_json=canonical_json(normalized_fields),
        thresholds_json=canonical_json(normalized_thresholds),
        status=normalized_status,
        contract_hash=contract_hash,
        created_by=normalized_creator,
    )
    db.add(contract)
    db.flush()
    return contract


def record_asset_version(
    db: Session,
    *,
    source_system: str,
    source_content_id: str,
    source_version: str,
    asset: Asset,
    occurred_at: datetime,
) -> tuple[AssetVersion, bool]:
    if asset.id is None:
        raise FieldDemandContractError("asset 必须先持久化")
    normalized_source = _required_text(source_system, label="source_system")
    normalized_content = _required_text(source_content_id, label="source_content_id")
    normalized_version = _required_text(source_version, label="source_version")
    normalized_time = _normalize_occurred_at(occurred_at)
    definition = {
        "source_system": normalized_source,
        "source_content_id": normalized_content,
        "source_version": normalized_version,
        "asset_id": asset.id,
        "sha256": asset.sha256,
        "mime_type": asset.mime_type,
        "size_bytes": asset.size_bytes,
        "occurred_at": normalized_time.isoformat(),
    }
    payload_hash = _hash(definition)
    existing = db.scalar(
        select(AssetVersion).where(
            AssetVersion.source_system == normalized_source,
            AssetVersion.source_content_id == normalized_content,
            AssetVersion.source_version == normalized_version,
        )
    )
    if existing is not None:
        if existing.payload_hash != payload_hash:
            raise FieldDemandContractError(
                "ASSET_VERSION_CONFLICT：同一来源版本对应了不同不可变素材载荷"
            )
        return existing, True
    latest_version = db.scalar(
        select(func.max(AssetVersion.version)).where(AssetVersion.asset_id == asset.id)
    )
    version = AssetVersion(
        source_system=normalized_source,
        source_content_id=normalized_content,
        source_version=normalized_version,
        asset_id=asset.id,
        version=int(latest_version or 0) + 1,
        asset_sha256=asset.sha256,
        storage_backend=getattr(asset, "storage_backend", "local") or "local",
        source_uri=getattr(asset, "source_uri", None),
        mime_type=asset.mime_type,
        size_bytes=asset.size_bytes,
        occurred_at=normalized_time,
        payload_hash=payload_hash,
    )
    db.add(version)
    db.flush()
    return version, False


def field_demand_contract_payload(contract: FieldDemandContract) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "id": contract.id,
        "contract_key": contract.contract_key,
        "version": contract.version,
        "category_key": contract.category_key,
        "consumer_key": contract.consumer_key,
        "owner": contract.owner,
        "fields": json.loads(contract.fields_json),
        "thresholds": json.loads(contract.thresholds_json),
        "status": contract.status,
        "contract_hash": contract.contract_hash,
        "created_by": contract.created_by,
        "created_at": contract.created_at,
    }


def asset_version_payload(version: AssetVersion) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "id": version.id,
        "source_system": version.source_system,
        "source_content_id": version.source_content_id,
        "source_version": version.source_version,
        "asset_id": version.asset_id,
        "sha256": version.asset_sha256,
        "mime_type": version.mime_type,
        "size_bytes": version.size_bytes,
        "occurred_at": version.occurred_at,
        "payload_hash": version.payload_hash,
        "created_at": version.created_at,
    }

===== FILE backend/app/field_review_policy.py =====
"""字段级人工校验强度：把「每条每字段都要人工确认」降为按重要度分层。

2026-09-03 运营反馈：知识图谱纠偏 10 条结果看了一小时，13 个字段一视同仁，
大多数字段模型本来就答对，运营为「确认正确」付出了和「改错」一样的成本。
运营给出的分层口径（见知识图谱标签识别诉求表）在这里落成可执行策略。

抽检用「轮次 + 条目 + 字段」的确定性哈希决定，同一轮内稳定可复现，
换一轮自动换一批样本，长期覆盖率不会漏掉某个字段。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any, Mapping

REQUIRED = "required"      # 每条必看
SAMPLED = "sampled"        # 抽检命中，本条要看
EXEMPT = "exempt"          # 抽检未命中，本条免检（直接采纳模型输出）

CRITICAL = "critical"      # 重点字段：错了下游直接用错
NORMAL = "normal"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class FieldReviewRule:
    rate: float          # 1.0 = 每条必看；0 < rate < 1 = 抽检比例
    emphasis: str = NORMAL


# 知识图谱（含 3D&SU 同构合同）：运营 2026-09-03 定的分层
_KNOWLEDGE_GRAPH_RULES: dict[str, FieldReviewRule] = {
    # 重点看，全量校验：身份与整体/单体错了，下游直接挂错素材
    "ll_id": FieldReviewRule(1.0, CRITICAL),
    "res_id": FieldReviewRule(1.0, CRITICAL),
    "is_single": FieldReviewRule(1.0, CRITICAL),
    "title": FieldReviewRule(1.0, CRITICAL),
    # 每条过
    "space": FieldReviewRule(1.0),
    "object": FieldReviewRule(1.0),
    "style": FieldReviewRule(1.0),
    "form": FieldReviewRule(1.0),
    "structure": FieldReviewRule(1.0),          # 运营表里的 spatial_structure
    # 50% 抽检
    "material": FieldReviewRule(0.5),
    "structural_features": FieldReviewRule(0.5),
    "architectural_element": FieldReviewRule(0.5),
    "color": FieldReviewRule(0.5),
    # 10-20% 抽检
    "soft_decoration": FieldReviewRule(0.15),
    "hard_decoration": FieldReviewRule(0.15),
}

_POLICIES: dict[str, dict[str, FieldReviewRule]] = {
    "knowledge_graph": _KNOWLEDGE_GRAPH_RULES,
    "model_3d_su": _KNOWLEDGE_GRAPH_RULES,
}

SCHEMA_VERSION = "field-review-policy-v1"


def policy_for_category(category_key: str) -> dict[str, FieldReviewRule]:
    return _POLICIES.get(category_key, {})


def _bucket(salt: str, item_key: str, field: str) -> float:
    digest = hashlib.sha256(f"{salt}:{item_key}:{field}".encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
    return int(digest[:8], 16) / 0xFFFFFFFF


def resolve_field_review(
    *,
    category_key: str,
    field: str,
    salt: str,
    item_key: str,
) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    """判定单个字段本条是否需要人工看；无策略的类目返回 None（保持全量人工）。"""

    rules = policy_for_category(category_key)
    if not rules:
        return None
    rule = rules.get(field)
    if rule is None:
        # 策略里没列出的字段按必看处理，避免新字段被静默免检
        return {
            "schema_version": SCHEMA_VERSION,
            "state": REQUIRED,
            "emphasis": NORMAL,
            "sample_rate": 1.0,
        }
    if rule.rate >= 1.0:
        state = REQUIRED
    else:
        state = SAMPLED if _bucket(salt, item_key, field) < rule.rate else EXEMPT
    return {
        "schema_version": SCHEMA_VERSION,
        "state": state,
        "emphasis": rule.emphasis,
        "sample_rate": rule.rate,
    }


def review_summary(category_key: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """给运营看的策略说明。"""

    rules = policy_for_category(category_key)
    return {
        "schema_version": SCHEMA_VERSION,
        "category_key": category_key,
        "fields": {
            field: {"sample_rate": rule.rate, "emphasis": rule.emphasis}
            for field, rule in rules.items()
        },
    }

===== FILE backend/app/historical_corrections.py =====
"""Deterministic preview for Owner-confirmed historical correction workbooks.

The preview is deliberately non-persistent.  It reuses the hardened P0-E XLSX
reader, preserves source provenance, and never promotes a row to Gold.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import tempfile
import unicodedata
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Iterable

from .p0e_safe_import import ImportTarget, XlsxLimits, preflight_xlsx


HISTORICAL_PREVIEW_SCHEMA_VERSION = "historical-corrections-preview-v1"
_HUMAN_LEVEL_FIELDS = ("评测等级", "人工等级", "human_grade", "human_level")
_MODEL_LEVEL_FIELDS = ("豆包等级", "grade", "model_grade", "模型等级")
_REASON_FIELDS = ("hwreason", "人工原因", "纠偏原因")
_FALLBACK_REASON_FIELDS = ("reason", "cons", "image_defects")
_BUSINESS_KEY_FIELDS = (
    "llid",
    "ll_id",
    "img_id",
    "fa_img_id",
    "source_business_id",
    "img_url",
    "image_url",
    "url",
)


def _canonical_json(value: Any) -> str:
    return json.dumps(
        value,
        ensure_ascii=False,
        sort_keys=True,
        separators=(",", ":"),
        default=str,
    )


def _normalized_key(value: object) -> str:
    return unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", str(value or "")).strip().casefold()


def _clean(value: object) -> object | None:
    if value is None:
        return None
    if isinstance(value, str):
        stripped = value.strip()
        return stripped or None
    return value


def _field(
    values: dict[str, Any],
    columns: list[dict[str, Any]],
    candidates: Iterable[str],
) -> object | None:
    candidate_set = {_normalized_key(candidate) for candidate in candidates}
    for column in columns:
        if _normalized_key(column["raw_header"]) not in candidate_set:
            continue
        value = _clean(values.get(str(column["internal_name"])))
        if value is not None:
            return value
    return None


def _row_hash(
    *,
    file_hash: str,
    sheet: str,
    source_row: int,
    values: dict[str, Any],
) -> str:
    material = {
        "file_hash": file_hash,
        "sheet": sheet,
        "source_row": source_row,
        "values": values,
    }
    return hashlib.sha256(_canonical_json(material).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


def _holdout(row_hash: str, ratio: float) -> bool:
    threshold = int(ratio * 10_000)
    bucket = int(row_hash[:8], 16) % 10_000
    return bucket < threshold


def _business_key(
    values: dict[str, Any],
    columns: list[dict[str, Any]],
    row_hash: str,
    source_file: str,
) -> str:
    value = _field(values, columns, _BUSINESS_KEY_FIELDS)
    if value is None:
        return f"row:{row_hash}"
    namespace = "su" if "su" in _normalized_key(source_file) else "3d"
    return f"business:{namespace}:{_normalized_key(value)}"


def map_historical_row(
    *,
    source_file: str,
    content_sha256: str,
    sheet: str,
    source_row: int,
    columns: list[dict[str, Any]],
    values: dict[str, Any],
    blind_holdout_ratio: float = 0.2,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Map one RAW row into a correction candidate without inventing truth."""

    if not 0 <= blind_holdout_ratio < 1:
        raise ValueError("blind_holdout_ratio 必须在 [0, 1) 范围")
    row_hash = _row_hash(
        file_hash=content_sha256,
        sheet=sheet,
        source_row=source_row,
        values=values,
    )
    source_reason_only = "3dreason" in _normalized_key(source_file)
    human_level = None if source_reason_only else _field(
        values, columns, _HUMAN_LEVEL_FIELDS
    )
    model_level = _field(values, columns, _MODEL_LEVEL_FIELDS)
    human_reason = _field(values, columns, _REASON_FIELDS)
    reason = human_reason or _field(values, columns, _FALLBACK_REASON_FIELDS)
    reason_only = source_reason_only or human_level is None
    if reason_only:
        role = "reason_only"
    elif _holdout(row_hash, blind_holdout_ratio):
        role = "blind_holdout"
    elif human_reason is not None or (
        human_level is not None
        and model_level is not None
        and _normalized_key(human_level) != _normalized_key(model_level)
    ):
        role = "target_error"
    else:
        role = "stable_control"
    return {
        "schema_version": HISTORICAL_PREVIEW_SCHEMA_VERSION,
        "dedupe_key": _business_key(
            values, columns, row_hash, source_file
        ),
        "sample_role": role,
        "correction_candidate": {
            "scope": "overall",
            "human_level": human_level,
            "model_level": model_level,
            "reason": reason,
            "reason_only": reason_only,
        },
        "provenance": {
            "source_file": source_file,
            "sheet": sheet,
            "source_row": source_row,
            "source_file_sha256": content_sha256,
            "source_row_sha256": row_hash,
            "owner_confirmed": True,
        },
        "forms_gold": False,
    }


def preview_historical_workbooks(
    files: list[tuple[str, bytes]],
    *,
    blind_holdout_ratio: float = 0.2,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Safely parse uploaded bytes and return a bounded, deterministic plan."""

    if not files:
        raise ValueError("至少需要一个 XLSX 文件")
    file_summaries: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    mapped: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    duplicate_count = 0
    seen: set[str] = set()
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="3d66-historical-preview-") as temp:
        root = Path(temp)
        for index, (filename, content) in enumerate(files):
            safe_name = f"{index:04d}.xlsx"
            path = root / safe_name
            path.write_bytes(content)
            preflight = preflight_xlsx(
                path,
                target=ImportTarget(domain="3D", target_kind="candidate"),
                limits=XlsxLimits(preview_rows=100_000),
            )
            # The parser sees the safe temporary basename.  Restore the
            # authenticated upload name only as display/provenance metadata.
            sheet = str(preflight["sheet"]["name"])
            file_count = 0
            for row in preflight["raw_preview"]:
                item = map_historical_row(
                    source_file=filename,
                    content_sha256=str(preflight["content_sha256"]),
                    sheet=sheet,
                    source_row=int(row["source_row"]),
                    columns=preflight["columns"],
                    values=row["values_by_internal_name"],
                    blind_holdout_ratio=blind_holdout_ratio,
                )
                if item["dedupe_key"] in seen:
                    duplicate_count += 1
                    continue
                seen.add(item["dedupe_key"])
                mapped.append(item)
                file_count += 1
            file_summaries.append(
                {
                    "source_file": filename,
                    "content_sha256": preflight["content_sha256"],
                    "sheet": preflight["sheet"],
                    "batch_key": preflight["batch_key"],
                    "preview_item_count": file_count,
                }
            )
    mapped.sort(
        key=lambda item: (
            item["dedupe_key"],
            item["provenance"]["source_file"],
            item["provenance"]["source_row"],
        )
    )
    role_counts = {
        role: sum(1 for item in mapped if item["sample_role"] == role)
        for role in (
            "target_error",
            "stable_control",
            "blind_holdout",
            "reason_only",
        )
    }
    return {
        "schema_version": HISTORICAL_PREVIEW_SCHEMA_VERSION,
        "mode": "preview_only",
        "files": file_summaries,
        "summary": {
            "uploaded_file_count": len(files),
            "unique_item_count": len(mapped),
            "duplicate_count": duplicate_count,
            "blind_holdout_ratio": blind_holdout_ratio,
            "role_counts": role_counts,
        },
        "items": mapped,
        "writes_business_database": False,
        "downloads_performed": False,
        "model_runs_performed": False,
        "forms_gold": False,
    }

===== FILE backend/app/identity_logging.py =====
"""Keep short-lived identity credentials out of HTTP and callback logs."""
from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import re

_SECRET_QUERY = re.compile(r"(?i)([?&](?:access_token|authcode|code|state|ticket|client_secret)=)[^&\s\"']+")


def redact(value):
    if isinstance(value, str):
        return _SECRET_QUERY.sub(r"\1[redacted]", value)
    if isinstance(value, tuple):
        return tuple(redact(item) for item in value)
    if isinstance(value, dict):
        return {key: redact(item) for key, item in value.items()}
    return value


class IdentityLogFilter(logging.Filter):
    def filter(self, record: logging.LogRecord) -> bool:
        # Preserve the access formatter's structured argument tuple.
        record.msg = redact(record.msg)
        record.args = redact(record.args)
        return True


def install_identity_log_filters() -> None:
    for name in ("httpx", "uvicorn.access", "urllib3.connectionpool", "websockets.client"):
        logger = logging.getLogger(name)
        if not any(isinstance(item, IdentityLogFilter) for item in logger.filters):
            logger.addFilter(IdentityLogFilter())
    # DEBUG wire traces contain authorization headers, not just URLs.
    for name in ("httpcore.connection", "httpcore.http11", "httpcore.http2", "httpcore.proxy", "websockets.client"):
        logging.getLogger(name).setLevel(logging.WARNING)

===== FILE backend/app/image_first_seen_provider.py =====
"""Redacted provider boundary for a supplier's earliest image discovery time.

The result is deliberately *not* called an internet publication time.  Reverse
image search can only report the earliest record known to that supplier; it
cannot prove when an image was created or first published anywhere online.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import re
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any, Callable, Literal, Mapping, Protocol

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .model_quota import (
    ModelQuotaUnavailable,
    acquire_provider_quota,
    cool_down_provider_quota,
    release_provider_quota,
)
from .models import ImageFirstSeenCache
from .security import unprotect_secret
from .workflow_result_contract import hash_manifest


FirstSeenStatus = Literal[
    "found",
    "not_found",
    "timeout",
    "rate_limited",
    "unknown",
    "failed",
]
_DIGEST = re.compile(r"[0-9a-f]{64}")
_SAFE_KEY = re.compile(r"[a-z0-9][a-z0-9._-]{0,79}")


class FirstSeenProviderError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class FirstSeenResult:
    status: FirstSeenStatus
    earliest_discovered_at: datetime | None
    provider: str
    evidence_count: int
    cache_key: str

    def as_public_dict(self) -> dict[str, object]:
        return {
            "status": self.status,
            "earliest_discovered_at": (
                _as_utc(self.earliest_discovered_at).isoformat()
                if self.earliest_discovered_at is not None
                else None
            ),
            "provider": self.provider,
            "evidence_count": self.evidence_count,
            "cache_key": self.cache_key,
        }


class ImageFirstSeenProvider(Protocol):
    def lookup(self, image_digest: str, image_bytes: bytes) -> FirstSeenResult: ...


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ProviderHttpResponse:
    status_code: int
    payload: Mapping[str, Any] | None
    retry_after_seconds: int | None = None


class FirstSeenTransport(Protocol):
    def __call__(
        self,
        *,
        image_bytes: bytes,
        api_key: str,
        timeout_seconds: int,
